00:40:16.739 --> 00:40:17.340
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Papa V!

00:40:17.167 --> 00:40:23.714
[Riv (DM)]: She just she kind of just wrinkles her nose a bit and goes, oh, no.

00:40:18.332 --> 00:40:18.512
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Heh.

00:40:25.575 --> 00:40:26.036
[Riv (DM)]: I don't know.

00:40:27.918 --> 00:40:36.026
[Riv (DM)]: He's an old friend, known him for a long time, and anyway, I mean, I want to go and see him. He's around here somewhere, right?

00:40:34.419 --> 00:40:34.960
[Lacer (Kai)]: Right, let's...

00:40:36.267 --> 00:40:37.448
[Riv (DM)]: You would have seen him down here last time.

00:40:41.265 --> 00:40:43.206
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, let's walk and talk.

00:40:44.268 --> 00:41:10.296
[Riv (DM)]: Very cool. You start heading through the chamber. And up ahead, you can see there's a bit of a glowing aura through the darkness sort of coming from behind one of these large columns. And you get the vibe that you're sort of starting to head in the direction of maybe the central part of the chamber. As you get closer, you do see this is the dais with the large glowing

00:41:13.512 --> 00:41:32.649
[Riv (DM)]: How would you call like a large massive dish or basin with the glowing white liquid inside it. And there is a robed figure with long silver hair, a long white beard, sort of slightly hunched over, leaning on a staff, looking in and at your footsteps turns towards you.

00:41:35.312 --> 00:41:39.996
[Riv (DM)]: You see she breaks off from your group and starts running directly at him.

00:41:44.300 --> 00:41:45.120
[Riv (DM)]: Do you allow her to go?

00:41:44.746 --> 00:41:45.141
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Ashii.

00:41:45.140 --> 00:41:50.943
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

00:41:48.352 --> 00:41:49.378
[JC (Ryo)]: Softer or Tron Tackler?

00:41:52.016 --> 00:41:55.367
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I am just immediately whistling to make sure that he has attention towards her.

00:41:57.026 --> 00:41:58.027
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, okay.

00:42:00.548 --> 00:42:06.211
[Riv (DM)]: So, you see the old man, he turns as he hears your footsteps and the little girl breaks away from the group and starts running.

00:42:07.751 --> 00:42:08.772
[Riv (DM)]: Ryo, you chase after her.

00:42:10.528 --> 00:42:15.951
[Riv (DM)]: I'll give you a, yeah, probably also a dexterity saving throw to see how quickly you're able to react.

00:42:17.692 --> 00:42:21.653
[JC (Ryo)]: I also smack myself with my free cast of Longstrider.

00:42:22.675 --> 00:42:23.355
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool.

00:42:25.094 --> 00:42:26.595
[JC (Ryo)]: Just in case I need the extra movement.

00:42:27.357 --> 00:42:27.497
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

00:42:28.415 --> 00:42:36.357
[JC (Ryo)]: Dex save, you said? Oh, sorry, I did ability check, not saving throw.

00:42:30.859 --> 00:42:31.059
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

00:42:36.377 --> 00:42:40.559
[JC (Ryo)]: Plus nine, not plus five.

00:42:37.423 --> 00:42:39.444
[Riv (DM)]: That's all right. What's your mod for save?

00:42:41.319 --> 00:42:41.559
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah.

00:42:42.976 --> 00:42:43.876
[Riv (DM)]: Damn, you got a plus nine?

00:42:45.845 --> 00:42:46.170
[Lacer (Kai)]: Avernus.

00:42:47.298 --> 00:43:04.365
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. All right, cool. Yeah, I mean, easily enough, she's a little girl. You've got long legs, plus you... I mean, that's taking action to cast a long strider, but you're probably still able to quickly catch up with her. You dive and, you know, so are you just going for like a full blown like rugby tackle?

00:43:07.194 --> 00:43:11.952
[JC (Ryo)]: Not tackled to the ground, like tackle and pick her up in the air and not let her keep moving forward.

00:43:12.028 --> 00:43:13.264
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Yeah, cool.

00:43:14.946 --> 00:43:20.752
[Riv (DM)]: You reach out and try and like grab her by the shoulders and your hands pass straight through her.

00:43:21.813 --> 00:43:37.528
[Riv (DM)]: Just you grab nothing. You grab here and you look up. She's still beelining us straight for Praetor Varanus. And as you look up and see him, he's actually got a little bit of a smile starting to curl on his face.

00:43:40.500 --> 00:43:53.227
[Riv (DM)]: She just like dives into him, gives him a big hug around the waist and then steps back and she's like, I brought you a present. And she holds up the potion that she took from the magic store.

00:43:55.989 --> 00:44:00.391
[Riv (DM)]: And he sort of steps back and sort of brushes himself off a bit, sort of straightens up his robes.

00:44:01.772 --> 00:44:08.496
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, what's this then, child? Your present for me? And he raised out a hand for the potion.

00:44:10.824 --> 00:44:11.324
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

00:44:12.505 --> 00:44:13.026
[Riv (DM)]: Uncorks it.

00:44:14.146 --> 00:44:15.387
[Riv (DM)]: And like, takes a little bit of a sniff.

00:44:16.428 --> 00:44:16.788
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

00:44:17.949 --> 00:44:20.871
[Riv (DM)]: Do I want to know what this is? It's a surprise!

00:44:22.552 --> 00:44:23.773
[Riv (DM)]: And he goes...

00:44:23.712 --> 00:44:24.178
[JC (Ryo)]: I wouldn't.

00:44:25.868 --> 00:44:26.288
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Do it!

00:44:27.976 --> 00:44:36.322
[Riv (DM)]: He kinda looks at you. Ah, my friends. I'm glad to see you return from the Vale. Um... One moment.

00:44:38.623 --> 00:44:41.041
[Riv (DM)]: And he starts to... Put it up to his mouth.

00:44:42.221 --> 00:44:44.042
[Riv (DM)]: Do you allow him to continue?

00:44:46.652 --> 00:44:48.695
[Vael (Berberis)]: I don't think there's any stopping if he really wants to.

00:44:50.071 --> 00:44:51.532
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Chug, chug, chug, chug!

00:44:53.896 --> 00:44:55.643
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Expired potion of instant death.

00:44:54.028 --> 00:44:54.308
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

00:44:54.785 --> 00:45:01.515
[Vael (Berberis)]: He's an ancient silver fucking dragon. I'm pretty sure he's not susceptible to random poisons.

00:45:02.996 --> 00:45:28.496
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, unless, uh, unless someone tries to poison him. Oh, good on you, mate.

00:45:08.748 --> 00:45:09.928
[Lacer (Kai)]: Probably immune to poison.

00:45:08.960 --> 00:45:17.236
[JC (Ryo)]: Oh, the old ad don't so.

00:45:12.044 --> 00:45:15.586
[Riv (DM)]: Well, she's been holding onto that potion for a while. Either way.

00:45:18.168 --> 00:45:18.388
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

00:45:21.270 --> 00:45:25.633
[Riv (DM)]: He throws it back and drinks it in one long drought.

00:45:29.756 --> 00:45:34.700
[Riv (DM)]: We'll go with...Dondar, roll me your d4.

00:45:37.132 --> 00:45:39.957
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: D4. That's a D6.

00:45:50.454 --> 00:45:51.496
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Flowers in the ears?

00:46:02.096 --> 00:46:15.840
[Riv (DM)]: He starts to go, hmm, it's kind of cold and frosty. And as he's saying this, just like a big hiccup and just like a big puff of snow comes out of his mouth. It just sort of like floats through the sky.

00:46:14.432 --> 00:46:14.555
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Hm.

00:46:16.928 --> 00:46:21.892
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yes, hmm, very frosty. And again, another hiccup and another big puff of snow.

00:46:22.913 --> 00:46:23.994
[Riv (DM)]: And he just like, chuckles a bit.

00:46:30.880 --> 00:46:40.348
[Riv (DM)]: He um, puts the vial down and just looks down at her, the little girl, and he says, Ellesandra.

00:46:42.089 --> 00:46:48.105
[Riv (DM)]: And she's like, you got old. And he's like, I was always old.

00:46:49.125 --> 00:46:49.965
[Riv (DM)]: You got small.

00:46:51.226 --> 00:46:54.827
[Riv (DM)]: And she says, well, it was convenient for the time.

00:46:56.127 --> 00:47:11.532
[Riv (DM)]: And as she says this, you see the sort of shimmer start to surround her as she grows taller and taller, her black sort of short cropped hair.

00:47:12.656 --> 00:47:24.985
[Riv (DM)]: Start to unfurl and long silver hair starts to stream down her back as she grows taller again until she's standing upright, full-sized, elven woman.

00:47:26.752 --> 00:47:30.160
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: God, I had no idea. You fooled us all, your majesty.

00:47:33.070 --> 00:47:37.633
[Riv (DM)]: Praetor says, I felt you go. I waited for you to come back.

00:47:38.994 --> 00:47:41.256
[Riv (DM)]: And she's like, I know.

00:47:42.284 --> 00:47:45.605
[Riv (DM)]: But I couldn't. I had to stay behind to keep the darkness in check.

00:47:46.666 --> 00:47:48.166
[Riv (DM)]: Two thousand years, child.

00:47:50.107 --> 00:47:50.868
[Riv (DM)]: I'm sorry I never came.

00:47:55.109 --> 00:47:55.610
[Riv (DM)]: You came now.

00:47:56.990 --> 00:48:20.703
[Riv (DM)]: And at that, she just kind of steps forwards again and kind of collapses into a hug, just sobbing, just crying. And here you see him kind of like look across at you, at the group. Gives you a kind of a sort of a nod, recognition of, you know, something that you've done.

00:48:28.946 --> 00:48:35.588
[Riv (DM)]: She after time sort of pulls away and she's looking a lot more sort of fragile now, you know.

00:48:38.672 --> 00:49:02.202
[Riv (DM)]: Very much reminiscent of the woman you saw back in the Citadel in the Vale of Eternal Night. But kind of just like someone who has been in sort of a tension for longer than you can possibly imagine. Just having some of that tension released, even if it's just for a little bit of time.

00:49:06.644 --> 00:49:14.192
[Riv (DM)]: She pulls away, just so you know. Wipes her eyes a bit, and turns back to the group of you.

00:49:15.652 --> 00:49:26.639
[Riv (DM)]: And she says, simply, Thank you. It might not seem like much, but you have no idea what you've given me here.

00:49:28.440 --> 00:49:30.962
[Riv (DM)]: As far as I am concerned, our bargain is complete.

00:49:32.783 --> 00:49:34.844
[Riv (DM)]: Whenever you're ready, I will release him.

00:49:36.288 --> 00:49:38.790
[Riv (DM)]: Go quickly when I do, though. You will not stay helpless for long.

00:49:40.111 --> 00:49:44.335
[Riv (DM)]: And when you return, send us a message, and the stone is yours.

00:49:50.888 --> 00:49:59.811
[Lacer (Kai)]: Before we do, if you're here, who's sitting in the throne in the Veil of Eternal Night, like right now?

00:50:01.949 --> 00:50:05.772
[Riv (DM)]: Little smile, and she says, it's kind of hard to explain.

00:50:08.113 --> 00:50:08.434
[Riv (DM)]: I guess...

00:50:11.757 --> 00:50:13.458
[Riv (DM)]: I'm not really here, is the thing.

00:50:14.720 --> 00:50:16.221
[Riv (DM)]: I am, but I'm not.

00:50:20.145 --> 00:50:31.816
[Riv (DM)]: And she just kind of walks over to you and sort of like, you know, holds out a hand for you to touch. And you touch it and you feel it, it feels solid. But then she just sort of smiles slightly and your hand just passes straight on through it again.

00:50:33.900 --> 00:50:35.233
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's very tricky.

00:50:40.864 --> 00:50:47.709
[Riv (DM)]: I will let you know, she has not been with us any of this time. It was just me.

00:50:50.231 --> 00:50:59.337
[Riv (DM)]: It took, well, quite a bit of convincing on my end, but she was willing to grant me this time.

00:51:04.401 --> 00:51:08.624
[Riv (DM)]: In exchange for the deal, of course, but the time given, nonetheless, was

00:51:11.080 --> 00:51:12.040
[Riv (DM)]: Everything I could dream.

00:51:13.461 --> 00:51:15.482
[Riv (DM)]: It's only temporary. I will go back to her soon.

00:51:16.882 --> 00:51:23.525
[Riv (DM)]: And I will continue to hold my vigil for as long as I must. But for now, I have this time.

00:51:26.926 --> 00:51:28.487
[Riv (DM)]: She walks over to you, Ryo.

00:51:35.110 --> 00:51:36.110
[Riv (DM)]: I've seen you watching me.

00:51:37.471 --> 00:51:40.392
[Riv (DM)]: And I am sorry for what it's worth.

00:51:43.340 --> 00:51:54.066
[Riv (DM)]: I hope you know that, well, she kind of pauses a little bit and you see some emotions cross her face. And she reaches out a hand for your face.

00:51:55.287 --> 00:51:56.127
[Riv (DM)]: Do you pull away?

00:52:00.690 --> 00:52:02.471
[Riv (DM)]: Her hand just like touches the side of your face.

00:52:04.272 --> 00:52:10.736
[Riv (DM)]: And yeah, as she touches your face, you kind of, you feel like an immense,

00:52:12.096 --> 00:52:19.099
[Riv (DM)]: Knowledge and experience. Like this is a towering woman standing in front of you. Just sort of like flashes through you.

00:52:20.120 --> 00:52:25.882
[Riv (DM)]: But she smiles warmly and says, I see you in him. I see a lot of you in my son.

00:52:28.103 --> 00:52:28.904
[Riv (DM)]: Wish you would have met him.

00:52:30.404 --> 00:52:30.604
[Riv (DM)]: But...

00:52:34.206 --> 00:52:36.207
[Riv (DM)]: Some things don't go the ways you hope.

00:52:37.687 --> 00:52:38.728
[Riv (DM)]: I know he would be proud of you though.

00:52:42.312 --> 00:52:54.197
[Riv (DM)]: You hold the weight of thousands of years. I hope you know that the weight of expectations don't go with that.

00:52:55.578 --> 00:53:01.300
[Riv (DM)]: It's still your free choice what you want to do.

00:53:04.262 --> 00:53:09.224
[Riv (DM)]: But just know that at least someone from your family is thinking of you.

00:53:14.544 --> 00:53:17.805
[Riv (DM)]: And she steps back away.

00:53:20.407 --> 00:53:28.710
[Riv (DM)]: And yeah, she kind of goes back to Praetor and just kind of looks at him.

00:53:31.451 --> 00:53:42.776
[Riv (DM)]: And they sort of start idly discussing a few things, silly old inside jokes and things like that, just catching up on things a little bit. And they sit down on the side of the dais.

00:53:44.776 --> 00:54:11.528
[Riv (DM)]: Just chat for a bit. And as they chat, you see she sort of starts growing warmer, smiling more regularly, and eventually sort of looks over at you and almost mid-sentence starts to fade a bit. It's like that wind came back somehow into the chamber and just a part of her just sort of blows away. And another bit of wind comes through, another section of her blows away in the other direction.

00:54:12.720 --> 00:54:18.342
[Riv (DM)]: Praetor just sort of smiles, reaches out her hand one more, sort of just passes through where her head would be, and she's gone.

00:54:29.324 --> 00:54:40.887
[Lacer (Kai)]: That was very... She disappeared.

00:54:29.447 --> 00:54:32.188
[Riv (DM)]: Praetor stands up and just looks at you.

00:54:37.632 --> 00:54:38.332
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait, she's gone?

00:54:37.740 --> 00:54:38.737
[JC (Ryo)]: Like, she's gone.

00:54:40.152 --> 00:54:40.532
[Riv (DM)]: She's gone.

00:54:40.907 --> 00:54:44.028
[Lacer (Kai)]: Wow, that was very short.

00:54:46.598 --> 00:54:48.840
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: How do we get the stone if she's gone?

00:54:50.345 --> 00:54:51.462
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Send her a message, she said.

00:54:52.862 --> 00:54:52.962
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Ah.

00:54:53.791 --> 00:54:57.672
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, because she just has to release him, and then his spirit will...

00:54:58.946 --> 00:55:00.988
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, but we still need the stone.

00:54:59.072 --> 00:55:04.272
[Lacer (Kai)]: Go back to the... No, that's after we kill the Archdevil.

00:55:05.111 --> 00:55:06.912
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, we still need the stone.

00:55:08.524 --> 00:55:10.855
[JC (Ryo)]: Which we get after we kill the Yachtable.

00:55:12.680 --> 00:55:15.724
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay, so we'll go back to the veil and pick it up.

00:55:19.257 --> 00:55:24.440
[Riv (DM)]: From what you gathered, she said, send her a message and she will get the stone to you.

00:55:29.543 --> 00:55:29.944
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Fair enough.

00:55:31.624 --> 00:55:31.824
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

00:55:34.325 --> 00:55:37.047
[Riv (DM)]: Praetor stands up and says, well, my friends.

00:55:39.368 --> 00:55:42.810
[Riv (DM)]: Seems one part of your journey is complete.

00:55:46.412 --> 00:55:46.612
[Riv (DM)]: I...

00:55:48.104 --> 00:56:00.395
[Riv (DM)]: Apologize for any deception that might have been made on my part here. I did genuinely not know of what her fate was.

00:56:01.696 --> 00:56:02.256
[Riv (DM)]: I had hopes.

00:56:05.139 --> 00:56:09.823
[Riv (DM)]: And there was the slightest chance that you would learn something.

00:56:05.864 --> 00:56:06.337
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Who is she?

00:56:10.583 --> 00:56:18.706
[Riv (DM)]: But I was still genuinely trying to help you. Stone is still essential. To bring about the Crimson Shadow's demise.

00:56:19.927 --> 00:56:21.648
[Riv (DM)]: And that should be our main focus right now.

00:56:23.710 --> 00:56:24.591
[Riv (DM)]: Who is she, you say?

00:56:26.072 --> 00:56:32.517
[Riv (DM)]: You've just met Ellesandra Sulmara. The... ancestor of your friend here.

00:56:33.618 --> 00:56:34.339
[Riv (DM)]: Previous ruler.

00:56:36.160 --> 00:56:50.595
[Riv (DM)]: She... at the height of the conflict between our people here in Sumara and the Vale of Eternal Night. Went alone into the darkness and never returned.

00:56:51.856 --> 00:56:52.637
[Riv (DM)]: The war ending.

00:56:55.239 --> 00:57:00.542
[Riv (DM)]: She is celebrated as a hero as it is assumed that she sacrificed herself to end the war.

00:57:04.005 --> 00:57:04.445
[Riv (DM)]: Now we know.

00:57:05.786 --> 00:57:11.890
[Riv (DM)]: We know we have confirmed the terrors that she endures in order to hold the darkness at bay.

00:57:13.451 --> 00:57:14.192
[Riv (DM)]: And she's very proud

00:57:15.420 --> 00:57:34.913
[Riv (DM)]: Of your great grandmother, Ryo.

00:57:23.316 --> 00:57:31.868
[JC (Ryo)]: Two quick questions before we go. Do you know of any way to get her back away from Mirileth?

00:57:36.855 --> 00:57:37.775
[Riv (DM)]: All things are possible.

00:57:39.857 --> 00:57:50.266
[Riv (DM)]: But the question next would be what happens If she is separated, what would Mirileth do?

00:57:53.157 --> 00:57:54.707
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, who would keep her in check?

00:58:02.192 --> 00:58:02.792
[Riv (DM)]: Your next question?

00:58:02.996 --> 00:58:12.884
[JC (Ryo)]: Great question. Oh, other than my auntie Erendor's mother, do I have any other relatives alive out there?

00:58:15.881 --> 00:58:16.125
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Avernus.

00:58:17.852 --> 00:58:19.753
[Riv (DM)]: None living, I'm afraid.

00:58:26.416 --> 00:58:26.896
[JC (Ryo)]: Very well.

00:58:32.608 --> 00:58:33.117
[JC (Ryo)]: To the hells.

00:58:34.768 --> 00:58:39.550
[Lacer (Kai)]: I have a question, a weird question though.

00:58:41.082 --> 00:58:45.644
[Riv (DM)]: He hiccups again and another big ball of snow comes out. He's like, sorry, excuse me.

00:58:47.792 --> 00:58:59.576
[Lacer (Kai)]: So when the shattering happened, like, I don't know, two and a half thousand years ago, did the devils play any part in the Chaos Wars?

00:59:02.580 --> 00:59:04.241
[Riv (DM)]: Mmm. They did not.

00:59:06.003 --> 00:59:11.186
[Riv (DM)]: The conflict was, at that point, mainly confined to the material realm.

00:59:13.828 --> 00:59:22.474
[Riv (DM)]: They, I'm sure, would have profited from the souls, people making bargains for power and that sort of thing, but they were only ancillary.

00:59:23.575 --> 00:59:25.176
[Riv (DM)]: They were not directly involved in the conflict.

00:59:27.456 --> 00:59:33.659
[Lacer (Kai)]: That changes my opinion, because if they were part of it, I would have gone to the hells and killed every devil I could.

00:59:35.920 --> 00:59:38.901
[Riv (DM)]: Oh now that, that would be an impressive feat.

00:59:42.204 --> 00:59:49.668
[Lacer (Kai)]: But now that I know that they didn't participate, it's given me a different opinion on them.

00:59:56.346 --> 00:59:56.606
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

00:59:59.687 --> 01:00:00.067
[Riv (DM)]: Very well.

01:00:02.228 --> 01:00:03.168
[Riv (DM)]: Do you plan to leave soon then?

01:00:06.624 --> 01:00:07.344
[Lacer (Kai)]: I look at Ryo.

01:00:08.665 --> 01:00:09.665
[Lacer (Kai)]: Are we going soon?

01:00:11.392 --> 01:00:29.793
[JC (Ryo)]: We need to try and get there before he reforms, so the sooner the better.

01:00:15.486 --> 01:00:34.732
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, I don't think he's been released yet. We haven't messaged her to release him. Oh, that is true.

01:00:29.993 --> 01:00:32.596
[JC (Ryo)]: If we can get to where his body is before he's even released.

01:00:34.972 --> 01:00:41.943
[Lacer (Kai)]: We could get there. And then, but we'll have to dig out, we can do some, I guess, investigating.

01:00:50.432 --> 01:00:53.436
[Lacer (Kai)]: So where in the hells are we going anyway?

01:00:58.720 --> 01:00:59.400
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Quick question.

01:01:00.781 --> 01:01:03.503
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can messages cross planes?

01:01:05.232 --> 01:01:06.353
[Riv (DM)]: Sending can, yeah.

01:01:08.948 --> 01:01:22.353
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, they seemed to work fine when I was messaging the young girl in Val... What's it called?

01:01:24.804 --> 01:01:25.184
[Riv (DM)]: Miriel.

01:01:25.694 --> 01:01:29.335
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, in this case, yeah, Val Miriel, the other day.

01:01:39.266 --> 01:01:43.052
[Riv (DM)]: Great. So is there any final bits of preparation you guys want to do?

01:01:44.476 --> 01:01:49.458
[Lacer (Kai)]: Anyone want to ask Praetor Varanus anything since he is so knowledgeable?

01:01:59.572 --> 01:02:06.241
[Riv (DM)]: The bane of the DM is giving the players a super knowledgeable character that they can ask all the questions and get any answer.

01:02:06.905 --> 01:02:12.828
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm looking at Berberis because I know he's got some juicy question he wants to ask, but I don't know if he wants to ask it.

01:02:13.300 --> 01:02:16.383
[Vael (Berberis)]: No, no, I'm half asleep with the keyboard.

01:02:18.544 --> 01:02:26.431
[Vael (Berberis)]: If he has any information on the layout of the hells and where we're going to find this one that we need to kill within the hells is probably handy.

01:02:29.674 --> 01:02:36.640
[Vael (Berberis)]: Or any advice on finding a specific demon within the hells, which there are probably many, many, many, many, many, many, many.

01:02:40.448 --> 01:02:51.791
[Riv (DM)]: So I guess like to recap the research that you did the other day. See if I can bring up what I gave you guys.

01:02:51.308 --> 01:02:55.072
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I had to ride on the river Styx to get to the second layer. It's where he'll be.

01:02:54.332 --> 01:02:54.612
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:02:55.092 --> 01:02:58.736
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: He's gonna be the fastest way there, but...

01:02:57.273 --> 01:02:57.673
[Riv (DM)]: So the.

01:02:57.350 --> 01:02:58.335
[Lacer (Kai)]: Golden Fuse.

01:02:59.914 --> 01:03:05.235
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, perfect. So everyone who enters the Hells arrives in Avernus.

01:03:06.415 --> 01:03:10.937
[Riv (DM)]: This is the land of the Blood War. Eternal conflict between devils and demons.

01:03:12.318 --> 01:03:28.149
[Riv (DM)]: The next plane, the next circle of the hells is Dis, which is known as the Iron City. It's not just on the second layer, it pretty much is the second layer, an entire city that just spans the entire horizon.

01:03:30.370 --> 01:03:38.816
[Riv (DM)]: It's believed that he will be reforming in Dis. And I think I would have told you, yeah, I'm pretty sure I told you

01:03:38.028 --> 01:03:44.312
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, that was a theater, yeah.

01:03:38.368 --> 01:03:39.993
[JC (Ryo)]: He gave us the Rustward Quarter.

01:03:40.260 --> 01:03:41.961
[Riv (DM)]: Correct, the Rustward quarter.

01:03:41.077 --> 01:03:43.124
[JC (Ryo)]: In a theatre in the Rustward Quarter.

01:03:44.943 --> 01:03:49.966
[Riv (DM)]: There was a theatre where people might know something in the Rustward. Correct, yep.

01:03:52.788 --> 01:03:56.470
[Riv (DM)]: That was from a natural 20, I believe, in the history library.

01:04:01.261 --> 01:04:06.084
[Lacer (Kai)]: Did we find out what the environment was? Was it like on fire when we enter the...

01:04:07.100 --> 01:04:09.624
[Lacer (Kai)]: The hell plane or the realm of the hells.

01:04:10.240 --> 01:04:17.023
[Riv (DM)]: It'll be temperate. It won't just instantly start burning you. It's not like the plane of fire.

01:04:15.912 --> 01:04:21.259
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, that's what I was thinking. We go in and we start taking flame damage as we burn to our deaths.

01:04:17.783 --> 01:04:18.023
[Riv (DM)]: Nah.

01:04:22.265 --> 01:04:34.470
[Riv (DM)]: Nah, it'll be hot. It'll be very stressful if you travel for too long, but chances are you might start requiring some sort of checks to push through. But yeah, it's not just like scorching fire.

01:04:35.572 --> 01:04:47.158
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Is there a UV light? Will we get a tan? So we will get a tan.

01:04:40.288 --> 01:04:45.670
[Riv (DM)]: Well, there is probably some magical sunscreen somewhere, if you want it.

01:04:40.532 --> 01:05:03.112
[Lacer (Kai)]: That'll be alright.

01:04:48.450 --> 01:04:50.131
[Riv (DM)]: Hey, if you want a tan, you can get a tan.

01:04:48.739 --> 01:04:49.340
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Interesting.

01:04:52.748 --> 01:04:54.503
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Make sure you're happy to deal with the cancer as well.

01:04:54.972 --> 01:05:01.494
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, exactly. I don't know if Suramar has like a mole map or anything like that, so you might be out of luck.

01:05:02.627 --> 01:05:03.908
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Humors for everyone!

01:05:04.353 --> 01:05:06.455
[Lacer (Kai)]: We'll cure it with lesser restoration.

01:05:07.836 --> 01:05:37.740
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, true. Dude that's crazy that like a spell like that exists that just cures any disease like you have to balance it man like um most priests just can't access that i think otherwise the game is like the world is just broken yeah all right sweet um yeah cool anything else is there any anything else you wanted to stock up on in the city or are we just gonna head straight there

01:05:25.498 --> 01:05:39.770
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yes.

01:05:39.808 --> 01:05:40.229
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Dondar.

01:05:41.391 --> 01:05:42.332
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, you go. Go ahead.

01:05:43.232 --> 01:05:45.154
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Dondar wants to send a message to Belinda.

01:05:46.836 --> 01:06:07.222
[Riv (DM)]: Right. And what does Dondar say? Yeah, we did.

01:05:49.779 --> 01:06:00.512
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Dondar does not have sending prepared. Can he pass it ritually or something or pretend to have a long rest and change his spells?

01:06:01.612 --> 01:06:05.375
[Lacer (Kai)]: We did have one last night. If you wanted to do it, you should have.

01:06:07.722 --> 01:06:15.624
[Riv (DM)]: We did intentionally lock in Jose to like, make sure you prep your spells last night. But I think it doesn't kind of change.

01:06:13.772 --> 01:06:39.586
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, it's true, I have... Not really.

01:06:16.196 --> 01:06:19.577
[Riv (DM)]: Spell on a short rest, so you could potentially change it once you.

01:06:21.866 --> 01:06:24.788
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess you could be short resting right now as we're talking.

01:06:28.881 --> 01:06:34.124
[Riv (DM)]: The other option would be you could ask Kai to send unless there is a secret message.

01:06:42.547 --> 01:06:43.508
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can you send it Kai?

01:06:45.468 --> 01:06:53.416
[Lacer (Kai)]: I can send it. I don't know where she is. I can't 100% guarantee it will make it to her, but I can try.

01:06:59.156 --> 01:07:04.419
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, if Kiwi wasn't so retarded, he would know where she was. Dondar knows.

01:07:05.120 --> 01:07:35.424
[Riv (DM)]: Hm. The last you heard from her, she had sent you a magical message in the form of a letter that flew by itself through the sky, and it whacked you on the side of your face while you were travelling up the Eastern Road to Valnoran. And the letter essentially just spelled out that she did in fact manage to find her destination, her dream, her life's goal essentially, which was finding a way to get to the flying city of Archangel. The bastion of knowledge of all arcane practitioners on the world.

01:07:37.366 --> 01:07:45.375
[Riv (DM)]: It was what she was crafting in Lighthaven, the rod of transportation that would allow her to get there, attuned to Archangel.

01:07:46.677 --> 01:07:49.480
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, and so she made it there. That would have been the last you heard of her.

01:07:53.476 --> 01:08:00.642
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: So Donda wants to send a message that says, Hey Belinda, how's life? Going to hell.

01:08:02.364 --> 01:08:03.184
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Do you have any advice?

01:08:08.821 --> 01:08:14.643
[Lacer (Kai)]: I will say that, but I say, hey, this is Kai. Donda wants to tell you this.

01:08:33.708 --> 01:08:34.388
[Riv (DM)]: Voice comes back.

01:08:36.789 --> 01:08:37.749
[Riv (DM)]: Great to hear from you.

01:08:40.550 --> 01:08:41.930
[Riv (DM)]: Or never been to the hells myself.

01:08:44.791 --> 01:08:45.891
[Riv (DM)]: Don't give out your name for free.

01:08:47.572 --> 01:08:51.513
[Riv (DM)]: Stock up on potions of resistance of fire if you can.

01:08:53.093 --> 01:08:54.494
[Riv (DM)]: Make sure you bring some food and water.

01:08:56.074 --> 01:08:57.555
[Riv (DM)]: You might struggle to find some down there.

01:08:59.295 --> 01:09:00.456
[Riv (DM)]: Good luck. Let me know how it goes.

01:09:01.456 --> 01:09:02.076
[Riv (DM)]: Bring me a present.

01:09:04.128 --> 01:09:06.810
[Riv (DM)]: That was probably long 25 words, but hey, we hand wave those.

01:09:07.017 --> 01:09:08.163
[JC (Ryo)]: I was gonna say, that was about 16.

01:09:09.653 --> 01:09:10.553
[Riv (DM)]: I'm allowed to be in the rules.

01:09:11.854 --> 01:09:14.837
[Riv (DM)]: When we bring back an NPC we haven't heard from in like two years.

01:09:16.734 --> 01:09:17.915
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, okay.

01:09:17.399 --> 01:09:29.429
[Riv (DM)]: We can say it was a couple of sendings.

01:09:19.997 --> 01:09:28.343
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, she's probably got like magical trinkets that with she's probably got a ring of spell storing and just like spammed out sending couple of times.

01:09:29.849 --> 01:09:32.391
[Riv (DM)]: Ring of increased duration of sending.

01:09:35.469 --> 01:09:38.271
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's been encrypted and condensed so she could fit more in.

01:09:38.837 --> 01:09:39.057
[Riv (DM)]: True.

01:09:41.132 --> 01:10:03.227
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm just gonna say damn she's got she sent me a long-ass message and this that's what she said this is what she said stock up on potions of fire resist bring water food and a present and I look directly at Donna she wants a present

01:09:41.297 --> 01:09:44.375
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Ring of, I have more SMS tokens than you.

01:10:05.879 --> 01:10:06.399
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Naturally.

01:10:12.232 --> 01:10:15.215
[Lacer (Kai)]: And she also said, don't give out our true names.

01:10:12.404 --> 01:10:12.764
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Excellent.

01:10:15.295 --> 01:10:22.260
[Lacer (Kai)]: So I'm guessing we should make ourselves some aliases to be known by.

01:10:24.244 --> 01:10:33.747
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: In this, in D&D, is your name your true name? In other fantasy lore, your true name is not your name.

01:10:35.147 --> 01:10:35.507
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Is that...

01:10:36.786 --> 01:10:47.794
[Riv (DM)]: So, yes, like you would know what your true name is and you would either be choosing to give it to people or not. And I suppose it's up to you.

01:10:49.075 --> 01:10:50.656
[Riv (DM)]: So it wouldn't be hidden from you either.

01:10:50.072 --> 01:10:50.432
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay.

01:10:51.392 --> 01:10:55.955
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: So this is where we find out someone in the party's true name is not the name they've been going by.

01:10:57.288 --> 01:10:59.029
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Ooh.

01:10:57.952 --> 01:10:58.873
[Riv (DM)]: Potentially, yeah.

01:10:58.777 --> 01:10:59.957
[Lacer (Kai)]: How would you know?

01:11:00.458 --> 01:11:02.420
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Because they use the name they've been going by.

01:11:00.549 --> 01:11:01.549
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: How would you know?

01:11:03.140 --> 01:11:13.516
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I mean, in most circumstances, like, for example, on the material realm, it doesn't really matter. Right? But potentially, there are some places where you might not want to be giving it.

01:11:21.456 --> 01:11:21.996
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Interesting.

01:11:24.271 --> 01:11:25.072
[Lacer (Kai)]: Or we can call.

01:11:26.676 --> 01:11:29.117
[Lacer (Kai)]: General Slayer. General Slayer.

01:11:30.177 --> 01:11:32.118
[Lacer (Kai)]: Enryo can be the Hag Slayer.

01:11:33.824 --> 01:11:38.347
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I mean, surely last hit. General last hit is more accurate.

01:11:43.380 --> 01:11:45.405
[Riv (DM)]: Alrighty. Cool. Cool, cool, cool. That was awesome.

01:11:47.071 --> 01:11:50.313
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: OK, well, I will definitely be doing those things.

01:11:47.349 --> 01:11:47.590
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:11:51.679 --> 01:11:51.959
[Riv (DM)]: Great.

01:11:51.703 --> 01:11:55.562
[Lacer (Kai)]: Damn, we don't have any potions of How about the rest of us?

01:12:00.419 --> 01:12:12.905
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I think theoretically I have. Fire resistance, but probably not that much. Oh, sorry. Yeah, yeah, we should care for each other.

01:12:15.769 --> 01:12:16.290
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Good idea.

01:12:16.108 --> 01:12:19.271
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, so do we do a bit of stocking up first?

01:12:20.115 --> 01:12:20.696
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yes, please.

01:12:21.416 --> 01:12:36.203
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess we can, yeah, we need to stock up on a few things before we head out based on Belinda's and everyone else's information that we gathered.

01:12:38.176 --> 01:12:40.817
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Dondar also wants to steal the scrying ball.

01:12:42.529 --> 01:12:45.912
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, when are you going to do that?

01:12:44.058 --> 01:12:44.418
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, no.

01:12:45.048 --> 01:12:45.528
[Lacer (Kai)]: From Rio.

01:12:45.078 --> 01:12:47.799
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: He wants to use it, if he can.

01:12:52.360 --> 01:12:52.740
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can he?

01:12:52.360 --> 01:12:55.219
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, you need to ask Rio.

01:12:52.880 --> 01:12:58.882
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can he?

01:12:54.652 --> 01:12:55.313
[JC (Ryo)]: I can use it.

01:12:59.602 --> 01:13:01.203
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Brio can use it. Can I use it?

01:13:03.263 --> 01:13:04.984
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Or is it only the person who's attuned to it?

01:13:07.107 --> 01:13:11.952
[JC (Ryo)]: You'll need to attune to it, and you've got like three or four attuned items already, even though the max is three.

01:13:13.534 --> 01:13:14.976
[JC (Ryo)]: So you'll have to drop something to use it.

01:13:13.844 --> 01:13:14.825
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I've got two.

01:13:17.977 --> 01:13:21.859
[Lacer (Kai)]: Why didn't you ask to borrow it off Rio, Donda? You don't have to steal it off him.

01:13:23.152 --> 01:13:24.313
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, I meant borrow.

01:13:25.635 --> 01:13:30.859
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You know, steal as in the, you know, I'll just steal that potion from you, I'll get it back.

01:13:32.701 --> 01:13:33.021
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You know.

01:13:32.905 --> 01:13:38.688
[Lacer (Kai)]: Right. You're just gonna drink the potion and then give it back, like, pee it back out or something?

01:13:39.367 --> 01:13:40.548
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, yeah, just like that.

01:13:42.904 --> 01:13:44.686
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: That's how dwarves recycle.

01:13:46.304 --> 01:13:47.364
[Lacer (Kai)]: Dwarves are funny.

01:13:49.005 --> 01:13:53.426
[Lacer (Kai)]: I can understand why Ryo and Erendor have this weird aversion to Dondonio.

01:13:54.154 --> 01:13:59.839
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, oh, oh, now the shit's coming out. On the edge of hell, you're bringing this up now.

01:13:58.327 --> 01:14:05.769
[Lacer (Kai)]: Well...

01:14:02.721 --> 01:14:06.184
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Anyone else? No, no, no one else. No one else have a go.

01:14:10.248 --> 01:14:12.537
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait. The other guys have an aversion to me?

01:14:18.140 --> 01:14:18.980
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Let's just forget this.

01:14:18.328 --> 01:14:34.116
[Lacer (Kai)]: very very very very minor very minor that's that's what i that's what that's that's what i think but i don't think very much so you know

01:14:21.582 --> 01:14:30.687
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wow, you are stupider than you look. I mean, that's good to know. Thank you for telling me this troubling information.

01:14:35.029 --> 01:14:35.270
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yep.

01:14:36.930 --> 01:14:38.371
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Good. Team bonding.

01:14:44.932 --> 01:14:50.700
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You know what? It can wait. It can wait till we get back from hell, because who knows?

01:14:53.132 --> 01:14:55.093
[JC (Ryo)]: I look at Praetor. I got a question for you.

01:14:57.260 --> 01:15:00.923
[Riv (DM)]: He's been watching this, just a coy smile on his face. Go on.

01:15:03.377 --> 01:15:11.001
[JC (Ryo)]: Do we have to have enchanted weapons to work in Avernus, or will non-enchanted magical weapons work?

01:15:13.412 --> 01:15:14.072
[Riv (DM)]: A good question.

01:15:15.303 --> 01:15:16.404
[JC (Ryo)]: My hand goes to my bow.

01:15:17.475 --> 01:15:18.836
[Riv (DM)]: Are you planning on doing much fighting?

01:15:22.044 --> 01:15:28.248
[JC (Ryo)]: I'm assuming we're going to have to do some fighting to kill this guy's body before it reincarnates.

01:15:30.509 --> 01:15:34.812
[JC (Ryo)]: Otherwise we'll have to kill said archdevil after he's reincarnated.

01:15:36.482 --> 01:15:47.350
[Riv (DM)]: Indeed. There are many beings in the Circles of Hell that are resistant to unenchanted weapons.

01:15:52.194 --> 01:15:58.940
[Riv (DM)]: It's unknown, I guess, how likely you are to encounter them, but it's good to be prepared.

01:16:07.200 --> 01:16:15.046
[JC (Ryo)]: As I put my longbow on my back and swap to the plus one short sword and star four shield.

01:16:16.901 --> 01:16:24.850
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, remember you do have the arrows, right?

01:16:20.850 --> 01:16:23.552
[JC (Ryo)]: I know I have enhance, I know I have a spell.

01:16:25.353 --> 01:16:29.216
[JC (Ryo)]: I do have a few arrows, but do they need the bow to be enchanted as well?

01:16:30.157 --> 01:16:31.738
[Riv (DM)]: Nah, nah, when you shoot them they count.

01:16:34.281 --> 01:16:34.541
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:16:36.020 --> 01:16:38.842
[Lacer (Kai)]: I think I don't need a few more of those if you're gonna spam them.

01:16:40.236 --> 01:16:47.048
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah well I've got magic weapon as well so I can turn my bow magical. Does that work for the ammo as well or no?

01:16:47.408 --> 01:16:52.252
[Lacer (Kai)]: Or does he have to turn the arrows magical? Because he's shooting arrows from the bow.

01:16:54.068 --> 01:17:00.714
[Riv (DM)]: No, so if you've got a plus one bow, you don't need like magical arrows for it to count.

01:17:02.836 --> 01:17:16.628
[Riv (DM)]: And if you have a magical bow and you shoot magical arrows, I have no idea if they stack. Does anyone know? Vale, have you ever encountered that? I actually genuinely don't know if they stack.

01:17:15.892 --> 01:17:40.612
[Vael (Berberis)]: I think yeah the arrow is just a modifier on the bow itself so like a plus one bow shooting a plus two arrow would do the the arrow doesn't add to hit but it does add to the damage if i recall so a plus one bow shooting a plus one arrow would have a plus one to hit and a plus two to damage

01:17:20.211 --> 01:17:21.692
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that's an interesting one.

01:17:28.992 --> 01:17:36.457
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Yeah, that's interesting. I might actually do a bit of research on that later because I don't think I've ever encountered that.

01:17:41.541 --> 01:17:41.781
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:17:41.960 --> 01:17:43.741
[Lacer (Kai)]: Ah, so there's damage stacks.

01:17:44.508 --> 01:17:50.253
[Vael (Berberis)]: But the arrow is just, it doesn't add to, because it's not a weapon, it's just a modifier.

01:17:51.007 --> 01:17:51.247
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

01:17:52.113 --> 01:17:55.776
[JC (Ryo)]: Magic arrows don't make it a magic attack, because the bow was non-magical.

01:17:56.271 --> 01:18:07.800
[Lacer (Kai)]: Do you have a magic bow?

01:17:56.631 --> 01:17:58.172
[Riv (DM)]: No, no, it would, it would.

01:17:56.858 --> 01:17:57.699
[Vael (Berberis)]: But they would, yeah.

01:17:59.432 --> 01:18:00.192
[Riv (DM)]: I'm just, I'm just...

01:17:59.561 --> 01:18:03.204
[Vael (Berberis)]: But also you can find normal arrows from a magical bow and it counts as a magic attack.

01:18:02.874 --> 01:18:04.674
[Riv (DM)]: They count as magical, yes, correct.

01:18:04.044 --> 01:18:06.447
[JC (Ryo)]: Okay, well I've only got seven magical arrows, so...

01:18:06.995 --> 01:18:07.155
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:18:09.389 --> 01:18:11.832
[JC (Ryo)]: If I cast magic weapon, I'll have a magic bow for an hour.

01:18:12.438 --> 01:18:12.638
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:18:14.038 --> 01:18:17.380
[Riv (DM)]: And to, I'm just, I actually just did a quick flick through.

01:18:18.900 --> 01:18:27.004
[Riv (DM)]: I actually think that in 2024, they removed all resistances to non-magical weapons. I think they took that out because it was bullshit.

01:18:26.048 --> 01:18:51.572
[Vael (Berberis)]: No, there is you I will be to look at like someone like monk.

01:18:27.944 --> 01:18:38.291
[Riv (DM)]: That's just, that's ringing a bell for me because I'm just flicking through basically every fiend and I don't think they actually have any resistance to non-measured weapons so I think he actually might be fine.

01:18:39.312 --> 01:18:57.564
[Riv (DM)]: I would say Praetor knows that and he is up to date on the 2024 rules and he would have let you know. Hmm.

01:18:47.248 --> 01:18:48.735
[Lacer (Kai)]: He just says, you're fine.

01:18:47.488 --> 01:18:50.139
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Village cares not for your modern rules.

01:18:52.456 --> 01:19:05.531
[Vael (Berberis)]: And if they don't have the ability where you hit a certain level and now you can punch ghosts, that would make sense that they would just remove the...

01:19:01.600 --> 01:19:04.282
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I think they changed all that stuff just because it was kind of dumb.

01:19:06.372 --> 01:19:12.800
[Vael (Berberis)]: They've gone and Elder Scrolls to our D&D. You no longer need weapons to hurt ghosts, you can just do it.

01:19:10.726 --> 01:19:23.735
[Riv (DM)]: They added... Yeah. I think they, to compensate, they added a bunch more like normal resistances to other stuff, like elemental types and things like that. So they kind of like, they tried to tweak in both directions, I think.

01:19:26.497 --> 01:19:31.460
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, cool. All right, sweet. Shopping will hand waver. Izzy, so what supplies do you want to get?

01:19:34.368 --> 01:19:35.128
[Lacer (Kai)]: I like go first.

01:19:35.544 --> 01:19:35.945
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Drink.

01:19:37.856 --> 01:19:38.377
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, go for it.

01:19:38.726 --> 01:19:39.627
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Magical resist.

01:19:40.111 --> 01:19:45.354
[Lacer (Kai)]: I want two shovels, two picks, and two sledgehammers.

01:19:47.127 --> 01:19:52.614
[Riv (DM)]: Okay. Okay, cool. Yeah, easy enough. Okay.

01:19:54.180 --> 01:19:56.201
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, that's what I'm going to put in the bag of holding.

01:20:01.084 --> 01:20:01.644
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess I'll...

01:20:03.016 --> 01:20:14.381
[Lacer (Kai)]: Get some potions of fire resist. How many do you think we need? Oh, okay.

01:20:11.060 --> 01:20:20.123
[Riv (DM)]: You would be more limited on how many they have available. So Arcanist level would probably be the one you'd go to and she would have two and they are 300 gold each.

01:20:20.743 --> 01:20:24.485
[Lacer (Kai)]: Wow, that is so expensive, but okay.

01:20:23.820 --> 01:20:24.501
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can we make them?

01:20:26.166 --> 01:20:30.027
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, but they're going to take time, don't know.

01:20:26.364 --> 01:20:27.925
[Riv (DM)]: Probably not quickly enough.

01:20:30.047 --> 01:20:33.376
[Lacer (Kai)]: Making potions takes ages. Even with help.

01:20:32.666 --> 01:20:34.707
[Riv (DM)]: Shovels are a couple of gold each as well.

01:20:33.133 --> 01:20:34.856
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Thought these elves were magical.

01:20:35.907 --> 01:20:39.308
[Riv (DM)]: I think you could get your two shovels and two perks for like five gold.

01:20:36.077 --> 01:20:45.161
[Lacer (Kai)]: I can... Alright, I will mark off five gold and stuff. Someone can do the potion stuff while I do this stuff.

01:20:49.903 --> 01:20:52.444
[Lacer (Kai)]: And whatever else. What else do you want?

01:20:52.253 --> 01:20:55.335
[JC (Ryo)]: Any potions of healing? Greater healing? Superior healing? I don't know.

01:20:56.172 --> 01:21:00.978
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I think you actually bought a few potions of healing last time you were here.

01:21:02.099 --> 01:21:09.327
[Riv (DM)]: So there wouldn't be any standard potions of healing, but you would have one greater potion of healing, which you can pick up for 200 gold.

01:21:13.329 --> 01:21:16.892
[JC (Ryo)]: I dropped 200 gold for that. That's 20 blood, isn't it?

01:21:23.757 --> 01:21:27.426
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I'll grab one of the fire resists.

01:21:30.093 --> 01:21:30.975
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: How much was that again? Sorry.

01:21:31.604 --> 01:21:31.864
[Riv (DM)]: 300.

01:21:45.690 --> 01:21:48.891
[Riv (DM)]: Food and water, you can just hand wave. Easy enough to get.

01:21:52.412 --> 01:22:00.916
[Riv (DM)]: But yeah, I'd say you would have got the impression that you want to stack up on that because you don't necessarily trust the food that you might get down there.

01:22:01.640 --> 01:22:05.401
[Lacer (Kai)]: I got it covered. Water is covered. Food is covered with good berry.

01:22:08.370 --> 01:22:14.055
[JC (Ryo)]: Plot a greater healing from SRD, DDB, DDB items or equipment.

01:22:16.695 --> 01:22:17.295
[Riv (DM)]: Come again, sorry?

01:22:18.105 --> 01:22:21.827
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, I'm trying to rip items across. Is it where?

01:22:21.856 --> 01:22:23.217
[Riv (DM)]: Are you looking in the compendium?

01:22:22.187 --> 01:22:25.528
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, it's changed a bit. It's from the SRD.

01:22:27.918 --> 01:22:35.720
[Riv (DM)]: I don't know where you guys are looking, but so if you go to compendium packs, you should see at the top level, there's D&D, go to modern content, and it'll be under equipment.

01:22:31.304 --> 01:22:47.969
[Lacer (Kai)]: Let's add two shovels.

01:22:36.444 --> 01:22:37.968
[Riv (DM)]: And then you should see pushing.

01:22:38.752 --> 01:22:41.374
[JC (Ryo)]: Okay, I was just using the search all the Commendium packs.

01:22:41.959 --> 01:22:42.180
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:22:53.891 --> 01:22:56.032
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess it's a minus pick here.

01:23:06.135 --> 01:23:09.818
[JC (Ryo)]: Rations and a second... Second or third, second waterskin maybe?

01:23:11.079 --> 01:23:11.559
[JC (Ryo)]: How many?

01:23:11.124 --> 01:23:20.974
[Lacer (Kai)]: I've got all the water you need, Ryo. You know that decanter I bought way, way back?

01:23:17.765 --> 01:23:18.586
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, if it breaks.

01:23:18.080 --> 01:23:19.761
[Riv (DM)]: What happens if you lose it?

01:23:18.606 --> 01:23:34.560
[JC (Ryo)]: Do you want to charge me for a waterskin and say a dozen rations?

01:23:21.254 --> 01:23:22.735
[Lacer (Kai)]: I can create water with magic.

01:23:23.404 --> 01:23:23.964
[Riv (DM)]: It's true.

01:23:27.527 --> 01:23:28.808
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Okay. Easy as.

01:23:30.969 --> 01:23:31.790
[Riv (DM)]: We do a bit of shopping.

01:23:35.512 --> 01:23:40.836
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that's fine. Just, I don't know, take off like a gold. You'll be fine. Maybe at least like you do five silver.

01:23:42.217 --> 01:23:43.078
[Riv (DM)]: That sort of thing's quite cheap.

01:23:48.928 --> 01:23:50.930
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. Shopping is done.

01:23:54.508 --> 01:23:55.609
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: High resistance clothing?

01:23:56.395 --> 01:23:58.137
[Riv (DM)]: Did you randomly just roll fire then?

01:23:58.064 --> 01:23:58.707
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I did, yes.

01:23:59.058 --> 01:24:00.779
[Riv (DM)]: Holy shit.

01:24:00.315 --> 01:24:00.475
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Huh.

01:24:01.220 --> 01:24:01.820
[Riv (DM)]: Congrats.

01:24:04.142 --> 01:24:05.283
[Riv (DM)]: Good meme. All right, nice.

01:24:06.885 --> 01:24:09.928
[Riv (DM)]: Last call for shopping. Speak now or forever hold your peace.

01:24:10.885 --> 01:24:11.966
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: High resistance clothing?

01:24:13.511 --> 01:24:14.332
[Riv (DM)]: Resistance clothing?

01:24:15.991 --> 01:24:17.032
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Does such a thing exist?

01:24:18.697 --> 01:24:21.980
[Riv (DM)]: In theory, yeah, you could get magical items that have resistance.

01:24:23.421 --> 01:24:31.068
[Riv (DM)]: They would be very expensive and also quite rare. They would be used in the war effort, so unlikely to be available for you.

01:24:33.292 --> 01:24:47.698
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: But we have the king. I didn't even look at you!

01:24:37.493 --> 01:24:40.216
[Riv (DM)]: That is true, but unfortunately you can't just...

01:24:41.052 --> 01:24:45.236
[JC (Ryo)]: I'm going to start casting silence on Dondar every time he refers to me as royalty in a moment.

01:24:45.856 --> 01:24:46.837
[Lacer (Kai)]: He he he he he he.

01:24:47.718 --> 01:24:50.860
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You were the one who assumed I meant you.

01:24:52.163 --> 01:24:53.104
[JC (Ryo)]: That was above the table.

01:24:58.444 --> 01:25:10.449
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Okay, sweet. That's it then. I would say that we finish up. It's probably still before midday, I would put it.

01:25:12.270 --> 01:25:16.832
[Riv (DM)]: And we can start a break as we're standing before the Worldgate, ready to pass on through.

01:25:18.252 --> 01:25:32.718
[Riv (DM)]: I'll give you a bit of time over the break to sort out any items and stuff like that. If there's anything else you remember that you want to get, just grab it in the break and take off the right amount of gold for any sort of like minor stuff, tools and things like that. Just like, yeah, don't worry about it, just hand wave it, it's fine.

01:25:34.772 --> 01:25:37.935
[JC (Ryo)]: I was gonna buy like a dozen or two dozen arrows. I'm just trying to figure out the cost.

01:25:40.072 --> 01:25:43.353
[Vael (Berberis)]: Is there anywhere within this town where a man would buy holy water?

01:25:45.300 --> 01:25:49.042
[Lacer (Kai)]: I... I kinda asked for this, I think.

01:25:52.624 --> 01:25:56.986
[Lacer (Kai)]: And they don't have much silver. Like, even if I were to make it.

01:25:55.824 --> 01:25:56.084
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:25:58.325 --> 01:26:03.229
[Riv (DM)]: Holy Water is a tricky one because they are not very priestly here.

01:26:05.943 --> 01:26:06.924
[Vael (Berberis)]: What makes it holy?

01:26:06.491 --> 01:26:14.596
[Riv (DM)]: But, well, the gods, right, and the elven society here are quite secular.

01:26:13.776 --> 01:26:14.376
[Lacer (Kai)]: God lists.

01:26:15.277 --> 01:26:19.259
[Riv (DM)]: Well, not necessarily godless. Their god is the universe, right? Their god is the world tree.

01:26:19.580 --> 01:26:21.722
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, that's the World Trade, it's everything, right.

01:26:22.420 --> 01:26:22.900
[Riv (DM)]: Why don't you?

01:26:22.747 --> 01:26:26.380
[Vael (Berberis)]: So you're saying I need to go talk to the plant people to get holy water?

01:26:23.000 --> 01:26:23.320
[Riv (DM)]: Why don't we?

01:26:28.022 --> 01:26:31.102
[Riv (DM)]: Why don't we just roll a investigation check for you?

01:26:42.646 --> 01:26:45.166
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, yeah.

01:26:44.278 --> 01:26:46.200
[Lacer (Kai)]: Whoa, yep, that's rough.

01:26:47.127 --> 01:26:47.847
[Riv (DM)]: Have you got a?

01:26:48.868 --> 01:26:50.408
[Riv (DM)]: Have you got components that need holy water?

01:26:51.074 --> 01:26:52.612
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah, and protection from good naval.

01:26:53.972 --> 01:27:02.960
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm. Okay. Have a think over the break and see if you can come up with a ploy for how you could get it.

01:27:04.321 --> 01:27:17.112
[Riv (DM)]: And we'll see.

01:27:07.424 --> 01:27:10.145
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm gonna ask Ferethes if he's found any silver.

01:27:11.345 --> 01:27:14.206
[Lacer (Kai)]: I need silver powder. If not, I can craft it.

01:27:16.107 --> 01:27:32.291
[Lacer (Kai)]: But I need a bit of it. I need a fair bit of silver powder. I will tell you, every time I cast the ceremony spell, it's gonna create, I think, one for one for holy water and

01:27:19.394 --> 01:27:19.594
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:27:21.088 --> 01:27:21.943
[Vael (Berberis)]: How much do we have left?

01:27:34.556 --> 01:27:58.220
[Lacer (Kai)]: Where is my ceremony spell? It should be in level... Um, yeah, it'll be 25 gold pieces worth of powdered silver, so it's a one-to-one. Makes one vial of holy water. Will take me an hour to make every time, so I can spend a night making holy water.

01:27:59.948 --> 01:28:03.110
[Riv (DM)]: You could convert some silver coins into silver powder.

01:28:05.812 --> 01:28:08.635
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'll need Dondar. I need Dondar's power.

01:28:07.392 --> 01:28:07.572
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

01:28:09.633 --> 01:28:14.135
[Riv (DM)]: That would probably be the right way to do it, I think, if you want the protection spell.

01:28:15.996 --> 01:28:22.800
[Riv (DM)]: Fabricate into ceremony, into holy water.

01:28:23.409 --> 01:28:30.196
[Lacer (Kai)]: Do they have silver coins here in the city? Like, is there a place I can convert gold and plat into silver coin?

01:28:29.916 --> 01:28:55.777
[Riv (DM)]: Um yeah they they have normal currency here and because of their overlap with the um all right nice easy peasy all right cool um

01:28:34.977 --> 01:28:35.167
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Look.

01:28:35.830 --> 01:28:37.712
[Vael (Berberis)]: Honestly, I'll just grab a different spell.

01:28:35.968 --> 01:28:48.724
[Lacer (Kai)]: Can I... Alright, I will try, that's what I'll do in the break. In the break I'm gonna go to the elven coin exchange and exchange some currency for silver coin.

01:28:55.252 --> 01:28:57.827
[Vael (Berberis)]: Well, instead of making them not hit me, I will just hit them harder.

01:28:59.628 --> 01:29:06.216
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, oh man, it's good to finally have resources matter, eh?

01:29:08.298 --> 01:29:14.065
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, sweet, I'm gonna do a little note and catch you guys in 15 minutes, yeah? 20 minutes.

01:29:16.227 --> 01:29:16.808
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, it's already 20.

01:29:17.396 --> 01:29:18.203
[JC (Ryo)]: On the hour of quarter.

================= Session Break =================

01:54:10.964 --> 01:54:39.900
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: podcast dammit there was one last thing i wanted to do i've instantly forgotten what it was oh shit oh well yeah i can wait if we're not dead

01:54:14.064 --> 01:54:20.101
[Riv (DM)]: Alrighty. We're back. Okay, sweet. So we've got all our supplies. You guys have sorted out your inventories.

01:54:21.181 --> 01:54:27.607
[Riv (DM)]: We're standing in front of the world gate in the center of Sumara.

01:54:34.172 --> 01:54:34.492
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, no.

01:54:38.616 --> 01:54:40.077
[Riv (DM)]: Give me five seconds.

01:54:42.656 --> 01:54:43.325
[Lacer (Kai)]: While he's thinking.

01:54:43.040 --> 01:54:43.480
[Riv (DM)]: I believe in you.

01:54:43.080 --> 01:54:50.304
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I was just going to say open the Grimoire and have a read of it just for fun, but I can probably wait.

01:54:48.660 --> 01:54:57.710
[Riv (DM)]: Mmm. Did Donna do any shopping? We could say that you did it while the rest of the gang were out doing this stuff. You were just having a little browse for an hour.

01:54:59.630 --> 01:55:00.230
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Sure.

01:55:03.076 --> 01:55:03.256
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

01:55:04.172 --> 01:55:08.395
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, why not? I mean, I thought I went shopping as well, but that's fine.

01:55:13.076 --> 01:55:17.887
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: If there were limited potions, it sounds like there wasn't much I could buy anyway, so that's all good.

01:55:19.531 --> 01:55:21.576
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, crack it open, see what it's got to say.

01:55:23.372 --> 01:55:24.452
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool.

01:55:26.813 --> 01:55:29.714
[Riv (DM)]: There we go.

01:55:31.274 --> 01:55:32.895
[Riv (DM)]: You spend a bit of time studying.

01:55:34.955 --> 01:55:50.800
[Riv (DM)]: At first, it's quite difficult to wrap your mind and even eyes around it because the script on the page just keeps on dancing away. Whenever you look at a certain spot, the letters seem to flow away from where you're looking and it almost makes you feel a little bit dizzy. There's a little bit of a headache.

01:55:52.448 --> 01:56:01.124
[Riv (DM)]: It's been an hour kind of poking through it trying to see if you can make anything of it. I'll get you to make a intelligence saving throw for me, please.

01:56:03.188 --> 01:56:04.169
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Doesn't sound good.

01:56:07.251 --> 01:56:09.713
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, Foundry. White screen of death.

01:56:13.355 --> 01:56:14.276
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Anytime Foundry.

01:56:17.318 --> 01:56:19.480
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: It's still playing the music. I've just got a white screen.

01:56:22.208 --> 01:56:23.148
[Lacer (Kai)]: Just reload quickly.

01:56:27.606 --> 01:56:30.168
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: And I've been logged out. Okay. Nice.

01:56:42.176 --> 01:56:44.677
[Lacer (Kai)]: While Dondar's doing that, can I ask the party?

01:56:47.999 --> 01:56:49.819
[Lacer (Kai)]: Now I forgot what I was going to say. Shit.

01:56:52.033 --> 01:57:03.823
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

01:56:55.796 --> 01:57:01.280
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Sorry, so saving throw of something.

01:57:03.863 --> 01:57:17.474
[Riv (DM)]: Intelligence aim throw, yeah. Okay.

01:57:05.740 --> 01:57:10.299
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Hey, I'm a smart guy!

01:57:18.335 --> 01:57:19.055
[Riv (DM)]: Very good. Yeah.

01:57:21.260 --> 01:57:27.762
[Riv (DM)]: You got a little bit dizzy, you get a little bit of a headache, but eventually you're able to kind of see spot patterns and stuff like that.

01:57:30.023 --> 01:57:36.044
[Riv (DM)]: Roll a d6 and add two to it.

01:57:39.052 --> 01:57:40.853
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Two plus two is four!

01:57:39.285 --> 01:57:42.586
[Riv (DM)]: So we got a four.

01:57:40.873 --> 01:57:55.600
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Good play.

01:57:44.187 --> 01:57:47.728
[Riv (DM)]: Do you have the grimoire in your inventory at the moment? Who's got the grimoire?

01:57:50.380 --> 01:57:51.461
[Riv (DM)]: It's in the bag of holding, right?

01:57:57.365 --> 01:57:59.927
[Riv (DM)]: Who last had it?

01:57:58.226 --> 01:57:58.718
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yes it is.

01:58:00.727 --> 01:58:04.951
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool. We'll just dump that onto Dondar and remove it from the bag.

01:58:09.093 --> 01:58:09.394
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

01:58:13.837 --> 01:58:15.758
[Riv (DM)]: A spell is revealed to you from the book.

01:58:17.159 --> 01:58:17.980
[Riv (DM)]: A fourth level spell.

01:58:18.984 --> 01:58:20.525
[Riv (DM)]: I will add it in now.

01:58:24.967 --> 01:58:26.767
[Riv (DM)]: I'll just add it directly into the.

01:58:29.869 --> 01:58:31.029
[Riv (DM)]: Into the grimoire, there you go.

01:58:34.291 --> 01:58:46.676
[Riv (DM)]: So now you can treat this as any spell book, you can spend time to learn it if you wish to copy that spell out. Sorry, what I mean is copy it into your own spell book.

01:58:54.380 --> 01:59:22.548
[Riv (DM)]: very good all right um but yeah you you found that very mentally taxing and um the thought of doing that again today kind of makes you almost want to throw up so um yeah you you close the book and the fingers around the edges kind of like clasp together and you almost want to stop looking at it so you throw it into your bag but there you go you have uncovered one of the spells within

01:59:06.864 --> 01:59:09.166
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, this has foreboding written all over it.

01:59:23.780 --> 01:59:24.120
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Roger.

01:59:25.248 --> 01:59:43.915
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, sweet. Kai, did you remember what you were going to say?

01:59:27.508 --> 01:59:36.273
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yes, I did. I was gonna ask, should we tell someone where we're going? Like, since we're gonna depart now.

01:59:37.294 --> 01:59:38.614
[Lacer (Kai)]: Do they know that we're leaving?

01:59:43.935 --> 01:59:46.856
[Riv (DM)]: I mean, if that's a question for me, then yes, I would say that.

01:59:46.179 --> 01:59:48.700
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh no, it was a question to the party. Like...

01:59:56.006 --> 01:59:57.939
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Aw, look, I mean, what's the worse that happens?

02:00:00.041 --> 02:00:01.076
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess we die.

02:00:01.524 --> 02:00:02.304
[JC (Ryo)]: They try and stop me.

02:00:05.548 --> 02:00:35.016
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Do we uh well do we have to think about anything like succession or you know not that there's anyone in our party with royal blood but if there were you know that vault's never going to get opened again uh do we need to it's true with the world ending and everything do we want to

02:00:25.978 --> 02:00:29.320
[JC (Ryo)]: None of them here even thought they could have opened the vault for the last 68 years.

02:00:37.536 --> 02:00:38.677
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Make some kind of plan.

02:00:41.118 --> 02:00:42.218
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You could marry someone.

02:00:48.160 --> 02:00:49.461
[JC (Ryo)]: And what would that accomplish?

02:00:51.662 --> 02:00:53.443
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: More blood, potentially.

02:00:56.866 --> 02:00:59.168
[JC (Ryo)]: Just marrying someone doesn't share the blood.

02:00:57.045 --> 02:00:57.925
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: More royal blood.

02:01:01.066 --> 02:01:04.308
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, you gotta, you know, follow through.

02:01:06.853 --> 02:01:08.514
[JC (Ryo)]: How long do you want to hang around here for?

02:01:09.200 --> 02:01:09.560
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: One night.

02:01:11.481 --> 02:01:17.642
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Just to get the, get the business done, get the ball rolling on, uh, potential elven ears.

02:01:16.619 --> 02:01:17.480
[JC (Ryo)]: How young is the dwarf?

02:01:21.763 --> 02:01:22.384
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: How, what, sorry?

02:01:22.956 --> 02:01:28.099
[JC (Ryo)]: How young of a dwarf are you? You should know it doesn't always... it takes more than one go.

02:01:30.526 --> 02:01:33.687
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay.

02:01:33.303 --> 02:01:34.524
[JC (Ryo)]: Things don't just happen.

02:01:33.747 --> 02:01:44.676
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Never, never in the racy dwarven poetry does it Not happened the first time, but all right. It's just an idea, thinking about your future.

02:01:45.904 --> 02:01:47.024
[Riv (DM)]: Are we making that canon?

02:01:48.378 --> 02:01:48.758
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yes.

02:01:48.625 --> 02:01:49.965
[Riv (DM)]: Dwarves can't fail.

02:01:48.685 --> 02:01:49.886
[Lacer (Kai)]: That's what I was thinking.

02:01:49.985 --> 02:01:52.906
[Riv (DM)]: Holy shit. I need to think about that.

02:01:52.761 --> 02:01:55.063
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: That's why there's so many of us.

02:01:54.287 --> 02:01:57.648
[Riv (DM)]: Maybe that's why they're so populous. They keep springing out of holes in the ground.

02:01:56.344 --> 02:01:56.664
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah.

02:01:57.668 --> 02:02:00.108
[Riv (DM)]: Holy shit.

02:02:00.668 --> 02:02:02.595
[JC (Ryo)]: Not only are they dwarves, they're also rabbits.

02:02:00.768 --> 02:02:06.710
[Riv (DM)]: Can't believe we're... Oh, jeez. Can't believe we're having this conversation right now.

02:02:05.668 --> 02:02:12.174
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Something, something, dwarves never fail, doesn't even require two dwarves, holes in the rock or something like that.

02:02:12.532 --> 02:02:12.852
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:02:13.403 --> 02:02:14.944
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: There are no dwarf women.

02:02:16.480 --> 02:02:19.326
[Riv (DM)]: I'd like to imagine Donda is just like genuinely confused.

02:02:16.758 --> 02:02:18.300
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Craig, forget that please.

02:02:20.509 --> 02:02:22.373
[Riv (DM)]: What do you mean you can't just have a baby instantly?

02:02:25.560 --> 02:02:26.021
[Riv (DM)]: Oh man.

02:02:26.855 --> 02:02:27.896
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait, is that what you meant?

02:02:29.277 --> 02:02:32.100
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Have a baby instantly. I meant conceive a baby.

02:02:35.092 --> 02:02:39.915
[JC (Ryo)]: I won't know it's there until ages. It's not like you find out the next morning. Oh, yeah, it worked.

02:02:44.156 --> 02:02:44.516
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: All right.

02:02:48.940 --> 02:02:50.681
[JC (Ryo)]: I'll look at Berberis. You need anything else, mate?

02:02:53.353 --> 02:02:54.281
[Vael (Berberis)]: Nope, I'm set to go.

02:02:56.164 --> 02:02:56.524
[JC (Ryo)]: All right.

02:02:57.925 --> 02:02:58.365
[JC (Ryo)]: To hell.

02:03:00.300 --> 02:03:02.602
[Riv (DM)]: Too hell. Cool. So the...

02:03:03.027 --> 02:03:04.168
[JC (Ryo)]: I open up to the Grimforge.

02:03:06.686 --> 02:03:07.646
[Riv (DM)]: Sorry, to where?

02:03:07.701 --> 02:03:15.205
[Lacer (Kai)]: He's thinking Glimmerforge.

02:03:08.556 --> 02:03:09.717
[JC (Ryo)]: I'm hoping I got the name right.

02:03:08.838 --> 02:03:10.380
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: The Grim Forge.

02:03:10.558 --> 02:03:11.459
[JC (Ryo)]: I think I got the name wrong.

02:03:12.471 --> 02:03:13.391
[Riv (DM)]: The Grimforge?

02:03:14.224 --> 02:03:14.745
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Glimmerforce.

02:03:15.285 --> 02:03:30.793
[Lacer (Kai)]: He wants to go to...

02:03:15.373 --> 02:03:16.594
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, Glimmerforge.

02:03:15.464 --> 02:03:16.746
[JC (Ryo)]: Glimmerforge, that's it.

02:03:17.844 --> 02:03:22.046
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: My god, is that like a parallel universe of Dwarven City?

02:03:17.956 --> 02:03:18.236
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:03:21.351 --> 02:03:21.572
[JC (Ryo)]: Yep.

02:03:22.266 --> 02:03:23.386
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: The Grimforge.

02:03:23.794 --> 02:03:25.376
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, open to the glimmerforge.

02:03:26.943 --> 02:03:27.504
[Riv (DM)]: Like, actually?

02:03:27.479 --> 02:03:28.580
[JC (Ryo)]: Look, ah look, hell.

02:03:27.508 --> 02:03:30.510
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Hey.

02:03:30.124 --> 02:03:36.329
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, Jesus Christ.

02:03:32.394 --> 02:03:34.435
[Lacer (Kai)]: The Glimmerforge is located in hell.

02:03:36.172 --> 02:03:38.693
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I mean, there's volcanoes, you're not far wrong.

02:03:36.342 --> 02:03:38.504
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Just a moment of panic of Riv.

02:03:40.034 --> 02:03:44.676
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Like, it could certainly look like the hells if you get into the forges and the volcano cores.

02:03:45.332 --> 02:03:47.396
[JC (Ryo)]: Never been there, I don't think I could open a portal there.

02:03:48.519 --> 02:03:50.080
[Riv (DM)]: Oh no. You could make a dice roll.

02:03:51.641 --> 02:03:53.163
[Riv (DM)]: It would be a very, very low chance, but you could.

02:03:53.929 --> 02:03:55.152
[JC (Ryo)]: This is just JC joking.

02:03:56.165 --> 02:03:58.627
[Riv (DM)]: Hey, anything is possible.

02:03:59.232 --> 02:04:00.773
[Lacer (Kai)]: Making ribs to that bullet.

02:04:02.936 --> 02:04:03.976
[Riv (DM)]: Oh man, we could probably wing it.

02:04:03.120 --> 02:04:03.883
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: The Scylla Cows.

02:04:04.757 --> 02:04:22.962
[Riv (DM)]: We could wing it for sure. I've got some stuff prepped for the dwarves. Yeah, so just on the whole note of like, should we tell anyone? I would imagine that you've already kind of had the conversation with Illythia and Kaelin when Ryo spoke to him the other night about this.

02:04:23.712 --> 02:04:23.912
[Lacer (Kai)]: Okay.

02:04:24.342 --> 02:04:30.104
[Riv (DM)]: Yes, it is very, very concerning that the only person who can open the world gate

02:04:31.336 --> 02:04:36.099
[Riv (DM)]: is about to go to a different plane of existence, a particularly chaotic plane of existence.

02:04:38.261 --> 02:04:52.230
[Riv (DM)]: They just found their society was, or could potentially longer be cut off, right? They could go back and rejoin the material realm and help join the fight. All that's true.

02:04:53.691 --> 02:05:00.416
[Riv (DM)]: And so you could imagine that some of them might be keen to actually strongly recommend or potentially even force you not to go Rio.

02:05:02.088 --> 02:05:05.511
[Riv (DM)]: But I think Illythia would have made the astute observation.

02:05:07.873 --> 02:05:17.962
[Riv (DM)]: If he doesn't go, the rest of you aren't coming back, right? Because he's the only one who could potentially open a world gate that may or may not exist on the other side.

02:05:12.640 --> 02:05:12.800
[Lacer (Kai)]: Hmm.

02:05:17.982 --> 02:05:27.290
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:05:20.832 --> 02:05:26.478
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I had the idea that he would leave the ring here, but then, oh, he can't open it without it, so.

02:05:29.280 --> 02:05:36.287
[JC (Ryo)]: So I need to send that party through to the reinforcements for Darmouth.

02:05:34.427 --> 02:05:34.928
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: McDonald's?

02:05:37.651 --> 02:05:38.452
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, yeah.

02:05:38.687 --> 02:05:41.988
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's only been a day, literally. I don't think that...

02:05:41.152 --> 02:05:42.912
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I think they'll still be prepping for that.

02:05:42.924 --> 02:05:44.427
[Lacer (Kai)]: They're not going to be that ready that quick.

02:05:42.932 --> 02:05:43.053
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:05:43.233 --> 02:05:46.236
[JC (Ryo)]: All right.

02:05:44.467 --> 02:05:50.578
[Lacer (Kai)]: They're not like robots or something.

02:05:45.793 --> 02:05:49.514
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, yeah, yeah.

02:05:46.256 --> 02:05:50.620
[JC (Ryo)]: I only requested it, I didn't demand it, so they can turn around and say no to that.

02:05:49.534 --> 02:05:57.436
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, there were a couple of things to discuss.

02:05:51.059 --> 02:05:55.668
[Lacer (Kai)]: They need to have a meeting. I think that we're going to talk about it more or something, another confluence.

02:05:57.456 --> 02:06:13.182
[Riv (DM)]: They were going to discuss the strike force that Ryo recommended to Kaelin, and they were going to discuss potentially unsealing an operative who is within the Dwarven Kingdom at the moment. To help with Dondar's request.

02:06:11.424 --> 02:06:16.052
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, yes. That'd be nice.

02:06:17.445 --> 02:06:17.686
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:06:20.248 --> 02:06:21.088
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Okay, sweet.

02:06:22.089 --> 02:06:22.770
[Riv (DM)]: The...

02:06:26.092 --> 02:06:35.660
[Riv (DM)]: The space and... Is that answering?

02:06:26.252 --> 02:06:29.415
[JC (Ryo)]: So I guess is the world guarded or is there no one here?

02:06:32.078 --> 02:06:34.160
[JC (Ryo)]: Is the world gate guarded or is there no one here?

02:06:35.680 --> 02:06:39.443
[Riv (DM)]: So the entire heartstone itself is guarded, like the bridge of recollection.

02:06:41.028 --> 02:06:53.038
[Riv (DM)]: They won't let anyone cross there, but you're already across. And so you can walk on in and it's very sort of like, you know, dusty and clearly hasn't been looked after. But yeah, there is no one in there.

02:06:54.860 --> 02:06:55.981
[Riv (DM)]: Just that big chamber.

02:06:56.724 --> 02:07:04.450
[Lacer (Kai)]: As I'm walking, prestidigitation, prestidigitation, prestidigitation, and just patches of dust just disappear.

02:07:00.264 --> 02:07:05.849
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, just like cleaning it up as you go. Yeah, yeah.

02:07:05.491 --> 02:07:06.612
[Lacer (Kai)]: But it just looks spotty now.

02:07:07.690 --> 02:07:07.911
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:07:11.372 --> 02:07:17.417
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Okay. The space in front of you tears open as Ryo places the ring in the dice.

02:07:19.078 --> 02:07:33.350
[Riv (DM)]: You see a roiling hot heat, like sort of heat waves starting to come through. The air sort of shimmering. And beyond you see a dark red wasteland.

02:07:36.233 --> 02:07:36.993
[Riv (DM)]: And you step on through.

02:07:38.555 --> 02:07:57.273
[Riv (DM)]: Instantly you feel the temperature. Shoot from mild tending on cold to hot tending on very hot. Immediately you feel the sweat starting to run down the sides of your face. It's not, doesn't feel dangerous, but it's very hot and a little bit uncomfortable.

02:07:58.574 --> 02:08:16.228
[Riv (DM)]: You look up, you are under a blazing burning sky. A ruined waste stretches out in front of you. Massive crushed and broken siege engines just sort of litter the landscape. Remnants of a war that begun before you could even possibly imagine and still continues to this day.

02:08:17.289 --> 02:08:40.024
[Riv (DM)]: The said war battles above you. You see flying creatures and beasts just clashing in the skies. And across the plains everywhere you see little pockets of dust being kicked up in between skirmishes. You see entire armies on the horizon clashing. And just the constant hum of screams and shouts and clashing of swords.

02:08:42.153 --> 02:08:49.319
[Riv (DM)]: I'll get people to roll a perception check for me.

02:08:48.876 --> 02:08:49.595
[Lacer (Kai)]: Is this a group?

02:08:49.419 --> 02:09:00.369
[Riv (DM)]: Or survival, perception or survival, whatever you feel like doing.

02:08:55.747 --> 02:08:56.211
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Ich was?

02:08:56.960 --> 02:08:57.704
[JC (Ryo)]: Fuck you, Dice.

02:09:00.409 --> 02:09:02.371
[Riv (DM)]: Probably just two people, yeah.

02:09:00.559 --> 02:09:01.484
[JC (Ryo)]: Plus ten, roll one.

02:09:03.331 --> 02:09:04.412
[Riv (DM)]: All right, cool.

02:09:03.902 --> 02:09:04.353
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, Tupi.

02:09:04.472 --> 02:09:10.841
[Riv (DM)]: So Kai's made a perception check. Yeah, nice. And so, yeah, you look around the landscape, Kai.

02:09:13.201 --> 02:09:35.748
[Riv (DM)]: You take all this in. You feel that sort of that weight dropping in your stomach as the reality of where you have just landed hits you and you feel potentially a bit of distance from yourself and your god as that sort of terror starts to enclose in around you. Looking back over your shoulder, you almost

02:09:37.940 --> 02:10:05.383
[Riv (DM)]: Anxiously look around and you see the last glimpse of the Worldgate room back in the Heartstone disappear as the tear in space zips itself back up. And you are alone and cut off from the burning wastes of Avernus. On a 26, a huge perception check. So you're standing up on sort of like a ridge line, looking down out across the wastes. I mean, far, far off to the distance.

02:10:07.484 --> 02:10:24.372
[Riv (DM)]: Over to, probably to the right, whatever direction you're in, you're completely disoriented. Do see a line of mountains. And at the base of the mountains, you see that sort of telltale sort of cut in the landscape that you would assume to be a river.

02:10:29.015 --> 02:10:29.235
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:10:30.395 --> 02:10:31.716
[Riv (DM)]: And you know what? I actually have...

02:10:35.180 --> 02:10:45.543
[Riv (DM)]: I have something lined up for you guys so I can drag you all over here for a bit of fun and set the scene a bit. And you guys probably can't see anything right now because I have got shadows on.

02:10:42.708 --> 02:10:43.495
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yep, no visibility.

02:10:45.723 --> 02:10:50.064
[Riv (DM)]: There we go. All right.

02:10:49.804 --> 02:10:56.007
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can I just ask a little question about how the Worldgate thing works?

02:10:52.185 --> 02:10:52.405
[Riv (DM)]: Yes.

02:10:57.227 --> 02:10:57.427
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:10:57.608 --> 02:11:02.871
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: He puts his ring in the dais, and then he takes it out again, and the portal stays open.

02:10:59.907 --> 02:11:00.067
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:11:04.551 --> 02:11:05.732
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: How long does it stay open for?

02:11:08.614 --> 02:11:10.178
[Riv (DM)]: I mean you probably haven't tested it so far.

02:11:11.261 --> 02:11:16.776
[Riv (DM)]: Generally so far you've seen that you step through and after a few seconds it closes up behind you.

02:11:24.108 --> 02:11:29.491
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: So if we had left it in the dais, maybe it would have stayed open permanently?

02:11:30.304 --> 02:11:32.146
[Lacer (Kai)]: That sounds like a terrible idea, Dondar.

02:11:35.055 --> 02:11:37.276
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I mean, a smart man would think that.

02:11:38.948 --> 02:11:40.731
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Leaving a portal to the hells open.

02:11:41.633 --> 02:11:42.895
[Lacer (Kai)]: To the fey realm.

02:11:42.514 --> 02:11:43.996
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Wonderful, wonderful idea.

02:11:44.496 --> 02:11:53.924
[Lacer (Kai)]: This is coming from a wise man. Not necessarily smart, but my wisdom tells me that that'd be very, very bad.

02:11:54.484 --> 02:12:08.412
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I'm just curious. Nice.

02:11:58.148 --> 02:12:00.129
[Lacer (Kai)]: How about we try that if we can get back?

02:12:04.808 --> 02:12:09.336
[JC (Ryo)]: I just look at the pair of them and start walking toward, and uh, I'm assuming Kai's pointed out the river.

02:12:10.294 --> 02:12:22.040
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yes, I point out the river. As I'm perceiving around me, do I notice any world gates similar to the one we just walked through around us? Or it's just desolate and war-torn?

02:12:23.070 --> 02:12:24.326
[Riv (DM)]: Nope, you don't see anything like that.

02:12:25.376 --> 02:12:27.257
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait, what?

02:12:29.818 --> 02:12:30.678
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I'm very confused.

02:12:32.360 --> 02:12:39.264
[Lacer (Kai)]: He's portaled us to the hells in a random location in the hells in the first layer, Dondar.

02:12:41.803 --> 02:12:42.104
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay.

02:12:43.604 --> 02:12:51.868
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: So it isn't like the teleportation stones that I have that go to a stone that just goes anywhere.

02:12:48.488 --> 02:12:55.252
[Lacer (Kai)]: No. It goes way...

02:12:53.356 --> 02:12:58.580
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm, I see what you mean. Yeah, so you're expecting like another gate on the other side to pass through.

02:12:58.613 --> 02:12:58.878
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah.

02:12:59.921 --> 02:13:07.187
[Riv (DM)]: So it does sometimes work like that. So if you want to sort of guarantee a location, you can connect it directly to another gate.

02:13:08.327 --> 02:13:45.636
[Riv (DM)]: So for example, you could go from Sumara to Ithilmara on the material realm and you have 100% chance of reconnecting to the gate. But if you want to choose any other place, you can. And then it works similarly to Planeshift where you just have a bit of a dice roll chance to see how close you are to where you wanted to go. With the Hells, the Hells is one that is slightly special. You can't exactly Planeshift to any particular location. Everyone comes through Avernus and then you have to fight your way down the different layers. Unless you're a super important or powerful entity who knows secrets around how you could skip

02:13:45.960 --> 02:13:51.965
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I see. That makes sense.

02:13:48.448 --> 02:13:53.196
[Riv (DM)]: So yeah, and that's why I didn't ask Ryo to roll this time because it's irrelevant. You always end up in Avernus.

02:13:54.207 --> 02:13:54.587
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Gotcha.

02:13:58.860 --> 02:14:19.057
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm gonna say this is very bad. We shouldn't be here. I see, I feel the sweat dripping off my face and I see it land on the ground.

02:14:07.819 --> 02:14:09.100
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Get your tan ready, boys.

02:14:19.177 --> 02:14:21.159
[Lacer (Kai)]: How fast does it evaporate off the ground?

02:14:23.424 --> 02:14:24.365
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Does it even hit the ground?

02:14:23.596 --> 02:14:29.960
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, um... Yeah, I mean, it's not that hot.

02:14:24.982 --> 02:14:27.244
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, does it evaporate as soon as it like drips?

02:14:30.080 --> 02:14:35.664
[Riv (DM)]: It's not like... Yeah. I mean, it probably sits on the ground for a few seconds, but it dries up pretty quick.

02:14:36.648 --> 02:14:44.512
[Lacer (Kai)]: Alright, I guess we better stay... Is it good to stay wet, Berberis, in this type of hell environment?

02:14:47.105 --> 02:14:47.610
[Vael (Berberis)]: Avernus.

02:14:48.834 --> 02:15:07.999
