[Lacer (Kai)]: Alright, I'm gonna try. And as I point out to the team, that there's a river ahead of us at the base of these mountains. You guys told me... While we were researching in the Nemorium, that to get to the second layer, we need to take a boat down the river Styx.

02:15:09.519 --> 02:15:19.763
[Lacer (Kai)]: I see a river ahead of us, but as I do that, as we're walking, I'm gonna pull out the decanter of endless water and splash everyone a bit.

02:15:20.792 --> 02:15:24.935
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

02:15:22.403 --> 02:15:23.204
[Lacer (Kai)]: To keep us cool.

02:15:25.015 --> 02:15:29.157
[Riv (DM)]: I was just like flicking the water and splashing all of you. How do you guys react?

02:15:34.508 --> 02:15:35.915
[JC (Ryo)]: Just look at him like, what the fuck?

02:15:36.481 --> 02:15:36.682
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:15:38.752 --> 02:15:39.413
[Lacer (Kai)]: Hey, it's hot.

02:15:40.413 --> 02:15:43.516
[Lacer (Kai)]: If you don't want it, just tell me if you don't want it and I'll just keep it to myself.

02:15:40.639 --> 02:15:40.991
[Vael (Berberis)]: If y'all

02:15:40.684 --> 02:15:46.387
[Riv (DM)]: The... Yeah, so the water will be drying pretty damn quickly.

02:15:49.068 --> 02:15:51.769
[Riv (DM)]: Do you mean to just like kind of continually splash it just to...

02:15:52.283 --> 02:15:58.268
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, with the, with the shape water, like, it'd be like, yeah, making it rain a bit.

02:15:52.727 --> 02:15:59.432
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: My... My... My... My beers will retain the moisture.

02:15:53.149 --> 02:15:56.670
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, so you're just kind of making it rain a little bit.

02:15:59.991 --> 02:16:01.592
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. All right, nice.

02:16:00.209 --> 02:16:05.714
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, at least, do we feel, do we feel any, um, breeze? So we get this cooling effect as we're walking?

02:16:07.974 --> 02:16:10.354
[Riv (DM)]: There's probably a little bit of breeze, yeah.

02:16:08.196 --> 02:16:08.516
[Lacer (Kai)]: Or does it,

02:16:10.714 --> 02:16:15.956
[Riv (DM)]: Every now and then you'll get like pockets of gusts and stuff like that. Like the clouds over here are definitely roiling, so there's a breeze.

02:16:17.152 --> 02:16:24.597
[Riv (DM)]: Coming. All right, nice. So we've got some comfort along the way. Kai is rehydrating us.

02:16:22.304 --> 02:16:23.569
[Lacer (Kai)]: A little bit, a tiny bit.

02:16:25.037 --> 02:16:28.179
[Riv (DM)]: Sweet. So do we just start heading in the direction of the river then?

02:16:31.302 --> 02:16:40.047
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. So the first little obstacle that you're going to have to figure out is the ridge that you're currently standing on. You're going to have to drop off it.

02:16:42.929 --> 02:16:57.683
[Riv (DM)]: Depending on how you want to go. It looks like you can probably do a bit of a search. And find a way down that looks alright. And we can get some saving throws from people. Unless you have any other way that you would like to get down.

02:16:58.060 --> 02:16:59.121
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, how far down is it?

02:17:00.906 --> 02:17:02.668
[Riv (DM)]: It's probably a good like couple of hundred.

02:17:03.929 --> 02:17:06.992
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, sort of a couple of hundred feet scramble.

02:17:11.047 --> 02:17:13.328
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, we're in no rush. Pull out the rope of climbing.

02:17:15.890 --> 02:17:18.251
[JC (Ryo)]: And just tell it to start going down and I'll hold onto it.

02:17:19.332 --> 02:17:19.992
[JC (Ryo)]: Come on, grab on.

02:17:19.592 --> 02:17:19.792
[Riv (DM)]: Alright.

02:17:20.012 --> 02:17:27.016
[JC (Ryo)]: I don't need to tie it to anything.

02:17:21.053 --> 02:17:25.275
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. So you just like tie it to a rock or something like that and allow it to dangle off?

02:17:29.913 --> 02:17:30.974
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: It's a flying rope.

02:17:31.584 --> 02:17:33.586
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, you do need to, you need to anchor.

02:17:31.955 --> 02:17:32.556
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Like a lift.

02:17:33.559 --> 02:17:34.620
[Riv (DM)]: It needs to anchor on.

02:17:33.826 --> 02:17:36.049
[Lacer (Kai)]: We, we talked, we talked about this Ryo.

02:17:35.199 --> 02:17:36.080
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: It's anchored to him.

02:17:36.532 --> 02:17:43.558
[JC (Ryo)]: If I hold one end of the rope and use an action to speak the command, the rope animates. I can command one end to move to a destination of my choice.

02:17:45.059 --> 02:17:45.920
[Lacer (Kai)]: But needs to anchor.

02:17:46.560 --> 02:17:47.201
[JC (Ryo)]: No, it doesn't.

02:17:49.162 --> 02:17:49.676
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Anchor to him.

02:17:50.884 --> 02:17:51.944
[JC (Ryo)]: It doesn't. Read it.

02:17:52.975 --> 02:18:04.064
[Riv (DM)]: So you can control it and it will it will move somewhere it will move somewhere but like you can't you it can't just like float vertically in the air and then you climb up it like it needs to actually wrap around some something

02:17:53.806 --> 02:17:55.948
[JC (Ryo)]: It just takes a long time.

02:18:04.595 --> 02:18:06.076
[JC (Ryo)]: But it doesn't say it has to wrap around.

02:18:06.208 --> 02:18:12.796
[Lacer (Kai)]: It says you can also tell the rope to fasten itself securely to an object or to unfasten itself. So right now it's...

02:18:12.324 --> 02:18:15.348
[JC (Ryo)]: Also, it doesn't say I have to.

02:18:15.992 --> 02:18:23.034
[Riv (DM)]: That is 100% how it works. So we're just going to, we're going to roll it that way. It needs to sort of fasten itself around something.

02:18:21.260 --> 02:18:21.952
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Avernus.

02:18:21.336 --> 02:18:21.756
[JC (Ryo)]: All right.

02:18:23.438 --> 02:18:30.988
[JC (Ryo)]: I was going to say I'd tell it to not. I'll climb down the 50 feet, tell the other end to go down to the other end, and then climb down 50 feet, and then tell it to go down another 50 feet.

02:18:30.656 --> 02:18:32.917
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, no, I mean, totally, that totally works.

02:18:31.657 --> 02:18:32.455
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Inderferent, yep.

02:18:33.217 --> 02:18:33.397
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:18:33.332 --> 02:18:36.134
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, I thought you meant it was just gonna magically stay in the air, like...

02:18:33.717 --> 02:18:43.380
[Riv (DM)]: Like there's, there's, yeah, there's no, yeah.

02:18:36.968 --> 02:18:42.391
[JC (Ryo)]: That's how I read it. It stays in the air, animated, and it can hold up to 3,000 pounds. Alright, alright.

02:18:41.433 --> 02:18:44.371
[Vael (Berberis)]: It's not a flying rope. It's still a rope.

02:18:44.776 --> 02:18:52.299
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, it's still a rope. You gotta tie it to something once it starts. I've been waiting.

02:18:47.933 --> 02:19:00.980
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, that moves at 10 feet a turn, so it doesn't move that fast, 10 feet every six seconds.

02:18:48.524 --> 02:18:51.665
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: We're hand waving the flying, but it is essentially a flying word.

02:18:52.319 --> 02:18:55.120
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, literally otherwise you could use it like a flying broomstick.

02:19:02.163 --> 02:19:09.566
[Riv (DM)]: Well, I'm just gonna make a little ruling. The rope of climbing is not a broomstick. I hope we're all good with that.

02:19:08.973 --> 02:19:12.215
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait, are they flying broomsticks in this game? In this world?

02:19:11.947 --> 02:19:13.528
[Riv (DM)]: There are flying broomsticks in this game, yeah.

02:19:13.395 --> 02:19:15.236
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Holy fuck. I want one.

02:19:14.740 --> 02:19:16.241
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Well, hey.

02:19:16.016 --> 02:19:16.365
[Lacer (Kai)]: Scott.

02:19:16.684 --> 02:19:18.365
[JC (Ryo)]: Flying broomsticks and magic wands.

02:19:18.663 --> 02:19:35.856
[Riv (DM)]: If only you knew a prodigy magic item crafter. If only, right? All right, sweet. Fasten it around a rock. Climb down a section. We'll give everyone just a group saving throw of strength with advantage because the rope is knotted.

02:19:49.536 --> 02:19:49.936
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Are we dead?

02:19:51.468 --> 02:19:52.329
[Lacer (Kai)]: Who rolled the 20?

02:19:52.340 --> 02:19:55.642
[Riv (DM)]: I think we're missing just one person.

02:19:54.671 --> 02:19:55.632
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, I rolled the 20.

02:19:56.043 --> 02:19:56.803
[Riv (DM)]: We're missing a Dondar?

02:19:58.284 --> 02:19:59.466
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait, you said we had a group.

02:19:58.675 --> 02:19:58.955
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:20:00.185 --> 02:20:01.226
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so everyone rolls.

02:20:00.216 --> 02:20:03.159
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, everyone still rolls. Roll with advantage.

02:20:05.491 --> 02:20:07.573
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh god, Kiwi's going retarded again.

02:20:08.171 --> 02:20:09.692
[Riv (DM)]: Just don't roll an extra one and you're fine.

02:20:11.744 --> 02:20:12.865
[JC (Ryo)]: saving throws, strength.

02:20:11.878 --> 02:20:14.280
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Saving... Oh, strength, gotcha.

02:20:16.091 --> 02:20:16.432
[Lacer (Kai)]: Nat 1.

02:20:16.847 --> 02:20:18.048
[JC (Ryo)]: Advantage, so roll again.

02:20:18.980 --> 02:20:20.522
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: A nat 18. Wait, what?

02:20:21.310 --> 02:20:22.291
[JC (Ryo)]: Roll it a second time.

02:20:25.472 --> 02:20:27.975
[Lacer (Kai)]: Now it's a triple.

02:20:25.767 --> 02:20:32.334
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Almost a 1. Oh, let's see.

02:20:28.544 --> 02:20:30.685
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so you rolled it with advantage the second time.

02:20:35.062 --> 02:20:35.503
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's fine.

02:20:35.419 --> 02:20:35.839
[JC (Ryo)]: You sure?

02:20:35.523 --> 02:20:37.805
[Lacer (Kai)]: It would have been a 7 and 18.

02:20:36.599 --> 02:20:37.300
[JC (Ryo)]: It's not my fault.

02:20:38.546 --> 02:20:39.307
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's fine.

02:20:39.690 --> 02:20:40.291
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

02:20:40.829 --> 02:20:42.791
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh my god, you can stop, dude.

02:20:42.232 --> 02:20:48.655
[Riv (DM)]: We're all good. So we'll take the 18 and the 11. So we get an 18. Perfecto, no problem at all.

02:20:50.837 --> 02:21:03.513
[Riv (DM)]: The rope allows you to truss down nice and easily. Man, I should... I should figure out a way to take that rope away from you guys, because pretty much every single terrain challenge is just made trivial, isn't it?

02:21:03.988 --> 02:21:07.334
[JC (Ryo)]: I don't know why you gave it to us at like the start of the campaign almost.

02:21:06.656 --> 02:21:12.160
[Riv (DM)]: Well, I mean, it's a cool item, but... Yeah, it's a...

02:21:07.368 --> 02:21:09.969
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: We could weave it into a magic carpet.

02:21:12.861 --> 02:21:14.742
[Riv (DM)]: I think it was the first magic item I gave you guys.

02:21:15.609 --> 02:21:16.852
[JC (Ryo)]: You gave it to us very early.

02:21:15.783 --> 02:21:16.184
[Riv (DM)]: Very cool.

02:21:17.265 --> 02:21:17.585
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:21:18.412 --> 02:21:21.013
[Vael (Berberis)]: Because we had to hang off a bridge underneath it.

02:21:18.786 --> 02:21:19.006
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:21:20.767 --> 02:21:26.012
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I was actually thinking ahead a bit that it might be handy for Skyreach shenanigans, but...

02:21:21.533 --> 02:21:40.740
[Vael (Berberis)]: But we couldn't then.

02:21:26.932 --> 02:21:27.092
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:21:27.881 --> 02:21:28.502
[Lacer (Kai)]: Question is...

02:21:27.972 --> 02:21:28.353
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful.

02:21:28.059 --> 02:21:30.540
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: We had someone who could spider climb though as well.

02:21:33.900 --> 02:21:35.462
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, you could just walk straight down Erundor.

02:21:36.210 --> 02:21:38.052
[Lacer (Kai)]: He can literally run down the wall.

02:21:39.878 --> 02:21:40.938
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. All right.

02:21:41.999 --> 02:21:51.323
[Riv (DM)]: So, um, you make it down, down to the bottom, the vast plains of the wasteland. Each of you stretches.

02:21:52.543 --> 02:21:54.524
[Riv (DM)]: You start traveling in the direction that you saw the river.

02:21:56.736 --> 02:22:03.919
[Riv (DM)]: Passing by these massive like giant trebuchets, the crate of which at the end of the arm is like the size of a house.

02:22:05.280 --> 02:22:08.521
[Riv (DM)]: Wheels absolutely just towering above you.

02:22:09.742 --> 02:22:11.503
[Riv (DM)]: Some of them still smoldering with embers.

02:22:13.564 --> 02:22:16.085
[Riv (DM)]: Ahead of you, you see like a gorge.

02:22:17.506 --> 02:22:22.628
[Riv (DM)]: Occasionally you might walk past little pools of lava, just like bubbling a little bit.

02:22:24.564 --> 02:22:29.668
[Riv (DM)]: At one point, you find yourselves coming up to one of these gorges. It sort of rises on either side.

02:22:31.590 --> 02:22:36.494
[Riv (DM)]: Fumes sort of starting to blow up ahead of you from some of these pools.

02:22:37.635 --> 02:22:43.420
[Riv (DM)]: And you hear footsteps heading your direction. Heavy, weighty footsteps.

02:22:46.812 --> 02:22:46.853
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I?

02:22:49.365 --> 02:22:49.906
[Riv (DM)]: Getting closer.

02:22:50.720 --> 02:23:05.364
[JC (Ryo)]: I'll throw a pass without a choice on the party as well.

02:22:51.432 --> 02:22:52.373
[Lacer (Kai)]: Is there anything around?

02:22:51.647 --> 02:22:52.208
[Riv (DM)]: Through the fog.

02:22:56.193 --> 02:22:56.873
[Riv (DM)]: Not much.

02:22:58.036 --> 02:23:02.800
[Lacer (Kai)]: There's no... Big rocks is better than nothing.

02:22:58.094 --> 02:23:00.316
[Riv (DM)]: There's a pool of lava. There's a couple of big rocks.

02:23:03.298 --> 02:23:03.479
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:23:06.481 --> 02:23:11.685
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. So you see a figure starting to burst through the fog ahead of you.

02:23:14.508 --> 02:23:14.988
[Riv (DM)]: Tall.

02:23:16.950 --> 02:23:23.135
[Riv (DM)]: Armoured. It's got a full plated helm entirely covering the face.

02:23:24.255 --> 02:23:34.088
[Riv (DM)]: Wings. Of fire poking out beyond and these blazing horns around coming through the front of the plated faceplate as well.

02:23:35.829 --> 02:23:46.134
[Riv (DM)]: You can see a big like coiled whip. It's almost sort of glowing at their waist and they are just running straight for you. However, you see they are limping slightly.

02:23:47.395 --> 02:23:55.880
[Riv (DM)]: Every other step has got a slight give And one of the wings seems to be almost scarred and broken. It's hanging off at an awkward angle.

02:23:57.141 --> 02:24:02.226
[Riv (DM)]: You go to make your way over to the rock to hide. I will roll.

02:24:05.228 --> 02:24:20.893
[Riv (DM)]: Just as you're heading to dip behind the rocks, you see the face as it's sort of just like, just crushing through the stone, patches towards you. See the head turn slightly, almost like a confused look. And a voice comes out.

02:24:22.034 --> 02:24:28.079
[Riv (DM)]: Mortals! Stand with me, and I will see you to the river! On my name and my wing, I swear it!

02:24:29.520 --> 02:24:42.209
[Riv (DM)]: And as you hear this, it keeps on running, you hear more footsteps, like they might be getting pursued. Alright.

02:24:37.708 --> 02:24:39.510
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Fun. What's your name?

02:24:43.971 --> 02:24:45.632
[Riv (DM)]: Do you continue to... what do you do?

02:24:54.752 --> 02:24:55.253
[JC (Ryo)]: We fight?

02:24:54.824 --> 02:24:55.444
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm pretty sure.

02:24:55.016 --> 02:24:55.418
[Vael (Berberis)]: Well...

02:24:57.163 --> 02:24:57.986
[Vael (Berberis)]: I just looked at the pony.

02:24:59.170 --> 02:25:00.755
[Vael (Berberis)]: Local guides are never a bad idea.

02:25:01.970 --> 02:25:03.412
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, friend.

02:25:01.985 --> 02:25:02.260
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: True.

02:25:02.747 --> 02:25:03.208
[JC (Ryo)]: We fight.

02:25:05.836 --> 02:25:06.076
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

02:25:06.490 --> 02:25:08.891
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm guessing Kiwi asks him, what's your name?

02:25:10.320 --> 02:25:11.280
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, maybe not.

02:25:10.540 --> 02:25:11.240
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, I did!

02:25:12.221 --> 02:25:13.522
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, probably no time for that.

02:25:13.221 --> 02:25:14.842
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: He said by his name!

02:25:13.562 --> 02:25:16.164
[Riv (DM)]: They are upon you.

02:25:18.026 --> 02:25:19.307
[Riv (DM)]: We'll get you all to roll initiative, eh?

02:25:19.816 --> 02:25:20.096
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm gonna

02:25:19.867 --> 02:25:20.848
[Riv (DM)]: The comment tracker is up.

02:25:25.612 --> 02:25:25.872
[Lacer (Kai)]: Sure.

02:25:30.668 --> 02:25:31.909
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Pretty landscape.

02:25:38.371 --> 02:25:39.612
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Wait, we're rolling with advantage?

02:25:40.332 --> 02:25:44.740
[JC (Ryo)]: I was going to say, why do you get an advantage, guy?

02:25:40.699 --> 02:25:42.359
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, I do.

02:25:41.079 --> 02:25:41.344
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I do.

02:25:42.379 --> 02:25:52.084
[Lacer (Kai)]: I have a special ability called, it's part of my class.

02:25:53.095 --> 02:25:53.596
[JC (Ryo)]: Okay, cool.

02:25:54.860 --> 02:26:00.768
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's called Vigilant Blessing. Allows me to give myself advantage. Or someone else, but I only gave it to myself.

02:26:02.530 --> 02:26:04.312
[Lacer (Kai)]: Because it only works for one person at a time.

02:26:07.977 --> 02:26:09.319
[Lacer (Kai)]: But I rolled like shit.

02:26:12.383 --> 02:26:13.044
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh no.

02:26:18.376 --> 02:26:23.699
[Riv (DM)]: Ignore all the dice, please. Okay, so you see this winged figure heading in your direction.

02:26:26.960 --> 02:26:37.125
[Riv (DM)]: Coming through the smoke behind, you see this figure chasing.

02:26:38.305 --> 02:26:39.125
[Riv (DM)]: These figures chasing.

02:26:41.226 --> 02:26:58.149
[Riv (DM)]: Chasing along, just like this large, shambling brute of a beast. Massive claws and then just a swarm of tiny little creatures almost like clamoring to claw their way over each other. First, you see drooling like that thirsty for blood.

02:27:00.332 --> 02:27:02.194
[Riv (DM)]: And actually top of the initiative, Ryo.

02:27:03.615 --> 02:27:04.276
[Riv (DM)]: That would be you.

02:27:15.184 --> 02:27:32.997
[JC (Ryo)]: I would like to use the feature from alert to initiative swap first and give it to Kai. I believe so, initiative swap.

02:27:22.892 --> 02:27:23.551
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Ha ha ha!

02:27:23.916 --> 02:27:24.396
[Lacer (Kai)]: What?

02:27:26.885 --> 02:27:34.509
[Riv (DM)]: Cool, so remind me, do you just swap turn, swap initiatives?

02:27:30.618 --> 02:27:32.418
[Lacer (Kai)]: I think we swap. Do we swap?

02:27:33.737 --> 02:27:37.680
[JC (Ryo)]: Immediately after you roll, you can swap your initiative with initiative one, a willing ally in the same combat.

02:27:38.091 --> 02:27:42.014
[Riv (DM)]: Cool, amazing, all right. So Kai, you're up first, I'll swap your initiative.

02:27:41.921 --> 02:27:42.861
[Lacer (Kai)]: Holy shit.

02:27:42.374 --> 02:27:45.176
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, wow. Yeah, you go right to the top.

02:27:46.823 --> 02:27:47.483
[Lacer (Kai)]: All right.

02:27:49.132 --> 02:27:49.871
[JC (Ryo)]: Show them who's boss.

02:27:51.084 --> 02:27:51.404
[Lacer (Kai)]: Sure.

02:27:53.720 --> 02:27:59.404
[Lacer (Kai)]: Now I need a... I was going to do something after people did stuff, but okay. 65 feet away.

02:28:00.784 --> 02:28:03.246
[Lacer (Kai)]: I will... Are they... How high are they flying in the sky?

02:28:04.993 --> 02:28:05.302
[Vael (Berberis)]: They're not.

02:28:05.908 --> 02:28:06.768
[Riv (DM)]: Who's flying in the sky?

02:28:07.688 --> 02:28:09.770
[Lacer (Kai)]: The pursuers. I thought they were flying.

02:28:10.790 --> 02:28:12.051
[Lacer (Kai)]: Are they on foot or are they flying?

02:28:11.110 --> 02:28:17.793
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, they're all, they're shambling. So you've got a big swarm of dudes at the back and then this big hulking horror chasing.

02:28:18.915 --> 02:28:20.776
[Lacer (Kai)]: Is it free action to yell out something?

02:28:21.515 --> 02:28:22.095
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, of course.

02:28:22.612 --> 02:28:29.536
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm gonna say a yellout to DemonGuy, who's coming towards us. Behind you, friendly or foe?

02:28:31.119 --> 02:28:33.820
[Riv (DM)]: They yell back, foe, demon, filth, slay them.

02:28:35.279 --> 02:28:36.660
[Lacer (Kai)]: Alright, cool.

02:28:37.800 --> 02:28:46.265
[Lacer (Kai)]: Sweet. I'm going to cast Moonspear Descent, which is my variant of Moonbeam.

02:28:49.546 --> 02:29:02.169
[Lacer (Kai)]: So as I weave my Hand signs. I cast it right here, so a glyph appears above them and a giant 30-foot spear slams down.

02:29:08.128 --> 02:29:12.530
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. So these two guys will be making their saving throw, yeah?

02:29:13.116 --> 02:29:13.456
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:29:13.310 --> 02:29:18.232
[Riv (DM)]: I wonder if I can do both at once.

02:29:20.293 --> 02:29:24.455
[Riv (DM)]: Let me just check one thing. Perfect. Oh, nope.

02:29:27.236 --> 02:29:27.496
[Riv (DM)]: Great.

02:29:30.978 --> 02:29:33.119
[Riv (DM)]: Next one on the left. Oh, fails.

02:29:35.360 --> 02:29:35.740
[Riv (DM)]: On the right.

02:29:37.816 --> 02:29:42.820
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, two failures. All right. Roll full damage. Which level did you cast this at? Level two?

02:29:43.168 --> 02:29:44.889
[Lacer (Kai)]: Level 2. I just wanted to see.

02:29:44.181 --> 02:29:44.421
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:29:44.929 --> 02:29:47.669
[Lacer (Kai)]: I didn't want to spam something too hard right in the beginning.

02:29:47.183 --> 02:29:47.344
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:29:50.186 --> 02:29:50.386
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:29:50.570 --> 02:29:52.911
[Lacer (Kai)]: So they take 8.

02:29:50.606 --> 02:29:50.886
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

02:29:51.987 --> 02:29:53.068
[Riv (DM)]: Eight damage to each.

02:29:53.651 --> 02:29:54.251
[Lacer (Kai)]: Great. Yeah.

02:29:54.769 --> 02:29:56.110
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Beautiful.

02:29:55.872 --> 02:30:10.256
[Lacer (Kai)]: And then I guess I'm going to bonus action, activate my starry form, and go into archer mode. And actually, no, I don't. I'm going to go into chalice mode.

02:30:12.608 --> 02:30:13.168
[Lacer (Kai)]: And that's about it.

02:30:15.629 --> 02:30:23.771
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess I'll move. I should move forward. I'll move between myself and the demon.

02:30:25.171 --> 02:30:38.955
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:30:26.636 --> 02:30:30.159
[Riv (DM)]: Here and up. Nice.

02:30:33.522 --> 02:30:35.964
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. And is that in your turn?

02:30:39.175 --> 02:30:50.563
[Lacer (Kai)]: If the demon, when it comes to this, when he runs by me, Can I hit him with a reaction spell since he's leaving my reach? If he does, like, leave my melee reach, yeah.

02:30:51.724 --> 02:30:54.186
[Lacer (Kai)]: And I'll attempt to heal him, I suppose, yeah.

02:30:56.476 --> 02:31:18.843
[Vael (Berberis)]: A reaction is not technically your turn.

02:30:57.349 --> 02:30:58.530
[Lacer (Kai)]: With Warcaster, yeah.

02:31:00.676 --> 02:31:06.638
[Riv (DM)]: So you, sorry, are you saying you want to hold an action or use a reaction?

02:31:06.997 --> 02:31:10.040
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, use a reaction to cast a spell.

02:31:11.319 --> 02:31:16.341
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I'm just wondering if that, how have we been ruling spells on other people's turns?

02:31:18.221 --> 02:31:23.823
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so I think it would be fine, right?

02:31:22.400 --> 02:31:24.124
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's not my turn anymore. Yeah.

02:31:23.988 --> 02:31:31.731
[Vael (Berberis)]: Same as like how you can like get multi-sneak attacking around because if you see a reaction of four.

02:31:27.044 --> 02:31:27.424
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:31:27.070 --> 02:31:28.934
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, that's what I was thinking.

02:31:29.312 --> 02:31:29.592
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:31:29.635 --> 02:31:31.920
[Lacer (Kai)]: But it depends on what you want to roll it, yeah.

02:31:34.073 --> 02:31:34.614
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, no, that's cool.

02:31:37.334 --> 02:31:41.135
[Riv (DM)]: Although I will just double check the Warcaster is damaging only.

02:31:42.114 --> 02:31:48.296
[Vael (Berberis)]: Apparently in 2024 they've changed the wording so it is just there is no longer need for hostile in the freezing.

02:31:47.257 --> 02:31:47.437
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:31:48.538 --> 02:31:54.979
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, it's just react to cast a spell on the creature. That's crazy. Very cool. All right.

02:31:56.480 --> 02:31:59.373
[Riv (DM)]: Sweet. Nice. All good. And was that your full movement?

02:32:00.096 --> 02:32:01.238
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yes, that's my four movements.

02:32:01.613 --> 02:32:02.053
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:32:03.234 --> 02:32:09.876
[Riv (DM)]: All right, sweet. So the giant luminous spear comes down and the creatures sort of screech out in horror.

02:32:11.236 --> 02:32:15.017
[Riv (DM)]: And then next up you see flapping in from the side.

02:32:13.555 --> 02:32:14.476
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh shit, I fucked up.

02:32:17.998 --> 02:32:20.979
[Riv (DM)]: See this giant bird creature. How far can it get?

02:32:23.020 --> 02:32:23.780
[Riv (DM)]: We can get to...

02:32:24.940 --> 02:32:25.774
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: There's a bird creature?

02:32:25.728 --> 02:32:27.122
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Did you not see that one Kai?

02:32:26.156 --> 02:32:27.056
[Riv (DM)]: There's a bird creature.

02:32:27.528 --> 02:32:34.314
[Lacer (Kai)]: I didn't see the one behind him that was ten feet behind him, this massive toad thing. I thought that was the environment.

02:32:33.096 --> 02:32:53.448
[Vael (Berberis)]: It kind of blends in with the terrain. I didn't see this one.

02:32:34.374 --> 02:32:35.515
[Lacer (Kai)]: I thought it was the environment!

02:32:36.800 --> 02:32:54.928
[Riv (DM)]: This guy, so this, this guy seems he sort of like swoops down from all the smoke and the dust in the sky. And, um, yeah, you probably would have had your eyes focused on the creatures in front of you. Um, he swoops down, comes in and he's going to cast second. Sorry.

02:32:58.432 --> 02:33:11.260
[JC (Ryo)]: He literally just put it on the map That one was just put on the map

02:33:00.372 --> 02:33:01.772
[Riv (DM)]: He was hidden.

02:33:03.632 --> 02:33:04.553
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: And since the beginning...

02:33:13.760 --> 02:33:15.242
[Lacer (Kai)]: It was flying.

02:33:18.768 --> 02:33:19.769
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I can't see a flag.

02:33:21.872 --> 02:33:23.714
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, that one! Sorry. Yes. Sorry.

02:33:25.295 --> 02:33:25.856
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Holy shit.

02:33:27.360 --> 02:33:28.840
[Riv (DM)]: I can't quite get all of you, I don't think.

02:33:32.739 --> 02:33:33.460
[Lacer (Kai)]: Unless it was.

02:33:32.776 --> 02:33:36.219
[Riv (DM)]: I'll have the three of you cast, sorry, do a constitution saving throw.

02:33:40.236 --> 02:33:41.029
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Those are gonna be fun.

02:33:40.262 --> 02:33:41.883
[Riv (DM)]: That sort of screeches out in horror.

02:33:48.989 --> 02:33:50.530
[Riv (DM)]: Ooh, okay.

02:33:57.976 --> 02:34:09.554
[Riv (DM)]: So Berberis and Dondar, you take 13 thunder damage. And you are stunned. And Erendor, you take six and you are not stunned.

02:34:13.840 --> 02:34:14.060
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:34:15.142 --> 02:34:19.248
[Riv (DM)]: All right. And that actually does bring us to Erendor.

02:34:24.848 --> 02:34:27.029
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Sorry, I'm just doing the next six.

02:34:34.972 --> 02:34:35.413
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: All right.

02:34:39.374 --> 02:34:40.455
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Those two are stunned.

02:34:43.976 --> 02:34:45.997
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Sorry, stunned effect.

02:34:48.098 --> 02:34:53.100
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Capacitated, seven throws affected. Attack balls against have advantage.

02:34:55.740 --> 02:34:56.020
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Okay.

02:34:57.801 --> 02:35:07.745
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Is there anything that I can do for them or nothing I can do other than just like we have to wait until the vrocks next turn?

02:35:06.681 --> 02:35:10.055
[Vael (Berberis)]: Stunbills, stunbills, stunbills, stunbills, fuck you.

02:35:10.486 --> 02:35:10.986
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yep, cool.

02:35:10.612 --> 02:35:12.306
[JC (Ryo)]: Yes, Dundas, they've lost it next turn.

02:35:13.127 --> 02:35:17.048
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Cool. How high off the ground is that one at the moment?

02:35:18.452 --> 02:35:25.399
[Riv (DM)]: It had to come quite close because it had to move very far so you would be able to reach it if you ran over to it. It's just flapping above the lava pool.

02:35:28.944 --> 02:35:30.565
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Alrighty.

02:35:32.866 --> 02:35:40.171
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Why not? Let's, um... Is it clear enough for me to stand there?

02:35:42.835 --> 02:35:43.696
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, absolutely.

02:35:43.613 --> 02:35:43.813
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yep.

02:35:43.876 --> 02:35:44.036
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

02:35:44.413 --> 02:35:49.276
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Wonderful. And I will be taking a swing at it with the Ember Blade.

02:35:50.396 --> 02:35:50.676
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:36:07.089 --> 02:36:07.669
[Riv (DM)]: Ooh, nice.

02:36:08.950 --> 02:36:16.436
[Riv (DM)]: That hits. You've got some pretty good mods on that thing now, eh?

02:36:13.036 --> 02:36:14.058
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Alrighty.

02:36:17.262 --> 02:36:19.766
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, it's got some pretty good mods on that.

02:36:21.468 --> 02:36:23.030
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: And I will...

02:36:27.837 --> 02:36:30.140
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Sorry, just checking the wording on that.

02:36:41.052 --> 02:36:45.694
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I will expend one charge on that as well with the damage.

02:36:50.797 --> 02:36:55.600
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: So hit it for that. And additionally then it is 1d6 radiant.

02:36:58.012 --> 02:37:08.137
[Riv (DM)]: Oh nice max damage.

02:37:09.197 --> 02:37:09.818
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, sweet.

02:37:15.360 --> 02:37:17.501
[Riv (DM)]: You pace through it, 20 damage on your first turn.

02:37:18.542 --> 02:37:27.600
[Riv (DM)]: Blast of like glowing energy spirals up the blade as you stab it in. You hear the creature streak out in pain. It's this unexpected radiant energy.

02:37:28.925 --> 02:37:29.508
[Riv (DM)]: Channels through it.

02:37:36.849 --> 02:37:42.333
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: And offhand attack with the dagger.

02:37:47.537 --> 02:37:48.338
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Just checking.

02:37:53.322 --> 02:38:00.748
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, because the second hit with the dagger has advantage.

02:38:02.808 --> 02:38:05.250
[Riv (DM)]: Yes, because your rapier has the vex.

02:38:05.748 --> 02:38:07.009
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, that's right.

02:38:07.112 --> 02:38:07.272
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

02:38:08.633 --> 02:38:09.353
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, that sounds right.

02:38:10.754 --> 02:38:13.196
[Riv (DM)]: If you hit a creature of your main attack, you get advantage on your next.

02:38:16.058 --> 02:38:18.020
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Yep, 22 will hit.

02:38:19.458 --> 02:38:22.740
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Okay. What's the damage on that?

02:38:27.052 --> 02:38:28.586
[Lacer (Kai)]: Well, it's not bad for a dagger.

02:38:30.748 --> 02:38:32.710
[Riv (DM)]: He's got it. He's got his Max Dex, I guess.

02:38:33.197 --> 02:38:36.404
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Sneak attack as well, cause advantage on that hit.

02:38:36.974 --> 02:38:37.194
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

02:38:51.106 --> 02:38:51.686
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:38:53.068 --> 02:38:53.808
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

02:38:54.624 --> 02:38:55.585
[JC (Ryo)]: Don't forget the Nick sign.

02:38:54.669 --> 02:38:55.430
[Riv (DM)]: Now we're cooking.

02:38:57.612 --> 02:38:57.952
[Riv (DM)]: Um.

02:38:59.689 --> 02:39:01.611
[JC (Ryo)]: Don't you get something extra for Nick from the dagger?

02:39:03.180 --> 02:39:08.124
[Riv (DM)]: It just means you can use it as part of your main attack or something like that, yeah? Yeah.

02:39:07.928 --> 02:39:08.509
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah.

02:39:10.766 --> 02:39:13.068
[Riv (DM)]: As part of the attack action instead of using up a bonus.

02:39:12.720 --> 02:39:14.506
[Vael (Berberis)]: So it's going to be space.

02:39:13.863 --> 02:39:14.424
[JC (Ryo)]: Alright, sorry.

02:39:15.000 --> 02:39:16.333
[Lacer (Kai)]: You can bonus action still.

02:39:15.349 --> 02:39:18.772
[Riv (DM)]: Or you could disengage or something.

02:39:21.338 --> 02:39:23.200
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, disengage. Why not?

02:39:31.866 --> 02:39:34.268
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: And just that extra step away.

02:39:36.160 --> 02:39:36.720
[Riv (DM)]: All right, nice.

02:39:41.302 --> 02:39:57.689
[Riv (DM)]: The massive creature comes chasing... Oh, and naturally catches up. You see, Kai, as you've gotten a bit closer now, this winged entity in front of you who has been calling for aid appears to be quite injured.

02:40:02.751 --> 02:40:04.952
[Riv (DM)]: All right, the creature catches up, though.

02:40:07.121 --> 02:40:08.042
[Riv (DM)]: It's going to...

02:40:12.145 --> 02:40:13.426
[Riv (DM)]: It's going to do a rind on it.

02:40:19.510 --> 02:40:23.693
[Riv (DM)]: You see it reach out these massive claws and one, two, three.

02:40:25.475 --> 02:40:27.076
[Riv (DM)]: So it catches up and just slices up his back.

02:40:35.980 --> 02:40:39.401
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, and only one of them hits. All right.

02:40:45.768 --> 02:40:51.611
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Look, I was kind of half expecting you'd get all three of those to hit and one of them be a crit and this guy just dies before his first turn.

02:40:45.843 --> 02:40:46.683
[Riv (DM)]: Those are nice looking dice.

02:40:51.205 --> 02:40:57.267
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, yeah. Yep, that would make this fight interesting, that's for sure. All right.

02:41:00.388 --> 02:41:01.388
[Riv (DM)]: Cool, yeah, and that'll be it's turn.

02:41:05.288 --> 02:41:05.668
[Riv (DM)]: Next up.

02:41:07.109 --> 02:41:09.310
[Riv (DM)]: It is actually our friend's turn here.

02:41:11.711 --> 02:41:26.497
[Riv (DM)]: Seeing you start to rally and you have cast spells, you've sliced at one of these birds, gaining a bit of morale, it turns and it strikes back. And so we will give it... What are we going to do here?

02:41:28.878 --> 02:41:33.400
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I think it's probably actually just going to respond with its...

02:41:34.572 --> 02:41:35.993
[Riv (DM)]: What about sword attack?

02:41:44.659 --> 02:41:45.940
[Riv (DM)]: First attack, we'll do another two.

02:41:56.267 --> 02:42:03.932
[Riv (DM)]: Oh man. Okay, so kind of reeling from being sliced, all three actually miss. You see it's sort of like,

02:42:05.137 --> 02:42:14.405
[Riv (DM)]: Almost starting to look a little bit exhausted. It turns, it swings its massive two-handed sword, and this horned creature just easily shrugs them away.

02:42:16.807 --> 02:42:27.496
[Riv (DM)]: And I think... Yeah... Um...

02:42:35.212 --> 02:42:56.003
[Riv (DM)]: I think actually, yeah, it's probably going to stay. All right, it's going to stay. It will also need to make a saving throw.

02:42:57.284 --> 02:43:04.648
[Riv (DM)]: So you can see this big like fume-ish cloud starting to sort of billow out from around this creature.

02:43:06.036 --> 02:43:10.999
[Riv (DM)]: The entity, the winged entity doesn't seem to be affected by the shrugs it off.

02:43:12.740 --> 02:43:13.021
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

02:43:16.202 --> 02:43:16.963
[Riv (DM)]: Up next, we've got.

02:43:19.084 --> 02:43:26.188
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:43:20.552 --> 02:43:24.320
[Lacer (Kai)]: Needs to make a con saving throw since it's in my spear's vicinity.

02:43:30.471 --> 02:43:31.251
[Riv (DM)]: Actually, you know what?

02:43:30.672 --> 02:43:32.316
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, it's saved.

02:43:35.072 --> 02:43:40.534
[Riv (DM)]: I think that after making its attacks, it probably would have run a bit further.

02:43:41.794 --> 02:43:48.376
[Riv (DM)]: It's going to come up and it's going to form ranks with you. And it will take an attack of opportunity from our guy here.

02:43:49.796 --> 02:43:58.559
[Riv (DM)]: We'll just another end. Oh, okay.

02:44:00.840 --> 02:44:01.400
[Riv (DM)]: Do damage.

02:44:06.688 --> 02:44:07.149
[Riv (DM)]: Alright.

02:44:11.573 --> 02:44:16.858
[Riv (DM)]: Another big slice and you see she almost stumbles as they move forward. Turn.

02:44:18.379 --> 02:44:19.220
[Riv (DM)]: Form ranks with you, Kai.

02:44:20.401 --> 02:44:25.326
[Riv (DM)]: First one has succeeded its con save, so is that half damage or...?

02:44:25.128 --> 02:44:28.009
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, it's half damage, so let's roll.

02:44:26.467 --> 02:44:27.387
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Yep, roll it out.

02:44:31.611 --> 02:44:31.932
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:44:33.280 --> 02:44:37.682
[Riv (DM)]: You see like a couple of the creatures in the swarm just like instantly sort of get vaporized.

02:44:38.842 --> 02:44:49.427
[Riv (DM)]: They will run their full distance to here. They sort of like scramble over the back of the... Oh yeah.

02:44:45.738 --> 02:44:48.220
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, it's a group. I thought it was a whole thing.

02:44:50.848 --> 02:45:11.415
[Riv (DM)]: It cannot make any further than that. So it's just going to use its action to dash, get a little bit closer and it comes into combat range with Yukai and you can see it's just like this This pile of these just absolutely hideous, almost like rat looking creatures, like goo, pustules growing all over their body, snarling and snapping at you, getting ready to try and tear you to shreds.

02:45:12.556 --> 02:45:27.226
[Riv (DM)]: But that actually ends its turn because it couldn't make it there in one. Berberis, is there anything you can do in a stun or we... Yeah, just wondering in case you had a secret trick.

02:45:23.045 --> 02:45:24.926
[Vael (Berberis)]: When you stomp, you just stomp, stomp your foot.

02:45:31.660 --> 02:45:35.401
[Riv (DM)]: This one is going to. All the way.

02:45:38.442 --> 02:45:38.683
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:45:41.484 --> 02:45:44.905
[Riv (DM)]: That takes us to Dondar, who I think you were also stunned.

02:45:47.966 --> 02:45:50.987
[Riv (DM)]: Erendor was the only one who survived, right? Or rather, who didn't get stunned. Cool.

02:45:51.987 --> 02:45:53.448
[Riv (DM)]: All right. And then that goes to Rio.

02:45:55.701 --> 02:45:59.544
[JC (Ryo)]: How high is this rib? Can I get up there or not really?

02:46:00.788 --> 02:46:05.933
[Riv (DM)]: You could make a athletics check to try and pull yourself up and use half your movement.

02:46:14.018 --> 02:46:16.540
[JC (Ryo)]: I'd swap back to the longbow before this fight if that's okay.

02:46:17.103 --> 02:46:17.744
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, all good.

02:46:21.256 --> 02:46:26.998
[JC (Ryo)]: No, I get like three Hunter's Mark Stone. Where is it? Favour Denimry is not yet.

02:46:29.099 --> 02:46:30.259
[JC (Ryo)]: On the bird thingy.

02:46:33.360 --> 02:46:35.461
[JC (Ryo)]: And I'm going to try and put some arrows into it.

02:46:43.623 --> 02:46:46.504
[JC (Ryo)]: Oh, I didn't mean to roll the damage, but I'm assuming that hits.

02:46:47.195 --> 02:46:56.460
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, totally. Where did their favorite enemy come from? All right, cool.

02:46:52.800 --> 02:46:54.221
[JC (Ryo)]: That's the 300's mark.

02:47:05.248 --> 02:47:07.209
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. So it's two heads.

02:47:06.634 --> 02:47:09.016
[JC (Ryo)]: I'm at quick damage, but yeah, two hits into the big thing.

02:47:09.351 --> 02:47:14.094
[Riv (DM)]: Yep. Perfect. Oh, sorry. Big, big thing. You mean the bird or the bird?

02:47:12.339 --> 02:47:13.040
[JC (Ryo)]: The bird, the bird.

02:47:15.235 --> 02:47:15.515
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:47:22.572 --> 02:47:29.854
[JC (Ryo)]: And I've got one more Hunter's Mark, which is a D6, I'm pretty sure. I'll roll another D6.

02:47:34.708 --> 02:47:34.988
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:47:36.756 --> 02:47:39.059
[Riv (DM)]: So we've got 12, 25, 30.

02:47:40.158 --> 02:47:46.940
[JC (Ryo)]: Twelve, 25, 34. I accidentally rolled the four from the Hunter's Mark earlier as well.

02:47:43.523 --> 02:47:45.165
[Riv (DM)]: All right, the falls from the hunter's mark.

02:47:48.169 --> 02:47:51.412
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Ah, yeah, yeah. So that's the, that was for the tax. Yep. Perfect.

02:47:51.488 --> 02:47:54.710
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, I didn't mean to roll the damage before it hit, but yeah, wrong button.

02:47:51.973 --> 02:47:52.213
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

02:47:53.735 --> 02:47:54.115
[Riv (DM)]: All good.

02:47:55.276 --> 02:47:58.340
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. You see the creature takes a couple of bolts.

02:47:59.444 --> 02:48:07.229
[Riv (DM)]: One into the wing, one right into its chest, and it sort of like shrieks out and you almost see it like struggle to lift itself up above the lava pool that it is hovering over.

02:48:08.590 --> 02:48:12.452
[Riv (DM)]: It looks like it took a good chunk of damage and is looking bloodied.

02:48:13.582 --> 02:48:17.024
[JC (Ryo)]: And then I will use my movement to get up here.

02:48:18.315 --> 02:48:20.517
[Riv (DM)]: All right, make a athletics saving throw.

02:48:22.958 --> 02:48:25.620
[Riv (DM)]: Sorry, athletics ability check is what we're after.

02:48:32.372 --> 02:48:41.860
[Riv (DM)]: Ooh, okay. You start scrambling up and you actually don't make it. We can say you are like hanging, if you like, halfway up and you can finish off on the next turn.

02:48:43.976 --> 02:48:52.166
[JC (Ryo)]: Hanging or I fell down, your call.

02:48:46.543 --> 02:48:50.567
[Riv (DM)]: What would you rather? So if you started realizing you're failing to get up, would you rather drop back down?

02:48:52.407 --> 02:48:53.388
[JC (Ryo)]: How high is the climb?

02:48:54.610 --> 02:48:54.970
[Riv (DM)]: Ten feet.

02:48:55.731 --> 02:48:56.612
[JC (Ryo)]: Oh yeah, drop back down.

02:48:57.172 --> 02:49:01.876
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, cool. Yeah, you're good. All right, nice. And then the last swarm.

02:49:05.631 --> 02:49:06.167
[Lacer (Kai)]: Unsave.

02:49:09.103 --> 02:49:09.904
[Riv (DM)]: We come to here.

02:49:12.905 --> 02:49:13.326
[Riv (DM)]: Alrighty.

02:49:18.088 --> 02:49:18.889
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, huge failure.

02:49:24.872 --> 02:49:30.695
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Another big lightning beam.

02:49:31.936 --> 02:49:38.294
[Riv (DM)]: Another bunch of them. Just evaporate. Beautiful. All right. So now that actually takes us to the top of the round. And Kai, you're up.

02:49:40.156 --> 02:50:00.145
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'd like to control my spear and move my spear. Let's see. Move 60 feet. So I'm guessing I'm going to drop it right on top of the mic here. I seem to not be able to move this thing, the spear.

02:49:58.900 --> 02:49:59.520
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, yeah, yeah.

02:50:00.652 --> 02:50:03.033
[Riv (DM)]: There's a different place where I can move it for you. It's all good.

02:50:05.333 --> 02:50:06.614
[Riv (DM)]: So you want to move it on top of these guys?

02:50:07.948 --> 02:50:08.428
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:50:09.580 --> 02:50:12.461
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, I suppose. So yeah, right there to hit both of them.

02:50:11.035 --> 02:50:25.119
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, it's all good. Yeah, it's great.

02:50:17.642 --> 02:50:29.846
[Lacer (Kai)]: So since it's my turn, I guess they have to make a con save. No, it's an action to move it.

02:50:25.139 --> 02:50:28.300
[Riv (DM)]: So bonus action, you move it, two con saves from the guys.

02:50:30.186 --> 02:50:31.926
[Lacer (Kai)]: It requires my action to move.

02:50:32.736 --> 02:50:33.036
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, is it?

02:50:33.507 --> 02:50:37.708
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, because I want to, yeah, requires on subsequent turns an action to move.

02:50:38.120 --> 02:50:45.407
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool. Like, one on the right is a success and one on the left is a fail.

02:50:46.124 --> 02:50:49.085
[Lacer (Kai)]: Do I need to roll damage twice or just once?

02:50:48.769 --> 02:50:53.433
[Riv (DM)]: It'll just be once, right?

02:50:52.766 --> 02:50:53.746
[Lacer (Kai)]: Up to you, what you want.

02:50:53.894 --> 02:50:55.615
[Riv (DM)]: Since it passes through them at the same time, at least once.

02:50:55.707 --> 02:50:55.907
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:50:55.735 --> 02:50:59.538
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, huge. You want that anyway. Nearly makes damage.

02:51:01.280 --> 02:51:05.010
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. So that would be... who succeeded this guy?

02:51:06.876 --> 02:51:08.963
[Riv (DM)]: Seven there, and fifteen here. Beautiful.

02:51:09.790 --> 02:51:15.312
[Lacer (Kai)]: All right, and then I'm going to use my bonus action to cast Healing Word on

02:51:16.332 --> 02:51:23.877
[Lacer (Kai)]: This devil guy next to me at first level, and then it's going to trigger my chalice.

02:51:28.321 --> 02:51:32.183
[Lacer (Kai)]: Let me hit chalice. Does auto do chalice? No.

02:51:44.132 --> 02:51:44.332
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yep.

02:51:46.840 --> 02:51:50.701
[Lacer (Kai)]: And I pumped that into him. I said, yeah.

02:51:52.736 --> 02:51:56.099
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, cool. Roll a wisdom check for me, please, Kai.

02:51:57.120 --> 02:51:59.062
[Riv (DM)]: You won a beta 10. That's it. Easy for you.

02:52:01.005 --> 02:52:02.085
[Lacer (Kai)]: I end up killing him.

02:52:02.942 --> 02:52:03.696
[JC (Ryo)]: If he's not dead...

02:52:05.006 --> 02:52:06.826
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, a wisdom check.

02:52:06.320 --> 02:52:07.786
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'm dead. He's a fake.

02:52:07.447 --> 02:52:09.667
[Lacer (Kai)]: Is it just a wisdom check? A regular wisdom check?

02:52:10.552 --> 02:52:10.752
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:52:11.508 --> 02:52:11.848
[Lacer (Kai)]: Okay.

02:52:16.918 --> 02:52:18.579
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. What are you worrying about?

02:52:20.152 --> 02:52:24.776
[Riv (DM)]: You see, so 20 total healing. Nice.

02:52:25.837 --> 02:52:46.536
[Riv (DM)]: So what does your healing normally look like? Hmm.

02:52:30.529 --> 02:52:45.957
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess it looks like it illuminates the... I guess some words come out of my mouth because it's a healing word and when it flies towards the entity, I guess it absorbs into their wounds and it starts to close up their wounds.

02:52:48.236 --> 02:52:51.539
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. And it's sort of like this lunar sort of glowing pale light, yeah?

02:52:52.120 --> 02:52:52.320
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:52:52.719 --> 02:53:00.185
[Riv (DM)]: You see it sort of like wraps around them and starts to target the broken wing, the claws that have been shredding up through the armor on the back.

02:53:01.185 --> 02:53:08.110
[Riv (DM)]: You hear the creature sort of scream out in pain as you see the lunar light. It's almost starting to like hiss and sizzle on the skin.

02:53:09.171 --> 02:53:25.177
[Riv (DM)]: But through the pain of it, the wing does start to mend a little bit. And it does start to heal. And you just, you hear the female voice say, ah, you're cursed. Holy. Keep that to yourself.

02:53:27.328 --> 02:53:28.069
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Email voice.

02:53:30.299 --> 02:53:31.920
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Anything else, Kai, on your turn?

02:53:30.975 --> 02:53:31.396
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, well.

02:53:32.756 --> 02:53:35.157
[Lacer (Kai)]: Ah, no, that ended my turn. That's it for me.

02:53:35.722 --> 02:53:39.344
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. All right. And that takes us to Erendor.

02:53:40.218 --> 02:53:48.461
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, wait, wait, wait. There's only one thing. As the spear flew by, leaving a trail of spears, it would have hit this guy, and he would have had to take a con save as well.

02:53:50.016 --> 02:53:50.717
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah, nice.

02:53:51.081 --> 02:53:51.282
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:53:52.218 --> 02:53:54.520
[Riv (DM)]: Does it work like that? Does it like, does it pass as like a laser?

02:53:53.242 --> 02:53:56.123
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah. Yeah, yeah. Like a laser.

02:53:55.601 --> 02:53:57.222
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right, nice.

02:53:57.323 --> 02:53:59.404
[Lacer (Kai)]: So it leaves a trail of spears.

02:54:01.846 --> 02:54:03.348
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. So he takes seven damage out.

02:54:04.509 --> 02:54:15.838
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. You see it just like passes over him and it just barely even nudges him. Just like burns like side of his arm, he just glances down slightly and then just continues looking straight ahead.

02:54:17.340 --> 02:54:22.785
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Sorry, I told a lie. It's actually the vrock's turn. Yeah.

02:54:25.586 --> 02:54:27.567
[Riv (DM)]: It's going to make two attacks on you, Erendor.

02:54:30.220 --> 02:54:31.141
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Jesus Crust.

02:54:33.309 --> 02:54:33.509
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:54:34.860 --> 02:54:35.546
[Lacer (Kai)]: Let's not breach.

02:54:36.649 --> 02:54:41.871
[Riv (DM)]: Well, it'll fly over. I think that's two hits.

02:54:40.211 --> 02:54:40.732
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Aw man.

02:54:41.931 --> 02:54:44.792
[Riv (DM)]: That's a plus six. The AC is less than 20.

02:54:45.600 --> 02:54:54.567
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Casey is 17.

02:54:48.044 --> 02:54:48.304
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:54:52.027 --> 02:54:52.788
[Riv (DM)]: Ignore that roll.

02:54:54.587 --> 02:55:01.873
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I am going to... Shield.

02:55:02.976 --> 02:55:03.897
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

02:55:04.898 --> 02:55:10.302
[Riv (DM)]: Okay. Which attack would you like to shield? Oh, sorry. It works for all of them. Yeah, yeah, yeah.

02:55:09.919 --> 02:55:10.340
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: It does.

02:55:11.103 --> 02:55:15.767
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Okay. So let's actually go back and look. What did I roll? It would have been a 21 and a 20.

02:55:17.608 --> 02:55:30.396
[Riv (DM)]: Shield goes up. Max AC goes to 22 and you're laughing. Amazing. So it comes in, you see it like trying to shred the claws or almost something to like glow with this dark like purplish energy.

02:55:31.616 --> 02:55:37.860
[Riv (DM)]: You see it just coming straight for your face and reaction shield goes up and it just bounces off this invisible barrier.

02:55:39.081 --> 02:55:39.561
[Riv (DM)]: Incredible.

02:55:40.940 --> 02:55:41.595
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Critical miss?

02:55:43.003 --> 02:55:46.616
[Riv (DM)]: Not a critical miss, but definitely A successful evasion.

02:55:48.457 --> 02:55:51.518
[Riv (DM)]: That's a action to do that, so he can't do that.

02:55:53.118 --> 02:55:56.339
[Riv (DM)]: Perfect. It'll be his turn, I think. He's not going to try and fly away.

02:56:00.620 --> 02:56:01.121
[Riv (DM)]: What is he?

02:56:02.501 --> 02:56:06.582
[Riv (DM)]: Nah, he's not going to fly away. All right, beautiful. And it then takes us to Erendor.

02:56:07.304 --> 02:56:09.105
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: You know, I think that's the first time I've used that.

02:56:10.023 --> 02:56:11.604
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Hey, it's a pretty good spell.

02:56:14.168 --> 02:56:19.170
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Well, um, shockingly, I'm gonna, uh, make another swing with the Ember Blade.

02:56:32.599 --> 02:56:33.947
[Riv (DM)]: Fifteen total, is it?

02:56:36.160 --> 02:56:36.360
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yes.

02:57:08.348 --> 02:57:10.189
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Ooh, Riv's muted.

02:57:13.920 --> 02:57:14.180
[Lacer (Kai)]: Is he?

02:57:15.252 --> 02:57:15.472
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yes.

02:57:17.584 --> 02:57:19.447
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, something must happen.

02:57:21.196 --> 02:57:21.476
[Riv (DM)]: We good?

02:57:22.170 --> 02:57:23.351
[Lacer (Kai)]: You're muted for a moment.

02:57:22.277 --> 02:57:23.197
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yep, I can hear you.

02:57:23.792 --> 02:57:23.972
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

02:57:24.958 --> 02:57:25.759
[Riv (DM)]: Hang on.

02:57:26.800 --> 02:57:29.421
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, yeah. I have a mute. I must have accidentally wiped it. Sorry.

02:57:30.682 --> 02:57:30.882
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yes.

02:57:31.243 --> 02:57:33.524
[Riv (DM)]: You know, 15's good. Yep. Sorry about that.

02:57:33.263 --> 02:57:34.004
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Fifteen's good? Okay.

02:57:43.471 --> 02:57:45.212
[Riv (DM)]: All right. Any charges on this one?

02:57:45.617 --> 02:57:47.240
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I am not dropping charges on that one.

02:57:51.246 --> 02:57:55.152
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: And I will then make a swing with the dagger.

02:58:05.664 --> 02:58:06.165
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that's good.

02:58:10.976 --> 02:58:13.898
[JC (Ryo)]: The bird had minus 10 to its movement speed this turn as well, sorry.

02:58:15.253 --> 02:58:22.058
[Riv (DM)]: Mmm. Good. Yeah. That'll probably convince it not to run away then. Nice. All right, cool. Nine damage.

02:58:23.600 --> 02:58:24.921
[Riv (DM)]: You are railing this thing.

02:58:29.425 --> 02:58:29.805
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

02:58:32.668 --> 02:58:32.968
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful.

02:58:35.712 --> 02:58:38.174
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. Any other bonus actions?

02:58:39.913 --> 02:58:43.683
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Erm... Yeah, I will disengage once more.

02:58:46.000 --> 02:58:46.240
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

02:58:48.702 --> 02:58:58.249
[Riv (DM)]: All right. That brings us to Hezrealm. It will just run forward. To be honest, I think it's actually going to...

02:59:05.588 --> 02:59:06.349
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

02:59:09.391 --> 02:59:18.357
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, it's going to go after its quarry. It's just going to run straight through, kick these guys aside, which it can easily because they're a swarm. And just, I think it's probably going to go here.

02:59:20.078 --> 02:59:21.699
[Riv (DM)]: And take a slash.

02:59:23.500 --> 02:59:24.561
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, I think it needs to go here.

02:59:24.067 --> 02:59:24.310
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Ew.

02:59:25.062 --> 02:59:25.262
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

02:59:26.262 --> 02:59:26.483
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

02:59:37.580 --> 02:59:43.523
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. All right, cool. Sorry about that. So we're going to do, plus seven to hit.

02:59:45.724 --> 02:59:50.286
[Riv (DM)]: All of them into our friend here. Oh, terrible.

02:59:51.667 --> 03:00:02.732
[Riv (DM)]: It's a 20 on the max, which so the first two come in, you can see this figure sort of dodge

03:00:03.732 --> 03:00:21.045
[Riv (DM)]: We've first two miss, and the last one comes, looks like it's gonna make contact, but they're gonna use their parry reaction, sword comes up and block it, giving them plus four AC and causing it to miss. So all three attacks miss the creature, powers and frustration.

03:00:23.246 --> 03:00:25.488
[Riv (DM)]: Cool, all right, and that will end its turn.

03:00:28.770 --> 03:00:31.212
[Riv (DM)]: Starting on our friend's turn here,

03:00:32.736 --> 03:00:40.701
[Riv (DM)]: It will make a saving throw and I think Kai you also need to make a saving throw please.

03:00:42.112 --> 03:00:42.552
[Lacer (Kai)]: What type?

03:00:42.662 --> 03:00:43.502
[Riv (DM)]: Constitution.

03:00:45.143 --> 03:00:46.244
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah I'll just look in the chat for you.

03:00:50.626 --> 03:01:00.112
[Riv (DM)]: Oh man we're starting to get a lot of um a lot of mess on the board. I'm gonna delete this one.

03:01:07.119 --> 03:01:09.000
[Lacer (Kai)]: Elstinch. Ugh.

03:01:15.328 --> 03:01:18.549
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, both fail. You have the poisoned condition.

03:01:19.724 --> 03:01:22.786
[Lacer (Kai)]: Do I need to take a concentration check for my spell?

03:01:23.452 --> 03:01:26.953
[Riv (DM)]: It doesn't do damage.

03:01:28.714 --> 03:01:34.376
[Riv (DM)]: It just means that you have disadvantage on ability checks and attack rolls.

03:01:33.893 --> 03:01:40.078
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, that's... Sure.

03:01:36.857 --> 03:01:40.819
[Riv (DM)]: So yeah, you can chuck the poison condition on yourself if you like, or I can do it for you.

03:01:40.378 --> 03:01:42.439
[Lacer (Kai)]: Okay, I will... I am now poisoned.

03:01:43.040 --> 03:01:43.280
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

03:01:43.080 --> 03:01:43.580
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yuck.

03:01:43.920 --> 03:01:56.949
[Riv (DM)]: So, and our friend here actually seems to be entirely immune, does not even worry about it at all, just passes over them. Cool, alright. And it is their turn, so they are going to...

03:02:06.595 --> 03:02:15.013
[Riv (DM)]: Cool, alright, nice. So, two sword attacks are going to come out. That's the first one. Oh dude, come on. Come on, you can do it.

03:02:17.054 --> 03:02:21.538
[Riv (DM)]: My streak of just terrible rolls is continuing, but this time you guys will want me to be rolling well.

03:02:22.859 --> 03:02:24.881
[Riv (DM)]: One of them hits. We'll just do the damage now.

03:02:30.605 --> 03:02:31.526
[Riv (DM)]: All right, so 24.

03:02:34.849 --> 03:02:43.356
[Riv (DM)]: And then you'll see the figure will, at its waist, the coiled sort of slightly glowing rope

03:02:44.396 --> 03:02:53.424
[Riv (DM)]: It'll just whip out one hand, sling it out in an extremely skilled whip, and the creature will make a saving throw.

03:03:01.811 --> 03:03:03.492
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, which it succeeds.

03:03:05.434 --> 03:03:12.680
[Riv (DM)]: Is there any damage on a... Nope, it is a save or suck, so all good.

03:03:13.340 --> 03:03:14.347
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: They've all suck.

03:03:22.048 --> 03:03:26.529
[Riv (DM)]: Will it move? No, it's going to hold its ground now, so it will stay there.

03:03:31.411 --> 03:03:37.494
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful. All right, here we go. We now go to one of the swarms. Kai, got some attacks coming your direction.

03:03:38.104 --> 03:03:38.924
[Lacer (Kai)]: Un-save first.

03:03:41.035 --> 03:03:42.076
[Riv (DM)]: Yup, let's do that.

03:03:43.828 --> 03:03:45.149
[Riv (DM)]: And these guys have been getting whittled down.

03:03:47.490 --> 03:03:48.230
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, I think that's failure.

03:03:51.548 --> 03:04:04.392
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, I just have to scroll all the way back up to always re-roll the damage. Oh yeah, I guess so, yeah.

03:03:58.914 --> 03:04:00.215
[Riv (DM)]: It's just a 2d10, right?

03:04:01.755 --> 03:04:02.195
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, nice.

03:04:04.596 --> 03:04:13.140
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, beautiful. Oh yeah, man, another couple of the creatures in the swarm actually die. There's just one left and it's going to come at you.

03:04:14.240 --> 03:04:15.962
[Riv (DM)]: And take a couple of swipes.

03:04:25.110 --> 03:04:26.331
[Riv (DM)]: First one misses.

03:04:28.473 --> 03:04:31.876
[Riv (DM)]: Next one also misses. All right. All right, cool.

03:04:33.457 --> 03:04:36.420
[Riv (DM)]: No stress there. All right, Berberis, you're up.

03:04:40.988 --> 03:04:42.709
[Vael (Berberis)]: Out of the days where I'm stunned.

03:04:44.190 --> 03:04:49.193
[Vael (Berberis)]: Look at the bird beside me. I'm not in melee range of it. It's pretty injured, I'm guessing.

03:04:50.484 --> 03:04:51.524
[Riv (DM)]: It's looking pretty injured, yeah.

03:04:53.155 --> 03:05:02.020
[Vael (Berberis)]: All right, non-issue, non-factor. I'm going to step forward. And I would like a 15 foot cube, please.

03:05:03.269 --> 03:05:03.749
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:05:06.383 --> 03:05:07.944
[Vael (Berberis)]: Do you have a 15 foot cube template?

03:05:06.870 --> 03:05:10.972
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, I can check something for you. What are you after?

03:05:12.492 --> 03:05:13.753
[Vael (Berberis)]: Now I'm thunder waving.

03:05:14.833 --> 03:05:22.778
[Vael (Berberis)]: Basically the three grid squares in front of me not catching our friend three by three. So I'm catching the two dredge packs and the big guy.

03:05:20.364 --> 03:05:21.125
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so like this.

03:05:23.226 --> 03:05:23.886
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, nice.

03:05:27.609 --> 03:05:27.829
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

03:05:29.670 --> 03:05:34.734
[Riv (DM)]: I think the Thunder Wave will probably give you a... Oh, is it not showing up for you?

03:05:33.964 --> 03:05:36.985
[Vael (Berberis)]: Oh yeah, it says place measure template.

03:05:38.656 --> 03:05:41.838
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, I've drawn something, but it's not showing up.

03:05:41.067 --> 03:05:41.928
[Vael (Berberis)]: There we go, yep.

03:05:42.099 --> 03:05:43.119
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, all good. Anyway, yep.

03:05:44.116 --> 03:05:45.877
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'm doing it at fourth level.

03:05:44.440 --> 03:05:44.660
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

03:05:46.542 --> 03:05:50.084
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, nice. All right, cool.

03:05:47.360 --> 03:05:47.529
[Lacer (Kai)]: Urgh.

03:05:48.762 --> 03:05:48.984
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Woah.

03:05:49.559 --> 03:05:53.201
[Vael (Berberis)]: DC 17 con saves on the two packs of the big guy.

03:05:53.612 --> 03:05:54.052
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful.

03:05:55.713 --> 03:05:58.493
[Riv (DM)]: Who would you like me to do a first for? Should we do the little dretches first?

03:05:58.803 --> 03:05:59.944
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah, just work from left to right.

03:06:00.454 --> 03:06:15.758
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. First one, failure. Big boy. Oh, also a failure. All right.

03:06:17.238 --> 03:06:17.879
[Riv (DM)]: And the last pack.

03:06:20.099 --> 03:06:23.180
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, look at that. Three for three. All right. So it'd be max damage.

03:06:24.388 --> 03:06:26.169
[Riv (DM)]: What's that? Is that 5d8?

03:06:26.600 --> 03:06:27.181
[Vael (Berberis)]: 5d8.

03:06:28.410 --> 03:06:28.791
[Riv (DM)]: Let's go.

03:06:29.945 --> 03:06:30.346
[Vael (Berberis)]: Mixed rolling.

03:06:31.752 --> 03:06:36.755
[Riv (DM)]: Pretty good. 21 to 3 targets is 63 damage.

03:06:37.616 --> 03:06:42.904
[Vael (Berberis)]: And they are all blasted back ten feet.

03:06:39.796 --> 03:06:54.868
[Riv (DM)]: Yep. You see these ones here go flying against the wall and they scatter and a couple of them almost like get pierced on these like sort of jagged cliff edges and these ones die. These ones.

03:06:56.089 --> 03:06:58.870
[Riv (DM)]: How far back do they go? Nice.

03:06:57.560 --> 03:06:57.932
[Vael (Berberis)]: Ten feet.

03:07:00.971 --> 03:07:05.233
[Riv (DM)]: These ones go flying back this way. Again, getting scattered, just like flying in all directions.

03:07:06.833 --> 03:07:11.735
[Riv (DM)]: Most of them die, only a couple left standing. And big guy also, actually let me just double check if he can be moved.

03:07:15.037 --> 03:07:16.397
[Riv (DM)]: Yes, seems like he can. There you go.

03:07:17.418 --> 03:07:22.600
[Riv (DM)]: Just like he was stumbling back this way. And he also takes the 21 damage. Beautiful.

03:07:27.292 --> 03:07:27.672
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:07:28.320 --> 03:07:29.142
[Lacer (Kai)]: Did they leave my reach?

03:07:31.256 --> 03:07:35.801
[Vael (Berberis)]: Forced action doesn't work.

03:07:32.194 --> 03:07:34.815
[Riv (DM)]: In theory, yes, but this wouldn't, it wouldn't trigger.

03:07:33.351 --> 03:07:36.778
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, it's forced action, never mind. Yeah, it's not, yeah, never mind.

03:07:36.362 --> 03:07:36.883
[Vael (Berberis)]: Unfortunately.

03:07:40.066 --> 03:07:45.112
[Vael (Berberis)]: And I'll step forward into the line and bonus action Shaloli into my hand.

03:07:46.180 --> 03:07:49.181
[Riv (DM)]: This stuff starts to charge up.

03:07:51.082 --> 03:07:54.564
[Riv (DM)]: So if you have a look at your stuff now, by the way, I've added the additional magic text to it.

03:07:55.980 --> 03:07:58.981
[Riv (DM)]: Do you have an idea around what it looks like when you charge it up with Shillelagh?

03:08:06.828 --> 03:08:11.452
[Vael (Berberis)]: Any changes? I don't see any changes.

03:08:08.706 --> 03:08:09.946
[Riv (DM)]: Sorry, I think you cut out a little bit.

03:08:11.472 --> 03:08:16.055
[Vael (Berberis)]: I see you've got topple on it. Oh yeah, and then you've got bloom.

03:08:13.748 --> 03:08:17.390
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, down the bottom.

03:08:16.135 --> 03:08:17.036
[Vael (Berberis)]: Okay, yeah, cool.

03:08:17.410 --> 03:08:20.031
[Riv (DM)]: It's the same thing, just it doesn't cost charges anymore, because why not?

03:08:20.637 --> 03:08:21.326
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Orlando.

03:08:23.340 --> 03:08:24.561
[Vael (Berberis)]: Just like glowing green.

03:08:27.283 --> 03:08:32.427
[Vael (Berberis)]: A few little leaves and sparkly planty flavorings.

03:08:33.129 --> 03:08:34.050
[Riv (DM)]: Lore gets charged up.

03:08:35.216 --> 03:08:41.921
[Vael (Berberis)]: Action and bonus. I just want to quickly check if any of my features are free. I don't believe so.

03:08:45.964 --> 03:08:57.872
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah, land-aid is an action. I can't just pray that I don't get hit with a weapon.

03:09:03.944 --> 03:09:05.108
[Vael (Berberis)]: Nope, that's me, I'm good.

03:09:06.874 --> 03:09:07.536
[Vael (Berberis)]: Avernus movement.

03:09:08.236 --> 03:09:16.922
[Riv (DM)]: You stand shoulder to shoulder with this winged armoured figure. You feel the flames off the wings starting to sort of brush against you and actually burn you a little bit.

03:09:18.303 --> 03:09:21.946
[Riv (DM)]: Incredible. All right. We go to the next swarm. Swarm that just got blasted away.

03:09:23.226 --> 03:09:36.376
[Riv (DM)]: Gibbering and jabbering. They will make a constitution save as they start running back into the fight. This moonbeam's been doing some fucking work, man. All right. I think it was DC 15, right?

03:09:37.461 --> 03:09:37.766
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, 17.

03:09:38.632 --> 03:09:39.853
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, nice. All right. Full damage.

03:09:40.780 --> 03:09:41.181
[Vael (Berberis)]: Seventeen?

03:09:43.688 --> 03:09:48.500
[Vael (Berberis)]: Wow, that's actually impressive because I have like a plus two to mine and I'm only seventeen.

03:09:59.243 --> 03:09:59.703
[Riv (DM)]: All righty.

03:10:00.864 --> 03:10:01.645
[Riv (DM)]: A damage. Cool.

03:10:03.285 --> 03:10:16.251
[Riv (DM)]: All right. There is one remaining dredge left. But it is enough. It's gonna come. I think it's gonna target you, Kai, again, now that you've been doing so much damage to it.

03:10:20.955 --> 03:10:24.998
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, nice, yeah.

03:10:26.380 --> 03:10:27.040
[Riv (DM)]: We do a rend.

03:10:33.586 --> 03:10:35.687
[Riv (DM)]: And we do another rend. What are you at?

03:10:37.440 --> 03:10:38.063
[Lacer (Kai)]: My AC?

03:10:39.408 --> 03:10:41.035
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, I've got 20 AC now.

03:10:41.540 --> 03:10:42.821
[Riv (DM)]: All right, cool.

03:10:43.862 --> 03:10:47.425
[Riv (DM)]: Last little guy's just trying to scrape at you and absolutely fails.

03:10:48.806 --> 03:10:49.186
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful.

03:10:53.830 --> 03:10:54.231
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:10:58.894 --> 03:11:05.440
[Riv (DM)]: We see another bird appear on the horizon, comes shrieking down, gets this far.

03:11:10.504 --> 03:11:14.765
[Riv (DM)]: I will get a constitution saving throw from Kai and Ryo.

03:11:16.384 --> 03:11:17.685
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh no, oh no.

03:11:19.431 --> 03:11:19.821
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh no.

03:11:21.687 --> 03:11:25.709
[Riv (DM)]: Ooh, I actually aimed the friend as well. I think Berberis, you're just outside of it.

03:11:28.180 --> 03:11:29.541
[Vael (Berberis)]: Kai, you have War Custer.

03:11:30.654 --> 03:11:35.837
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, wait, but that's only for my concentration spells? This is just a normal con save, isn't it, for thing?

03:11:35.312 --> 03:11:36.012
[Riv (DM)]: This is just a con save.

03:11:35.425 --> 03:11:45.012
[Vael (Berberis)]: I thought you got... Yeah, Concentration, yeah.

03:11:36.252 --> 03:11:36.752
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, yeah, yeah.

03:11:38.699 --> 03:11:44.163
[Lacer (Kai)]: I do have Warcaster, but it's only for concentrating on spells, right, is it? Or do I get advantage on all con saves?

03:11:45.036 --> 03:11:45.897
[Riv (DM)]: Just concentration.

03:11:45.464 --> 03:11:46.456
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yep. Yeah.

03:11:46.197 --> 03:11:55.524
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. OK, Kai, you're stunned. And our friend is actually also in this, so we'll make a con save for her.

03:11:50.600 --> 03:11:50.841
[Lacer (Kai)]: Orc.

03:11:57.586 --> 03:12:04.492
[Riv (DM)]: Our friend, not friend. Oh, god damn, it was so close to being a 19. All right, shit.

03:12:06.133 --> 03:12:06.814
[Riv (DM)]: We do the damage.

03:12:09.459 --> 03:12:10.260
[Lacer (Kai)]: All this damage.

03:12:11.938 --> 03:12:13.359
[Riv (DM)]: 16 damage to...

03:12:15.932 --> 03:12:19.214
[Riv (DM)]: The erinyes and Kai and then 9 damage to Ryo.

03:12:21.536 --> 03:12:23.838
[Lacer (Kai)]: Since I'm stunned, I can't react, right?

03:12:22.224 --> 03:12:43.484
[JC (Ryo)]: 8 or 9 damage Riv, you said 9.

03:12:24.677 --> 03:12:34.002
[Riv (DM)]: Nope.

03:12:25.933 --> 03:12:27.815
[Vael (Berberis)]: Also, concentration breaks.

03:12:28.844 --> 03:12:29.925
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: There's two of them?

03:12:29.522 --> 03:12:32.144
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, stun breaks concentration.

03:12:32.479 --> 03:12:33.480
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah, you are incapped.

03:12:34.042 --> 03:12:44.308
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

03:12:34.987 --> 03:12:41.632
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, no. Okay. Hold up, let me take... Okay.

03:12:44.348 --> 03:12:45.008
[Riv (DM)]: 9 damage, yeah.

03:12:45.887 --> 03:12:46.407
[JC (Ryo)]: Half 16 is 8.

03:12:46.224 --> 03:12:52.070
[Riv (DM)]: It was... Oh, sorry, I was looking at your saving throw.

03:12:54.192 --> 03:12:56.474
[Riv (DM)]: Your saving throw is 18. No, you're good. Yeah, it was eight.

03:12:59.116 --> 03:13:00.624
[Lacer (Kai)]: Let me just make a mark off some HP.

03:13:03.201 --> 03:13:10.488
[Riv (DM)]: Donda, you shrug yourself out of your days and you come to seeing your friends getting harassed. What do you do?

03:13:12.072 --> 03:13:16.054
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Alright, the wing thing on my left, how far above the lava is it?

03:13:18.373 --> 03:13:20.414
[Riv (DM)]: Probably like five feet, like it's just floating right above it.

03:13:22.196 --> 03:13:30.160
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I'm gonna cast Bigby's Hand and use Forceful Hand to punch it into the lava.

03:13:26.077 --> 03:13:26.577
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:13:29.939 --> 03:13:35.362
[Riv (DM)]: Okay. Let's put it in the chat, let me read the text. It's been a while since I've seen old Bigby's.

03:13:37.083 --> 03:13:38.924
[Riv (DM)]: All right, forceful hand. So you do a push.

03:13:42.566 --> 03:13:50.808
[Riv (DM)]: Saving throw, hand pushes up to five feet plus a number of feet. Five times spellcasting. All right. Incredible. So I need to make a strength saving throw.

03:13:49.632 --> 03:13:51.578
[Vael (Berberis)]: Time to Heim, you're still casting.

03:13:52.450 --> 03:13:53.671
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that's pretty good.

03:13:52.618 --> 03:13:52.803
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Oof.

03:13:54.171 --> 03:13:56.253
[Riv (DM)]: Let's see how strong this guy has aim.

03:13:59.136 --> 03:14:01.098
[Riv (DM)]: Can I roll it like that? Yeah, I think I can.

03:14:00.972 --> 03:14:01.625
[JC (Ryo)]: No casting.

03:14:04.501 --> 03:14:12.288
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, nearly a natural 20. But it's not able to. So this massive hand just appears. Or actually, Donna, you describe it for us.

03:14:15.148 --> 03:14:27.715
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I imagined it like a hand of Thanos with the gauntlet, but just an iron steel gauntlet.

03:14:22.000 --> 03:14:22.380
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah?

03:14:26.423 --> 03:14:29.144
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Sorta like Dwarven armor or something.

03:14:29.776 --> 03:14:33.458
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, dwarven armor, but otherwise purple. Yeah.

03:14:32.946 --> 03:14:35.988
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

03:14:37.008 --> 03:14:45.061
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: It will punch the winged thing in the center mass above the lava.

03:14:42.331 --> 03:14:42.532
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

03:14:46.723 --> 03:14:49.808
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: And yeah, straight into the lava.

03:14:47.194 --> 03:14:48.195
[Riv (DM)]: Straight down into it, yeah?

03:14:49.616 --> 03:14:59.375
[Riv (DM)]: Very nice. Giant purple hand, sort of golden burnished armor appears around outside it as well. Comes in, hand outstretched and just sort of grabs it and just shoves it straight down into the lava.

03:15:00.456 --> 03:15:19.092
[Riv (DM)]: Dondar, I'll get you to roll some lava damage for me. Now because you can push it, I think your spellcasting mod will be... 5, right? Is your ints 20? Your int's 4, so you can push it 25 feet. So you can push it real deep into the lava.

03:15:20.144 --> 03:15:26.690
[Riv (DM)]: Get your dice out for me. I want you to write a slash roll 18d10.

03:15:28.736 --> 03:15:29.336
[Lacer (Kai)]: Holy.

03:15:32.108 --> 03:15:32.548
[Vael (Berberis)]: Fire.

03:15:32.588 --> 03:15:33.509
[JC (Ryo)]: This ain't coming back.

03:15:32.949 --> 03:15:48.439
[Vael (Berberis)]: Hot. 18d10 is one word.

03:15:37.824 --> 03:15:38.867
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Sure, that's the right command.

03:15:39.621 --> 03:15:42.283
[Riv (DM)]: Slash roll space 18d10.

03:15:39.953 --> 03:15:54.384
[JC (Ryo)]: Splash R. Splash R space as well.

03:15:42.458 --> 03:15:45.547
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Slash O. Ah, without a space.

03:15:52.667 --> 03:15:53.768
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, God.

03:15:54.424 --> 03:15:54.764
[JC (Ryo)]: Dice.

03:15:56.342 --> 03:15:56.982
[Riv (DM)]: Oh shit.

03:15:58.783 --> 03:16:17.750
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah. That is a cooked bird. That is a barbecue. You just see it squawch. Like it's trying to like push back up against the hand. It's flapping its wings desperately. Its feet first go onto the lava. You can just smell the burning as they're starting to crust over and catch flames. And when just a couple of seconds, the whole thing is pushed under the lava and you just...

03:16:18.751 --> 03:16:23.777
[Riv (DM)]: The last thing you hear is a screeching as its beak goes under. And the whole thing disappears.

03:16:25.458 --> 03:16:29.480
[Riv (DM)]: And we will actually delete it from the whole map.

03:16:25.772 --> 03:16:26.292
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Glorious.

03:16:30.544 --> 03:16:32.345
[JC (Ryo)]: Let me guess, it had like two hit points left.

03:16:32.917 --> 03:16:33.397
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah.

03:16:33.342 --> 03:16:33.963
[Riv (DM)]: I had decent.

03:16:35.378 --> 03:16:36.059
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, decent.

03:16:36.444 --> 03:16:37.225
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that was decent.

03:16:36.519 --> 03:16:36.799
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah.

03:16:39.266 --> 03:16:44.389
[Riv (DM)]: All right, Donda, you've still got some movement in a bonus action. And I believe the hand sticks around, right, as you're concentrating on it?

03:16:44.945 --> 03:16:45.645
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: It should.

03:16:46.215 --> 03:16:47.336
[JC (Ryo)]: It says it's a minute, so...

03:16:47.751 --> 03:16:48.872
[Riv (DM)]: Yep. Beautiful.

03:16:49.628 --> 03:16:50.408
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: My next turn.

03:16:51.592 --> 03:17:04.361
[Riv (DM)]: I'll give you like a little token for it.

03:16:56.200 --> 03:17:03.302
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I think I can do anything else as a bonus action. I want to raise my shield, but that's a reaction, right?

03:17:04.401 --> 03:17:07.703
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Don't raise a shield until you get attacked would probably be the play.

03:17:07.644 --> 03:17:08.424
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, yeah.

03:17:08.924 --> 03:17:09.404
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful.

03:17:10.565 --> 03:17:18.271
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, I'm just trying to find a thing. What I might do, I might stick this here for you. This is your hand.

03:17:19.948 --> 03:17:20.248
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay.

03:17:22.404 --> 03:17:30.688
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's huge.

03:17:22.513 --> 03:17:23.813
[Riv (DM)]: I'll have to make it...

03:17:25.214 --> 03:17:26.874
[Riv (DM)]: I'll make you a real hand one of these days.

03:17:28.155 --> 03:17:28.935
[Riv (DM)]: There you go, there's your hand.

03:17:28.404 --> 03:17:29.065
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I'm just gonna.

03:17:31.456 --> 03:17:35.638
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, the hand is real big. I think it is actually a huge size, right?

03:17:34.814 --> 03:17:36.697
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, it is very big.

03:17:35.798 --> 03:17:46.182
[Riv (DM)]: Large hand. Yeah, so it's actually a little bit smaller than that, but yeah.

03:17:40.222 --> 03:17:41.484
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I'm just gonna back up a bit.

03:17:43.264 --> 03:17:43.764
[Vael (Berberis)]: Legends.

03:17:43.727 --> 03:17:44.328
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Just in case.

03:17:44.144 --> 03:17:45.145
[Vael (Berberis)]: Two good.

03:17:47.723 --> 03:17:50.364
[Riv (DM)]: Which is actually... Yeah, close enough. Anyway, you're all good.

03:17:49.895 --> 03:17:51.576
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, it's close. It's close enough. Yeah.

03:17:52.020 --> 03:18:00.788
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, all right, beautiful. There's Dondar's turn. And it takes us to Ryo, who is stunned, unfortunately. Didn't you get stunned in the last one?

03:17:57.520 --> 03:17:58.020
[JC (Ryo)]: I'm not.

03:18:01.541 --> 03:18:03.402
[JC (Ryo)]: No, I survived.

03:18:01.732 --> 03:18:01.932
[Vael (Berberis)]: Nope.

03:18:03.482 --> 03:18:04.322
[JC (Ryo)]: I rolled 18.

03:18:03.530 --> 03:18:07.173
[Riv (DM)]: Ah, were you the one that swiped? Beautiful, all right, cool. You go for it.

03:18:07.623 --> 03:18:10.804
[JC (Ryo)]: Can I see the big bird from where I am, or do I need to move a bit?

03:18:13.879 --> 03:18:19.164
[Riv (DM)]: Probably want to back up, yeah. I'll give you half cover if you show up from there.

03:18:22.428 --> 03:18:23.828
[JC (Ryo)]: I mean, sharpshooter, but yeah, okay.

03:18:24.364 --> 03:18:25.024
[Riv (DM)]: That's true, yeah.

03:18:26.165 --> 03:18:29.126
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, actually, you know what? With Sharpshooter, you could easily hit it from there.

03:18:31.752 --> 03:18:37.694
[JC (Ryo)]: How banged up is the... How banged... Yeah, I'm trying to ping.

03:18:35.248 --> 03:18:36.429
[Riv (DM)]: Sorry, you cut out. Can you go again?

03:18:37.734 --> 03:18:40.255
[JC (Ryo)]: It's not pinging. How banged up is the big orange thing?

03:18:40.690 --> 03:18:45.132
[Riv (DM)]: This guy? Yeah, he's taken a few hits. I wouldn't describe him as bloodied.

03:18:49.278 --> 03:18:53.039
[JC (Ryo)]: What the hell? It's hold left... What am I... Oh, I'm on the draw tools. That's why.

03:18:54.200 --> 03:18:54.980
[JC (Ryo)]: There we go, pinging.

03:18:57.128 --> 03:19:05.136
[JC (Ryo)]: All right, bonus action move Hunter's Mark from the barbecue chicken to the new bird.

03:19:05.572 --> 03:19:05.792
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

03:19:06.937 --> 03:19:09.500
[JC (Ryo)]: And thanks to Sharpshooter and everything, I don't have to stress too much.

03:19:11.221 --> 03:19:12.622
[JC (Ryo)]: So, bow.

03:19:13.799 --> 03:19:14.079
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

03:19:15.305 --> 03:19:18.408
[JC (Ryo)]: And away. That one misses.

03:19:26.172 --> 03:19:26.912
[JC (Ryo)]: 13 any good?

03:19:27.808 --> 03:19:29.369
[Riv (DM)]: Thirteen is no good, unfortunately.

03:19:30.794 --> 03:19:41.699
[JC (Ryo)]: Now I'm just going to curse and move. I've got 45 foot, so I don't want to bunch up too much. I'm going to go there.

03:19:42.459 --> 03:19:47.742
[Riv (DM)]: Damn, I can start throwing some big shit at you guys. You rolled a thirteen on a two. Sheesh.

03:19:48.202 --> 03:19:49.702
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, I have plus nine.

03:19:49.768 --> 03:19:52.095
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Oh no no no no, don't pay attention to that riff.

03:19:52.608 --> 03:19:53.495
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Ha ha ha ha!

03:19:53.273 --> 03:19:56.156
[Lacer (Kai)]: Please ignore.

03:19:55.424 --> 03:19:59.568
[JC (Ryo)]: I have plus 11, Ruv. I've had plus 11s for like, you know, five levels now.

03:19:56.972 --> 03:19:57.972
[Riv (DM)]: Plus 11. Yeah.

03:20:00.113 --> 03:20:01.674
[Riv (DM)]: All right. Very nice.

03:20:02.050 --> 03:20:04.092
[JC (Ryo)]: You gave me the braces. Don't get too upset.

03:20:07.016 --> 03:20:09.597
[Riv (DM)]: Kai, make a constitution saving throw, please.

03:20:11.758 --> 03:20:13.019
[Riv (DM)]: I'll put it in the chat for you if you like.

03:20:13.515 --> 03:20:14.316
[Lacer (Kai)]: I've got it, I've got it.

03:20:24.384 --> 03:20:24.624
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

03:20:26.772 --> 03:20:27.453
[Lacer (Kai)]: Am I stunned?

03:20:26.996 --> 03:20:31.358
[Riv (DM)]: You succeed? No, it's poison. You're actually already poisoned, so it doesn't matter.

03:20:32.959 --> 03:20:33.379
[Riv (DM)]: All good.

03:20:33.198 --> 03:20:37.742
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, do I- is the roll with disadvantage? Is poison fucked up my constitution roll?

03:20:38.861 --> 03:20:41.562
[Riv (DM)]: I don't think poison affects your saving throws.

03:20:38.924 --> 03:20:39.284
[Lacer (Kai)]: Stunned?

03:20:42.449 --> 03:20:43.281
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Ah, but the stand?

03:20:44.663 --> 03:20:45.344
[Riv (DM)]: Are you stunned right now?

03:20:45.770 --> 03:20:46.451
