[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, I'm stunned.

03:20:46.044 --> 03:20:46.704
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, you are stunned.

03:20:46.422 --> 03:20:46.633
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yes.

03:20:47.572 --> 03:20:47.752
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

03:20:48.925 --> 03:20:55.428
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, you will automatically fail dex and strength saving throws, but everything else is fine.

03:20:56.424 --> 03:20:57.671
[Lacer (Kai)]: Okay, so it's okay.

03:20:58.938 --> 03:20:59.160
[Lacer (Kai)]: Okay.

03:21:03.660 --> 03:21:11.628
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Uh, yeah, sorry. Yeah, you're stunned. So we skipped your turn. Sorry about that. I was, um, being brain dumb. All right, Erundor, over to you, dude.

03:21:14.772 --> 03:21:15.252
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Alrighty.

03:21:24.655 --> 03:21:35.118
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Not really any super easy area to get to anything. I can get to there.

03:21:36.118 --> 03:21:41.380
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: This guy is still up moderately high with him just flying in, or has he had to come down

03:21:40.861 --> 03:21:44.124
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, he'll be probably 15 feet up off the ground.

03:21:46.933 --> 03:21:56.819
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: In that case, I'm gonna get to there and take a swing at that, guys.

03:21:57.599 --> 03:21:57.860
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

03:22:15.245 --> 03:22:16.586
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, 13 hits.

03:22:15.933 --> 03:22:16.962
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Hezlo Rolls.

03:22:17.666 --> 03:22:19.288
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, no, you're good though, 13's good.

03:22:20.160 --> 03:22:34.507
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Wonderful. And because you are stunned, I don't get the ability to sneak attack off that.

03:22:36.640 --> 03:22:41.184
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

03:22:36.928 --> 03:22:40.170
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: So I need to go with the dagger as well then.

03:22:41.284 --> 03:22:45.756
[Riv (DM)]: But. You're all good because the first slice kills the last of the swarm.

03:22:43.211 --> 03:22:44.252
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: All good. Wonderful.

03:22:50.240 --> 03:23:08.517
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Cool. So, with this guy being up as far as he is, if I moved to there with a bonus action dash, would that be opportunity attack?

03:22:55.646 --> 03:22:55.906
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

03:23:04.796 --> 03:23:04.976
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

03:23:08.857 --> 03:23:11.840
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Or is it because it's high up enough?

03:23:13.032 --> 03:23:16.874
[Riv (DM)]: Yes, range is 15 feet away from you at the moment, so you're good. Unless it's got reach.

03:23:15.944 --> 03:23:16.224
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Okay.

03:23:20.356 --> 03:23:21.637
[Riv (DM)]: Which you don't know if he does or not.

03:23:22.516 --> 03:23:22.776
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah.

03:23:27.160 --> 03:23:27.360
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Well.

03:23:29.462 --> 03:23:29.782
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Fuck it.

03:23:32.104 --> 03:23:34.206
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I will bonus action dash.

03:23:34.984 --> 03:23:35.304
[Riv (DM)]: Ah, nice.

03:23:40.271 --> 03:23:41.612
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: And that'll be it.

03:23:44.192 --> 03:23:55.756
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. Sweet. So, yeah. And you wouldn't be able to attack with your dagger because it needs to be of the same attack. Beautiful. Yeah, that does it. Man, this giant like adventuring silhouette lady is just sending me for a trip, eh? Oh, man.

03:23:55.607 --> 03:23:56.662
[Lacer (Kai)]: Avernus, Avernus.

03:23:57.936 --> 03:24:01.297
[Riv (DM)]: All right. Cool. All right.

03:24:02.818 --> 03:24:03.198
[Riv (DM)]: This guy.

03:24:07.759 --> 03:24:10.200
[Riv (DM)]: You see he like sort of crouches down.

03:24:12.044 --> 03:24:17.649
[Riv (DM)]: It's sort of, he's tensing up and he's gonna jump through the air and land right behind both of you.

03:24:19.570 --> 03:24:35.284
[Riv (DM)]: And that's just start of your turn. Yeah, very nice. All right, cool. And he's just gonna swing on our good girl here. Ooh, we don't love the look of that.

03:24:35.973 --> 03:24:36.997
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, shit.

03:24:41.356 --> 03:24:43.337
[Riv (DM)]: All right. And one more.

03:24:48.359 --> 03:25:06.387
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. First attack comes in. Surprised by the surprising strength and dexterity, gets an easy slash straight into a side. It seems to pass through the plates and gets into maybe where her kidney is or something like that. And you hear them shriek out in pain. The twenty one comes in and.

03:25:08.640 --> 03:25:33.868
[Riv (DM)]: You see as she gets caught by the first critical attack she just quickly almost instinctively turns with her longs with a greatsword whips around and is able to deflect the next attack using her parry and the last attack goes wide um all right here's the critical hit all right

03:25:47.940 --> 03:26:02.231
[Riv (DM)]: Shit, son. Okay. You see she's like, she screeches and she almost drops to one knee and hand goes to her side. She's looking real bad, guys.

03:26:04.073 --> 03:26:12.785
[Riv (DM)]: And you see the creature's starting to smile and laugh. As we actually go to her turn, she gets up and.

03:26:15.186 --> 03:26:20.488
[Riv (DM)]: Looking in front of her, she sees a pack of weak creatures, she's just going to take a swing at them.

03:26:27.049 --> 03:26:32.291
[Riv (DM)]: With one easy hit and after neutral damage, she will dispatch the last of these this pack here.

03:26:33.771 --> 03:26:36.132
[Riv (DM)]: And then she will turn and make her next tack on.

03:26:37.632 --> 03:26:38.272
[Riv (DM)]: The hezrou.

03:26:40.014 --> 03:26:43.456
[Riv (DM)]: She finally gets a hit. Beautiful. So we do damage on him.

03:26:49.140 --> 03:26:50.781
[Riv (DM)]: Gosh, he can do so much damage, dude.

03:26:52.182 --> 03:26:55.165
[Riv (DM)]: And I think she's actually going to try and go with the rope again.

03:26:57.586 --> 03:26:59.748
[Riv (DM)]: And he will make his saving throw.

03:27:05.844 --> 03:27:08.486
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, she succeeds again! Alright.

03:27:10.028 --> 03:27:16.433
[Riv (DM)]: See, she's like, bare. Well, grit her teeth. Or you hear at least some growling from behind the helmet.

03:27:17.894 --> 03:27:26.502
[Riv (DM)]: Yes, I don't think she's going to move away. It's possibly too risky for her right now. So, she will stay. She's standing for a last stand.

03:27:27.883 --> 03:27:29.024
[Riv (DM)]: And we go to Berberis.

03:27:32.604 --> 03:27:35.705
[Vael (Berberis)]: Guys around me are down. Trust my friends are dealing with the other bird.

03:27:37.866 --> 03:27:41.828
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'm going to swing at this guy with my shillelagh that I've just empowered.

03:27:43.193 --> 03:27:43.376
[Riv (DM)]: Mmmm.

03:27:44.409 --> 03:27:47.410
[Vael (Berberis)]: Burn is actually going to burn a zephyr strike through it.

03:27:56.234 --> 03:27:57.994
[Vael (Berberis)]: I won't roll the damage on that just yet.

03:27:59.495 --> 03:28:02.036
[Vael (Berberis)]: But this is first attack at advantage because of the zephyr strike.

03:28:12.396 --> 03:28:15.341
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Yep, easy peasy. Oh yeah, you've got plus two on this eye. Sheesh.

03:28:17.144 --> 03:28:17.385
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

03:28:18.959 --> 03:28:20.621
[Vael (Berberis)]: The Shillelagh damage.

03:28:24.303 --> 03:28:24.804
[Vael (Berberis)]: Steam.

03:28:25.619 --> 03:28:25.900
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

03:28:26.125 --> 03:28:26.945
[Vael (Berberis)]: This is Epistrike.

03:28:32.930 --> 03:28:37.893
[Vael (Berberis)]: Um, topple attack, so it needs to do a... strength? Ooh, is it? Um...

03:28:43.393 --> 03:28:46.135
[Vael (Berberis)]: DC 19 con save for topple, apparently.

03:28:49.132 --> 03:28:51.193
[Riv (DM)]: But it's like you whack it on its head and it gets dazed or something.

03:28:52.000 --> 03:28:53.781
[Vael (Berberis)]: Oh yeah, sweeping leg. Well, that saves it.

03:28:54.374 --> 03:28:54.574
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

03:28:55.482 --> 03:28:57.924
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'll follow it up with a second attack then.

03:28:59.045 --> 03:28:59.746
[Vael (Berberis)]: It's just normal.

03:29:08.052 --> 03:29:12.276
[Vael (Berberis)]: Big damage.

03:29:08.118 --> 03:29:09.218
[Riv (DM)]: Nice, yep, good roll.

03:29:14.760 --> 03:29:15.040
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

03:29:16.132 --> 03:29:25.896
[Vael (Berberis)]: And it is injured, so Hunter's... Where is it? Hunter's Prey. Extra d8 damage.

03:29:30.458 --> 03:29:30.958
[Vael (Berberis)]: Let me take it.

03:29:30.716 --> 03:29:38.668
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. We're good. Yeah, we do take those ones. Beautiful. Yeah, it's taking the beating now. You see it's starting to get worn down. It's now bloodied.

03:29:40.482 --> 03:29:44.224
[Vael (Berberis)]: Oh, yep, so that's... Bonus attack.

03:29:45.568 --> 03:29:46.028
[Vael (Berberis)]: Movement.

03:29:48.069 --> 03:29:54.631
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'll stand here, shoulder to shoulder. I don't believe I've got any free action.

03:29:56.812 --> 03:29:58.533
[Vael (Berberis)]: I looked at this last time. It's been a long time.

03:30:03.194 --> 03:30:05.515
[Vael (Berberis)]: Not everything's action. So yeah, I'm just going to hold the line.

03:30:06.956 --> 03:30:07.996
[Vael (Berberis)]: Try and draw a bit of attention.

03:30:07.232 --> 03:30:07.573
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:30:08.674 --> 03:30:08.915
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

03:30:17.949 --> 03:30:20.473
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. It takes us to the bird.

03:30:38.572 --> 03:30:39.172
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm.

03:30:41.714 --> 03:30:43.695
[Riv (DM)]: Okay. Cool. It's gonna fly over here.

03:30:46.096 --> 03:30:48.177
[Riv (DM)]: I'm Dondar. Make a... Hmm?

03:30:47.696 --> 03:30:48.317
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Bonus action?

03:30:49.138 --> 03:30:50.859
[Riv (DM)]: Hmm? Session one.

03:30:50.698 --> 03:30:51.018
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Sorry.

03:30:53.019 --> 03:31:06.925
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: No. I don't like this.

03:30:58.062 --> 03:31:00.984
[Riv (DM)]: Make a strength saving throw.

03:31:12.208 --> 03:31:12.528
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Come here.

03:31:20.940 --> 03:31:21.480
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Too late.

03:31:21.216 --> 03:31:32.788
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah.

03:31:26.928 --> 03:31:28.835
[Vael (Berberis)]: The bird flies away with the dwarf and he's never seen it again.

03:31:32.084 --> 03:31:33.185
[JC (Ryo)]: And the party rejoices.

03:31:38.173 --> 03:31:39.755
[Riv (DM)]: Sorry, give me one moment please.

03:31:42.167 --> 03:31:44.488
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You're still within range and you're grouped.

03:31:51.244 --> 03:31:51.564
[JC (Ryo)]: Do it.

03:32:04.020 --> 03:32:29.892
[Riv (DM)]: Okay cool sorry about that yeah I was just looking at the um there were new uh rules for grapple in 2024 I was just making sure um cool all right unfortunately not bonus actions and reactions are different things um the creature comes in big claws come around you and it just starts flying up into the sky um it will it will have enough

03:32:05.925 --> 03:32:15.084
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I can't cast a bonus action as a reaction, can I?

03:32:30.748 --> 03:32:38.410
[Riv (DM)]: room to go 10 feet up into the air as it only moved 30 in its first round. So Donda, you are caught in its claws and currently 10 feet up in the air.

03:32:39.731 --> 03:32:40.791
[Riv (DM)]: Now into the bird's turn.

03:32:39.840 --> 03:32:43.267
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Good thing Donda knows how to fly, as long as Donda has flight prepared.

03:32:46.732 --> 03:32:48.695
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, I could, but I'm still grappled, so.

03:32:49.078 --> 03:32:50.040
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Oh yes, for now.

03:32:51.663 --> 03:32:51.984
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Oh no.

03:32:53.024 --> 03:32:55.150
[Lacer (Kai)]: Catch himself with his big B's hand.

03:32:55.865 --> 03:33:01.133
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Sorry.

03:32:58.079 --> 03:32:58.540
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, what the f-

03:32:58.276 --> 03:32:58.616
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:33:02.695 --> 03:33:03.336
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Can I still?

03:33:03.244 --> 03:33:28.342
[Riv (DM)]: I think it's not going to be able to reach the screech, so it's probably not going to use the screech this round. What it will do is... Oh, is it going to dash or is it going to... Yeah, I think it's just going to use this.

03:33:30.944 --> 03:33:31.484
[Riv (DM)]: So that will...

03:33:32.672 --> 03:33:34.212
[Riv (DM)]: Give it an emanation like that around it.

03:33:36.273 --> 03:33:42.595
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so you guys are like just outside. So this turn you won't be affected by it, but on subsequent turns you may be.

03:33:43.756 --> 03:33:45.437
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Holy shit, how many of these things are there?

03:33:46.696 --> 03:33:51.057
[Riv (DM)]: You are on the blood war right now.

03:33:47.072 --> 03:33:47.678
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Least another.

03:33:49.459 --> 03:33:49.919
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Jesus.

03:33:49.612 --> 03:33:50.211
[Lacer (Kai)]: There's another two.

03:33:50.256 --> 03:33:54.034
[Vael (Berberis)]: As many as it takes to make the fight look like it's hard.

03:33:56.399 --> 03:34:00.160
[Riv (DM)]: Donda, you are being held by a giant bird.

03:34:02.284 --> 03:34:05.586
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Nice.

03:34:10.188 --> 03:34:10.488
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well.

03:34:12.524 --> 03:34:16.928
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Do I am I in what is being grappled do to me?

03:34:17.832 --> 03:34:29.061
[Riv (DM)]: Absolutely nothing. It just means that you can't move. So you can end a grapple by making a strength check to try and break its escape DC, or you can use any other ability.

03:34:27.637 --> 03:34:27.897
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Right.

03:34:31.240 --> 03:34:33.521
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: So if I missed a step, am I still grappled?

03:34:34.005 --> 03:34:35.106
[Riv (DM)]: Nope, you can miss a step away.

03:34:40.247 --> 03:34:54.309
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: But in other words, The bird is protecting me while it's not hurting me.

03:34:46.075 --> 03:34:47.376
[Riv (DM)]: Well, who knows?

03:34:47.936 --> 03:34:48.618
[Lacer (Kai)]: You could say that.

03:34:48.204 --> 03:34:50.666
[Riv (DM)]: For right now, that's just got a hold of you.

03:34:54.970 --> 03:34:56.370
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, I'm going to misty step away.

03:34:57.332 --> 03:34:57.532
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

03:34:58.811 --> 03:35:02.013
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, yeah, that's probably the best.

03:35:07.915 --> 03:35:09.396
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Fuck, where did all the panels go?

03:35:12.032 --> 03:35:15.713
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I've got a massive screen until we play this and now everything's tiny.

03:35:23.276 --> 03:35:25.998
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: All right, I'm going to bonus Misty Step.

03:35:28.298 --> 03:35:29.179
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: If I can find it.

03:35:31.400 --> 03:35:32.080
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Oh, all right.

03:35:40.288 --> 03:35:43.249
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: And I can't select myself, but I have Misty stepped away.

03:35:45.760 --> 03:35:46.567
[Vael (Berberis)]: This round was a bird.

03:35:45.856 --> 03:35:55.021
[Riv (DM)]: All good. Yeah, no worries. So... Yeah, that's fine.

03:35:47.870 --> 03:35:54.872
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I'm under the bird, so I can't select myself to move yet.

03:35:55.061 --> 03:35:55.762
[Riv (DM)]: Was it like over here?

03:35:57.813 --> 03:36:02.694
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, it will be there around there, yeah.

03:36:05.788 --> 03:36:09.010
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Mr. Sepper's life, I think 30 feet or something, right?

03:36:09.088 --> 03:36:09.948
[Lacer (Kai)]: He has 30 feet.

03:36:09.436 --> 03:36:09.636
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah.

03:36:10.611 --> 03:36:12.632
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that's probably fine. Yeah, easy as.

03:36:14.056 --> 03:36:26.720
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Uh, and I will actually... No, I can't find that.

03:36:14.272 --> 03:36:17.251
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, given the bird was 10 feet up and the lich was 10 feet up.

03:36:18.271 --> 03:36:19.311
[Lacer (Kai)]: Now he's on top of the ridge.

03:36:21.868 --> 03:36:23.334
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, standing very precariously on the edge.

03:36:27.920 --> 03:36:28.160
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Um...

03:36:28.453 --> 03:36:31.734
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, you can be there if you want to be there.

03:36:32.321 --> 03:36:33.782
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I don't want to be on the edge of anything.

03:36:34.882 --> 03:36:38.243
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Why? Is that ridge ten feet up?

03:36:37.456 --> 03:36:37.656
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

03:36:38.390 --> 03:36:38.660
[JC (Ryo)]: Yes.

03:36:39.903 --> 03:36:40.164
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: What?

03:36:41.364 --> 03:36:41.644
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay.

03:36:48.254 --> 03:37:00.864
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: No, so I'm going to move my big hand over to the creature and punch it.

03:36:55.649 --> 03:37:05.268
[Riv (DM)]: Just double check. I believe moving Bixby hand is a bonus action. Yeah, it absolutely is.

03:37:01.305 --> 03:37:02.145
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, that's debatable.

03:37:05.388 --> 03:37:06.110
[Riv (DM)]: Why don't we check it?

03:37:06.969 --> 03:37:10.232
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: When you cast the... Ah, goddammit.

03:37:09.576 --> 03:37:11.401
[JC (Ryo)]: As a bonus action on later turns.

03:37:13.267 --> 03:37:16.316
[JC (Ryo)]: So if your bonus action was the mystic step, no, you can't move it, man.

03:37:19.104 --> 03:37:22.885
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Well, isn't that just annoying? All right, fireball level three.

03:37:25.086 --> 03:37:25.826
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Is that level three?

03:37:28.250 --> 03:37:29.703
[Lacer (Kai)]: Or higher if you want to go higher.

03:37:34.028 --> 03:37:35.989
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Actually, where are we? We're in the hells.

03:37:37.089 --> 03:37:39.429
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: It's probably invulnerable to fire, why not?

03:37:42.650 --> 03:37:47.152
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I mean, the other one wasn't to lava, so there's that, so maybe not.

03:37:49.164 --> 03:37:54.847
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay, then, yeah, five all at the fourth level, please.

03:38:04.040 --> 03:38:04.480
[Riv (DM)]: All righty.

03:38:04.771 --> 03:38:14.416
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: And let me just try not to five all. Whatever, it's on him unless someone's in range, but the template disappeared, so I can't tell.

03:38:14.865 --> 03:38:18.146
[Riv (DM)]: That's fine. Yeah, no, all good. We can easily hit him. Cool. Roll the damage.

03:38:21.484 --> 03:38:23.505
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Just gonna scroll down to everything.

03:38:30.871 --> 03:38:31.151
[Riv (DM)]: Sweet.

03:38:36.971 --> 03:38:38.412
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Spell Sniper, baby!

03:38:40.155 --> 03:38:52.025
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so the fireball goes out and blasts over it. And it does seem like fires don't quite catch as it would normally would on a normal bird. Seems like it possibly has a bit of resistance to it.

03:38:55.816 --> 03:38:57.078
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: So it doesn't take any damage.

03:38:57.930 --> 03:39:00.612
[Riv (DM)]: Takes a bit. Yeah, definitely takes a little bit. So it's not immune.

03:39:00.342 --> 03:39:00.603
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Okay.

03:39:02.686 --> 03:39:03.166
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Interesting.

03:39:05.576 --> 03:39:14.636
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right, you've got any movement or is that you? All right, we go to Rio.

03:39:11.778 --> 03:39:12.920
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I mean, I think that's probably me.

03:39:16.468 --> 03:39:21.009
[JC (Ryo)]: Alright, well he's already got Hunter's Mark on him, so I'm going to shoot twice into him.

03:39:22.510 --> 03:39:31.672
[JC (Ryo)]: And I also want to bonus action Hail of Thorns if I hit. Wait, can I do it? As you hit the creature, yes.

03:39:41.975 --> 03:39:43.516
[JC (Ryo)]: Alright, I'm assuming that hits.

03:39:44.516 --> 03:39:44.916
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah.

03:39:46.988 --> 03:40:07.232
[JC (Ryo)]: So bow, hunter's mark, bonus action. I need a DC 14 dex please.

03:40:09.868 --> 03:40:10.548
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah, yes.

03:40:18.396 --> 03:40:18.856
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

03:40:19.756 --> 03:40:25.548
[JC (Ryo)]: Okay, and I'm assuming it's definitely still there, because no one's really hit it yet, so second attack.

03:40:25.202 --> 03:40:28.885
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah, it's the first time it's been damaged other than the fire on the previous round.

03:40:31.768 --> 03:40:32.909
[Riv (DM)]: Nice, yep, 22's good.

03:40:41.937 --> 03:40:43.218
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right.

03:40:44.479 --> 03:40:45.000
[Riv (DM)]: Nice, number 13.

03:40:46.376 --> 03:40:51.359
[Riv (DM)]: Just double checking Halo Thorns. Does it have any like, I feel like it has like a restrain or something, right? Or was, am I imagining that?

03:40:52.544 --> 03:40:53.144
[JC (Ryo)]: No?

03:40:54.581 --> 03:40:55.161
[Riv (DM)]: Just more damage.

03:40:55.165 --> 03:40:58.627
[JC (Ryo)]: It hits the target and anything within five feet of it.

03:40:55.181 --> 03:40:55.641
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, okay.

03:40:59.163 --> 03:41:00.384
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, get blown up. Nice.

03:41:00.168 --> 03:41:15.456
[JC (Ryo)]: So if it was all bunched up, I could hit multiple, but... Yeah, but I'm assuming I can't really squeeze through this to spread out a bit.

03:41:03.606 --> 03:41:07.648
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, sweet. All right, cool. We go top of the round, unless you've got movement.

03:41:16.064 --> 03:41:32.840
[Riv (DM)]: You can try.

03:41:19.980 --> 03:41:20.710
[Lacer (Kai)]: Your cartwheel.

03:41:23.264 --> 03:41:23.817
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: The Flipper.

03:41:25.716 --> 03:41:30.979
[JC (Ryo)]: You know what? I'm actually going to move closer to it just to spread out so that way we don't all get hit by a mass stun again.

03:41:32.960 --> 03:41:37.445
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool.

03:41:35.282 --> 03:41:35.702
[JC (Ryo)]: Stay there.

03:41:37.465 --> 03:41:40.688
[Riv (DM)]: It was just going to be a dick save. See if you could squeeze on past that lava pool.

03:41:41.305 --> 03:41:42.906
[JC (Ryo)]: I've got 45. That's 15.

03:41:47.084 --> 03:41:48.685
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Really beautiful.

03:41:50.987 --> 03:42:05.296
[Riv (DM)]: Cool, top of the round we go to Kai. So yeah, you've seen this flame-winged creature beside you take an absolute beating, and just looking over to the side now as you sort of shake your head and you come out of the daze, you see that it appears to be very much on its last legs.

03:42:09.728 --> 03:42:20.114
[Lacer (Kai)]: Alright. How badly damaged is this stinky demon thing that's poisoned me?

03:42:18.024 --> 03:42:31.516
[Riv (DM)]: yeah it's it's yeah it doesn't seem like it's on death's door it's definitely bloody though it's taking a good chunk of damage nope you're not anymore top of the round you're you come out

03:42:26.577 --> 03:42:27.578
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm stunned at the moment.

03:42:29.659 --> 03:42:30.159
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, okay.

03:42:32.440 --> 03:42:33.341
[Lacer (Kai)]: Right, okay.

03:42:34.782 --> 03:42:37.863
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess I better do some healing. I really should do some healing.

03:42:39.556 --> 03:42:50.243
[Lacer (Kai)]: I will, guess I will, bonus action, I will go back since I lost my, um, my chalice mode, I'll have to do it again.

03:42:53.426 --> 03:42:59.350
[Lacer (Kai)]: And then I will cast a spell. Let's see what is the best spell I can cast.

03:43:04.213 --> 03:43:04.673
[Lacer (Kai)]: How is this?

03:43:06.414 --> 03:43:13.632
[Lacer (Kai)]: Alright, I'm gonna cast Aura of Vitality. At the double.

03:43:19.316 --> 03:43:23.700
[Lacer (Kai)]: Let's see. I guess. Yeah. Okay.

03:43:22.035 --> 03:43:23.476
[Riv (DM)]: All right, so 30 foot.

03:43:23.760 --> 03:43:24.540
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's an AOE.

03:43:24.856 --> 03:43:25.016
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

03:43:24.941 --> 03:43:25.241
[Lacer (Kai)]: Okay.

03:43:25.476 --> 03:43:27.818
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. And concentration.

03:43:27.203 --> 03:43:28.824
[Lacer (Kai)]: This is concentration.

03:43:27.918 --> 03:43:28.818
[Riv (DM)]: So that will.

03:43:30.325 --> 03:43:30.665
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

03:43:30.339 --> 03:43:33.120
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah, your forms are not concentration, are they? Cool. Good.

03:43:33.207 --> 03:43:44.763
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, it's not. So I guess on the first hit I can order because The... what's it called? The healing for Starry Form only does it on the first tick.

03:43:46.244 --> 03:43:46.924
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm pretty sure.

03:43:46.625 --> 03:43:46.815
[Riv (DM)]: Right.

03:43:46.944 --> 03:44:01.052
[Lacer (Kai)]: Because it says whenever you cast a spell, that heals. So the subsequent heal ticks don't count with the chalice mode since it's not casting a spell, it's the same spell on concentration.

03:44:04.394 --> 03:44:08.476
[Lacer (Kai)]: So I'm gonna pump 10 healing plus

03:44:10.180 --> 03:44:14.942
[Lacer (Kai)]: Seven into our demon girl here.

03:44:18.028 --> 03:44:18.969
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: I hope this doesn't hurt her.

03:44:20.036 --> 03:44:22.519
[Riv (DM)]: I feel like chalice would trigger here. Do you think it wouldn't?

03:44:23.247 --> 03:44:26.989
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, I mean on subsequent turns.

03:44:26.584 --> 03:44:27.885
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, I see what you mean.

03:44:27.369 --> 03:44:29.010
[Lacer (Kai)]: Chalice on sub- that's what I'm saying.

03:44:27.905 --> 03:44:29.647
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, yeah, yeah, yeah.

03:44:29.210 --> 03:44:33.252
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, only on the- Yes, yes.

03:44:29.667 --> 03:44:31.950
[Riv (DM)]: So when you burn a spell slot is when it triggers.

03:44:33.352 --> 03:44:38.357
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. Okay, nice. Roll a wisdom check.

03:44:40.139 --> 03:44:40.660
[Riv (DM)]: Just gotta be a 10.

03:44:42.240 --> 03:44:42.460
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yep.

03:44:46.966 --> 03:44:54.856
[Lacer (Kai)]: Is it? OK. Oh, no.

03:44:56.918 --> 03:44:57.819
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm so sorry.

03:44:59.600 --> 03:45:12.444
[Riv (DM)]: Again, the lunar light coagulates around the condensers? I don't know. Condensers, I think, is the word I was looking for.

03:45:12.916 --> 03:45:13.240
[Lacer (Kai)]: Maybe...

03:45:13.024 --> 03:45:25.548
[Riv (DM)]: Around the creature. And this time you lose focus slightly and the creature is burned and once again screams out,

03:45:28.840 --> 03:45:36.205
[Riv (DM)]: In pain as the holy light that it is very much opposed to.

03:45:37.846 --> 03:45:58.300
[Riv (DM)]: Sasa tearing your skin. OK. Sweet, yep, and you can cower down there.

03:45:44.404 --> 03:45:51.111
[Lacer (Kai)]: I would like to move. I'm going to yell out, I'm so sorry. I'm trying to help. I'm going to move, like, here.

03:45:58.780 --> 03:45:59.100
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

03:46:06.043 --> 03:46:07.784
[Riv (DM)]: All right, that's your, is that your turn?

03:46:08.307 --> 03:46:09.047
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, it's my turn.

03:46:08.905 --> 03:46:09.605
[Riv (DM)]: Bonus action.

03:46:12.346 --> 03:46:13.427
[Riv (DM)]: All right, sweet. We go to Erendor then.

03:46:15.120 --> 03:46:21.282
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Alrighty. I'm going in. I'm going in swinging.

03:46:22.476 --> 03:46:23.129
[Lacer (Kai)]: You're gonna kill her.

03:46:24.903 --> 03:46:25.524
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Hopefully not.

03:46:29.885 --> 03:46:30.745
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Ember Blade.

03:46:34.188 --> 03:46:46.319
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. That's... Three charges.

03:46:35.827 --> 03:46:38.008
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Wonderful. I will burn three charges.

03:46:47.500 --> 03:46:47.761
[Riv (DM)]: Oof.

03:46:48.882 --> 03:46:49.342
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:46:51.604 --> 03:46:52.665
[Riv (DM)]: Oof. All right.

03:46:56.088 --> 03:46:56.509
[Riv (DM)]: Okay.

03:46:57.224 --> 03:47:18.693
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Then... Yep.

03:46:57.650 --> 03:47:00.012
[Riv (DM)]: Good. Huge amount of damage, just shrieks in pain.

03:47:03.724 --> 03:47:05.746
[Riv (DM)]: Dagger successfully hits.

03:47:07.547 --> 03:47:07.887
[Riv (DM)]: Hang on.

03:47:09.068 --> 03:47:10.009
[Riv (DM)]: Just before you.

03:47:15.353 --> 03:47:15.734
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, roll.

03:47:19.234 --> 03:47:21.175
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Damage is rolled and...

03:47:22.339 --> 03:47:23.740
[Riv (DM)]: I was at dagger 10 damage.

03:47:22.575 --> 03:47:24.416
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Sneak attack. Yep.

03:47:24.921 --> 03:47:25.442
[Riv (DM)]: Damn.

03:47:25.771 --> 03:47:26.502
[Lacer (Kai)]: Neek Attack.

03:47:33.088 --> 03:47:33.428
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

03:47:35.404 --> 03:47:36.765
[Riv (DM)]: Twenty-four damage on the dagger.

03:47:40.247 --> 03:47:45.330
[Riv (DM)]: I will reveal how much health the creature had as I typed in a secret chat message.

03:47:49.412 --> 03:47:59.318
[Riv (DM)]: You come in with the dagger and you finish it off just as it was getting ready to step forward and slash this demonic entity to pieces, or should we say devil to pieces.

03:48:01.099 --> 03:48:03.340
[Riv (DM)]: Tell us about how you kill it with your dagger, Erendor.

03:48:08.316 --> 03:48:17.912
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I mean, the sword has gone for its arm to begin with, and... you know, I'm not making that much progress with that. It's doing a chunk, but...

03:48:20.397 --> 03:48:22.240
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: I'm going for its head as much as I can.

03:48:26.231 --> 03:48:41.904
[Riv (DM)]: So rapier goes into the arm, sort of like pins it down to its side. It gives you an angle to get in with an opening. Dagger comes up, flip it straight up under the jaw, up into its head. And it just like slowly groans and falls forward, slumps and thuds on the ground.

03:48:43.445 --> 03:48:51.832
[Riv (DM)]: You see the erinyes look in your direction and just nod once to you before you hear the voice say, good.

03:48:52.940 --> 03:48:55.521
[Riv (DM)]: That one is dead. Now just these pests to deal with.

03:48:56.742 --> 03:48:58.723
[Riv (DM)]: And it is actually her turn.

03:49:00.564 --> 03:49:03.406
[Riv (DM)]: Amazing. So what she's actually going to do, she's going to get up.

03:49:05.807 --> 03:49:11.250
[Riv (DM)]: She is bleeding profusely. One of her wings is now almost entirely detached.

03:49:12.631 --> 03:49:21.075
[Riv (DM)]: The rope is coming out. Yeah, 120 feet. Damn, this ability is awesome. I just wish it would hit once, please.

03:49:23.412 --> 03:49:24.333
[Riv (DM)]: Sorry, let me reveal them.

03:49:26.875 --> 03:49:31.018
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. She's going to use it on this bird. Yeah.

03:49:32.199 --> 03:49:41.346
[Riv (DM)]: All right, we're doing this one.

03:49:44.889 --> 03:49:48.132
[Riv (DM)]: All right, a failure. So we do the damage on that.

03:49:54.053 --> 03:49:55.951
[JC (Ryo)]: Mr Ping, the northern one or the southern one?

03:49:56.752 --> 03:49:57.733
[Riv (DM)]: Ah, sorry, yeah, the southern one.

03:50:01.016 --> 03:50:10.867
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. So the rope comes out, wraps around her, and the creature is now restrained. You can see she's starting to, like, pull on it, and she will run over towards it.

03:50:13.790 --> 03:50:17.934
[Riv (DM)]: And I think I see it. I got 10 feet up into the air. Does she have reach?

03:50:20.860 --> 03:50:25.045
[Vael (Berberis)]: If it's restrained, it should fall from the sky.

03:50:24.800 --> 03:50:28.061
[Riv (DM)]: Mmm, that is true.

03:50:25.460 --> 03:50:28.503
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, the speed is zero. Does that mean it's falls? Yeah.

03:50:29.962 --> 03:50:33.264
[Riv (DM)]: That is true. Flying.

03:50:34.508 --> 03:50:53.090
[Lacer (Kai)]: It takes full damage. Yeah, so it gets... Yeah, it does.

03:50:36.850 --> 03:50:37.751
[JC (Ryo)]: Not from 10 foot.

03:50:37.500 --> 03:50:40.244
[Vael (Berberis)]: If they don't hover, their speed is reduced to zero, they fall.

03:50:42.227 --> 03:50:48.070
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, all your fly speed is reduced to zero. There you go. So the creature actually falls to the ground and takes a d6 of damage.

03:50:50.371 --> 03:50:51.411
[Riv (DM)]: Ah, a beautiful one.

03:50:50.422 --> 03:50:52.024
[JC (Ryo)]: It takes fall damage from 10 foot?

03:50:52.872 --> 03:50:53.072
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

03:50:53.351 --> 03:50:54.212
[Lacer (Kai)]: Anything above 10.

03:50:53.920 --> 03:50:54.940
[Riv (DM)]: D6 every 10.

03:50:55.553 --> 03:50:55.785
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah.

03:50:56.681 --> 03:51:08.186
[Riv (DM)]: All right, so she whips out the rope, grabs it, just as it's starting to take off. It feels like, it thinks it's still got Dundar on its claw, and it's about to take him away to some nasty place for a meal.

03:51:09.446 --> 03:51:23.372
[Riv (DM)]: But instead, a blue glowing rope shoots out, grabs it around the wings, lock them at its side, and it just plummets out of the sky. She runs forward, just on death's door, but eyes forward, brave, two swings.

03:51:24.332 --> 03:51:25.994
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's prone though now on the floor, right?

03:51:26.880 --> 03:51:34.003
[Riv (DM)]: Yes, so it would be advantage, correct.

03:51:30.540 --> 03:51:32.141
[JC (Ryo)]: Well, Restraint gives Advantage anyway.

03:51:34.143 --> 03:51:34.344
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

03:51:35.524 --> 03:51:38.165
[Riv (DM)]: I think that might be...

03:51:36.346 --> 03:51:38.328
[Lacer (Kai)]: Wait, is restrained like auto-crit?

03:51:36.644 --> 03:51:39.666
[JC (Ryo)]: Restraints, attack, techniques, huh?

03:51:38.265 --> 03:51:40.526
[Riv (DM)]: I don't think restrained gives.

03:51:40.366 --> 03:51:42.728
[JC (Ryo)]: Attacks, rolls against, against you have Advantage.

03:51:40.586 --> 03:51:59.539
[Riv (DM)]: I think it's just... I think it's just incapacitated. No, you're right, restrained does. There's so many very slightly similar overlapping things like grappled, restrained, incapacitated, stunned. They're all kind of similar. Maybe they should simplify them. Anyway, all good. Advantage on the first attack, 21 will hit and we'll do another advantage attack like this.

03:52:03.680 --> 03:52:13.805
[Riv (DM)]: Damn, two terrible rolls on that one. Just the first attack hits. 25, beautiful.

03:52:15.565 --> 03:52:21.728
[Riv (DM)]: And you hear a yell out, focus on killing one of them at a time.

03:52:22.876 --> 03:52:28.080
[Riv (DM)]: Spread out so that you aren't affected by their disgusting poisons or screeches.

03:52:29.160 --> 03:52:32.042
[Riv (DM)]: And that will end her turn. Go to Berberis.

03:52:33.524 --> 03:52:35.125
[Vael (Berberis)]: How weird is that one looking?

03:52:36.585 --> 03:52:37.086
[Riv (DM)]: How wounded?

03:52:37.507 --> 03:52:37.787
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah.

03:52:38.386 --> 03:52:40.388
[Riv (DM)]: It's pretty messed up now. Yeah, I would say it's about bloodied.

03:52:50.354 --> 03:52:56.358
[Vael (Berberis)]: I will use a magic action. I'll step a little bit over this way first.

03:52:57.618 --> 03:52:58.999
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'll pick a point within 60 feet of me.

03:53:01.376 --> 03:53:02.977
[Vael (Berberis)]: Do a 10-foot radius on that.

03:53:04.438 --> 03:53:20.331
[Vael (Berberis)]: And I will do... is it actually... is Landsade one or the other? No, it's both. So, picking Landsade, which I will save for the... No, that's fine.

03:53:17.612 --> 03:53:18.172
[Riv (DM)]: Should I be worried?

03:53:22.092 --> 03:53:26.996
[Vael (Berberis)]: So, it is a DC-17 con save for the bird.

03:53:30.688 --> 03:53:36.290
[Vael (Berberis)]: And there is also two d6 of healing that go out at the same, in the same area on one ally within range.

03:53:37.017 --> 03:53:39.418
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right, so con save for bird.

03:53:44.079 --> 03:53:44.359
[Riv (DM)]: Fail.

03:53:45.519 --> 03:53:47.040
[Riv (DM)]: So is that full damage, I assume?

03:53:48.348 --> 03:53:49.209
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, beautiful.

03:53:49.134 --> 03:53:49.794
[Vael (Berberis)]: Six of product.

03:53:49.389 --> 03:53:49.689
[Riv (DM)]: So...

03:53:51.614 --> 03:53:52.815
[Vael (Berberis)]: Not much, but something.

03:53:53.875 --> 03:53:56.156
[Vael (Berberis)]: But it will also heal for seven.

03:53:54.173 --> 03:54:02.321
[Riv (DM)]: Cause... Oh yeah. Oh yeah, so the three was necrotic damage and then seven healing?

03:54:02.816 --> 03:54:03.717
[Vael (Berberis)]: Seven healing, yep.

03:54:03.822 --> 03:54:10.608
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. So yeah, so the roots and vines grow up and start to entangle themselves around finding places to...

03:54:09.960 --> 03:54:12.522
[Vael (Berberis)]: And I always assume the plants are...

03:54:14.643 --> 03:54:16.544
[Vael (Berberis)]: Native plants to this area?

03:54:17.120 --> 03:54:42.888
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, you don't see... Yeah, yeah, totally, yeah. So, I mean, there are all sorts of like withered and broken brambles and husks of trees. I think I imagine sort of these like very gnarled brambles starting to poke up through the ground and give some of their life force to the erinyes here. And because this is nature, she heals without any role required. Beautiful.

03:54:47.609 --> 03:54:55.092
[Vael (Berberis)]: Healing word is a bonus.

03:54:51.136 --> 03:54:52.822
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yes, but spell slot.

03:54:56.640 --> 03:55:06.288
[Riv (DM)]: Did you do land's aid from staff? Or was that a... Nice.

03:54:59.394 --> 03:55:05.397
[Vael (Berberis)]: No, lands aid is a feature for being a druid, part of being a druid.

03:55:06.977 --> 03:55:09.398
[Vael (Berberis)]: It's a magic action but it's not a spell slot.

03:55:09.971 --> 03:55:10.472
[Riv (DM)]: That's fine.

03:55:11.199 --> 03:55:12.640
[Vael (Berberis)]: It actually consumes a wild shape.

03:55:16.827 --> 03:55:17.902
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: The southern bird is dead?

03:55:19.324 --> 03:55:19.845
[Vael (Berberis)]: Not quite.

03:55:21.832 --> 03:55:33.080
[Lacer (Kai)]: If that's the case, does my chalice, which is druidic, heal the demon? Or is it still lunar, like, whole?

03:55:32.730 --> 03:55:33.786
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: You can try!

03:55:34.420 --> 03:55:34.981
[Lacer (Kai)]: I already did...

03:55:34.662 --> 03:55:36.344
[Vael (Berberis)]: I mean, it's cleric.

03:55:35.261 --> 03:55:38.383
[Lacer (Kai)]: I already... No, it's not cleric.

03:55:37.659 --> 03:55:37.919
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

03:55:38.443 --> 03:55:43.767
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's... What's it called? Chalice is not... Oh, wait, it is cleric. No, it's not. Starry Form is not cleric.

03:55:43.944 --> 03:55:52.210
[Riv (DM)]: Star Reform's druid, but I'm going to rule that your whole deal is Soluna bullshit, so, um, yeah.

03:55:45.188 --> 03:55:45.988
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's druid, yeah.

03:55:50.451 --> 03:55:51.552
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, okay. Okay.

03:55:52.860 --> 03:55:54.421
[Lacer (Kai)]: Fair. That's okay. Cool.

03:55:54.212 --> 03:55:54.412
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

03:55:54.962 --> 03:55:55.242
[Lacer (Kai)]: Fair.

03:55:56.685 --> 03:56:15.077
[Vael (Berberis)]: And for bonus action, I'll pump a healing word into our sudden arrival fiend friend. Maximum healing.

03:56:13.627 --> 03:56:13.811
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Nice.

03:56:14.224 --> 03:56:14.664
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, nice.

03:56:15.745 --> 03:56:16.265
[Riv (DM)]: Very good.

03:56:17.119 --> 03:56:17.819
[Lacer (Kai)]: Does it crit?

03:56:20.527 --> 03:56:22.647
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah, she's starting to look pretty, well, she's looking better.

03:56:23.343 --> 03:56:30.570
[Vael (Berberis)]: I assume it's not great, but And instead of being burning holy magic, it is nature.

03:56:31.391 --> 03:56:33.552
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, totally. Yep, no check here required.

03:56:36.632 --> 03:56:39.193
[Vael (Berberis)]: Step down one more just to make a bit more space.

03:56:41.434 --> 03:56:42.635
[Vael (Berberis)]: Action and bonus.

03:56:46.836 --> 03:56:51.480
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. We go to the bird's go actually.

03:56:53.082 --> 03:56:59.087
[Riv (DM)]: So I think it will try to get out of the rope, right?

03:57:03.291 --> 03:57:08.636
[Riv (DM)]: Sera, how does it get out? I assume it's just another saving throw, right?

03:57:08.781 --> 03:57:11.123
[Lacer (Kai)]: Isn't it strength save? You have to like try and break the rope.

03:57:15.116 --> 03:57:22.382
[Riv (DM)]: It doesn't say anywhere that... Yeah, but that's just to avoid it in the first place.

03:57:17.989 --> 03:57:19.911
[Lacer (Kai)]: Strength saving DC 16. Oh.

03:57:24.334 --> 03:57:30.559
[Lacer (Kai)]: I thought I read something where you... Yeah, you can attack the rope, yeah.

03:57:25.524 --> 03:57:32.770
[Riv (DM)]: Has the restraint condition until the rope is destroyed. So it needs to attack the rope. Let's see how much health the rope has.

03:57:33.922 --> 03:57:37.172
[Lacer (Kai)]: It's got not much, I'm pretty sure. Unless it's magic this time.

03:57:37.314 --> 03:57:44.760
[Riv (DM)]: Oh no, it's a pretty powerful rope. Yeah, the bird is just gonna start, I guess, trying to attack the rope.

03:57:48.140 --> 03:57:51.315
[Vael (Berberis)]: I mean, it can still attack while restrained, it's just got disadvantage.

03:57:51.803 --> 03:57:52.043
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

03:57:53.283 --> 03:57:58.586
[Riv (DM)]: I think the AC of the rope is 20.

03:57:59.866 --> 03:58:11.131
[Riv (DM)]: AC 20? Nice. OK, cool. So the first one hits. And it does actually make contact with the rope. 14 damage.

03:58:12.231 --> 03:58:15.848
[Riv (DM)]: All right, cool. Yeah. Rope honestly looks fine.

03:58:18.790 --> 03:58:29.254
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, next bird comes in, sees a nice big pack of people over here. Everyone get ready to roll your constitution saving throws.

03:58:30.884 --> 03:58:31.904
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, it's an AoE.

03:58:35.532 --> 03:58:47.584
[Vael (Berberis)]: Much bigger than the previous ones, isn't it? 25 you got.

03:58:38.078 --> 03:58:39.239
[Riv (DM)]: That should be the same, 20 foot.

03:58:45.324 --> 03:58:46.724
[Riv (DM)]: Twenty-four. Yeah, hang on.

03:58:47.904 --> 03:58:48.945
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: That shows like five.

03:58:47.945 --> 03:58:49.866
[Riv (DM)]: That looks like 25 on the map, eh?

03:58:50.390 --> 03:58:55.631
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, because it's big. It's a giant. It's big. Since it's a large creature, the range increases.

03:58:57.011 --> 03:59:03.738
[Lacer (Kai)]: Because it's hitbox is like a... 10x10, so it's 10x10 from, it's 20 foot from the 10x10.

03:59:04.858 --> 03:59:05.559
[Lacer (Kai)]: So that's right.

03:59:04.931 --> 03:59:06.331
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that makes sense. Yeah.

03:59:07.371 --> 03:59:08.072
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool.

03:59:15.381 --> 03:59:15.939
[Vael (Berberis)]: Eromeroi.

03:59:15.743 --> 03:59:17.644
[Lacer (Kai)]: God damn it.

03:59:15.796 --> 03:59:17.878
[Riv (DM)]: So it'd be Erendor, Dondar, Kai.

03:59:17.248 --> 03:59:17.388
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Woof.

03:59:18.798 --> 03:59:22.221
[Riv (DM)]: And I'll give you advantage on your check, Berberis, since you're on the edge.

03:59:25.580 --> 03:59:27.944
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: You'd say a con check or a strength check?

03:59:28.727 --> 03:59:30.368
[Riv (DM)]: Con saving, you can just click the button in the chat.

03:59:39.483 --> 03:59:40.044
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Did that work?

03:59:51.200 --> 04:00:01.748
[Riv (DM)]: Alright. Dondar, Erendor, and Kai are all stunned and take 12 damage. Berberis, you take 6.

03:59:59.445 --> 04:00:00.651
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Here we go again!

04:00:02.299 --> 04:00:03.183
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Reaction showed up.

04:00:03.492 --> 04:00:04.953
[Vael (Berberis)]: It says unsaved, no damage.

04:00:06.131 --> 04:00:06.411
[Riv (DM)]: Or does it?

04:00:07.094 --> 04:00:07.254
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yep.

04:00:09.113 --> 04:00:09.953
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful, alright.

04:00:09.114 --> 04:00:31.524
[Vael (Berberis)]: No, the chat feed, unsaved, no damage. I mean, I can take, it's fine, but... Yeah, shield doesn't work against AoE, only targeted.

04:00:11.895 --> 04:00:13.016
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, cool.

04:00:18.339 --> 04:00:19.100
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, no, you're good.

04:00:18.508 --> 04:00:20.109
[JC (Ryo)]: Wait, so I took damage for no reason then?

04:00:23.805 --> 04:00:24.265
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

04:00:24.949 --> 04:00:25.526
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, did I?

04:00:25.867 --> 04:00:27.608
[Riv (DM)]: Looks like it.

04:00:26.952 --> 04:00:28.833
[JC (Ryo)]: Steel doesn't save you from this one Dondar.

04:00:32.652 --> 04:00:33.375
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Still doin' it, though.

04:00:34.775 --> 04:00:35.816
[JC (Ryo)]: You can't, you're stunned.

04:00:36.121 --> 04:00:37.623
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, you can't even cast.

04:00:37.471 --> 04:00:37.571
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Aww.

04:00:37.723 --> 04:00:39.245
[Lacer (Kai)]: Once it hits you, you can't.

04:00:40.386 --> 04:00:41.688
[Lacer (Kai)]: You can't cast, yeah.

04:00:42.339 --> 04:00:45.281
[Riv (DM)]: You can chuck like 10 health back on or something if you want, Ryo.

04:00:46.444 --> 04:00:48.866
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, that's the only damage I've taken, so back to max.

04:00:49.504 --> 04:00:54.792
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. Alrighty, Dondar's stunned. Ryo is chillin' back in the corner. Your go, dude.

04:00:55.992 --> 04:01:00.816
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, well, this one's still got Hunter's Mark on it, so bow, bow.

04:01:01.958 --> 04:01:04.059
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, does your Sharpshooter help you with prone targets?

04:01:06.100 --> 04:01:07.041
[JC (Ryo)]: Oh, is it prone?

04:01:07.532 --> 04:01:08.389
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yes, Prone.

04:01:07.622 --> 04:01:07.822
[Riv (DM)]: Mmm.

04:01:09.023 --> 04:01:15.408
[JC (Ryo)]: I think we discussed this the other day, and it makes it a straight shot instead of the advantage, disadvantage.

04:01:09.920 --> 04:01:13.541
[Vael (Berberis)]: Locations should be fine.

04:01:19.312 --> 04:01:19.452
[Riv (DM)]: Uhh...

04:01:20.772 --> 04:01:24.995
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, when it's restrained, yes, it will cancel it out. So it will be a straight shot.

04:01:27.180 --> 04:01:29.822
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, I think we had this before when we were talking about.

04:01:29.727 --> 04:01:33.148
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah, and you're not getting any cover bonuses for being player leader, so...

04:01:29.842 --> 04:01:37.927
[JC (Ryo)]: That's a straight shot.

04:01:36.263 --> 04:01:38.825
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. Straight shot.

04:01:39.728 --> 04:01:41.049
[JC (Ryo)]: How fucked up does it look?

04:01:42.367 --> 04:01:43.148
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, it's getting there.

04:01:43.711 --> 04:01:44.511
[JC (Ryo)]: Getting there? Okay.

04:01:46.472 --> 04:01:47.273
[JC (Ryo)]: So number one.

04:01:51.676 --> 04:01:52.216
[JC (Ryo)]: Does that hit?

04:01:51.989 --> 04:01:53.146
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Oh yeah.

04:02:00.456 --> 04:02:09.825
[JC (Ryo)]: And I again would like to bonus action. Hang on a sec. Let me read the spell again.

04:02:12.888 --> 04:02:16.852
[JC (Ryo)]: Before I bonus action and pump in Hell of Thorns again.

04:02:16.812 --> 04:02:18.481
[Vael (Berberis)]: I think if you hail, you'll hit off.

04:02:21.436 --> 04:02:22.537
[Lacer (Kai)]: Uny aim off, right?

04:02:21.597 --> 04:02:24.900
[JC (Ryo)]: Each creature within five feet. Yeah, okay, so that'll hit him, yeah.

04:02:25.398 --> 04:02:29.481
[Lacer (Kai)]: Unless he goes for a wingshot. He's got a huge hitbox. It's a 10 by 10.

04:02:30.608 --> 04:02:30.848
[JC (Ryo)]: No.

04:02:32.289 --> 04:02:32.909
[JC (Ryo)]: Second attack.

04:02:41.268 --> 04:02:43.769
[Riv (DM)]: Ooh, there it is. There it is.

04:02:43.173 --> 04:02:44.034
[JC (Ryo)]: I think that hits.

04:02:43.789 --> 04:02:53.514
[Riv (DM)]: I like that it's... Oh yeah.

04:02:50.997 --> 04:02:52.897
[JC (Ryo)]: And is it crit on the hunters or no?

04:02:54.175 --> 04:02:55.636
[Riv (DM)]: Hell yeah.

04:02:54.252 --> 04:02:54.415
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yup.

04:02:56.659 --> 04:02:57.639
[JC (Ryo)]: All right, so double that one.

04:02:58.277 --> 04:02:58.577
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

04:02:58.340 --> 04:02:58.760
[JC (Ryo)]: Oh, nice.

04:03:00.018 --> 04:03:00.558
[Riv (DM)]: Very good.

04:03:01.779 --> 04:03:03.840
[Riv (DM)]: Huge damage. Yeah, it's very close. Very close.

04:03:05.504 --> 04:03:08.466
[JC (Ryo)]: I don't think I have anything I can do as a bonus action on its own.

04:03:11.088 --> 04:03:13.930
[JC (Ryo)]: Oh, hang on a sec. I have, where is this?

04:03:17.412 --> 04:03:18.953
[JC (Ryo)]: Just reading something. One second.

04:03:22.896 --> 04:03:34.804
[JC (Ryo)]: Bugger it. I'm going to pump one of these into it. This isn't doubled as well because of the crit, is it?

04:03:35.584 --> 04:03:35.904
[Riv (DM)]: It will be.

04:03:36.648 --> 04:03:49.156
[JC (Ryo)]: It will be. Okay, so that's a crit.

04:03:37.745 --> 04:03:39.465
[Riv (DM)]: Every single dice you roll for damage.

04:03:38.720 --> 04:03:39.190
[Vael (Berberis)]: Baldur's.

04:03:46.867 --> 04:03:47.327
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

04:03:50.328 --> 04:03:52.549
[Riv (DM)]: Beautiful. So another 14. Hell yeah.

04:03:54.069 --> 04:03:55.710
[Riv (DM)]: All right. Cool. Great turn.

04:03:58.170 --> 04:03:59.351
[Riv (DM)]: You happy chilling down there where you are?

04:04:00.283 --> 04:04:01.104
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, I'll chill you.

04:04:01.591 --> 04:04:05.713
[Riv (DM)]: Sweet. So we go back up to Kai who You were stunned, right?

04:04:06.133 --> 04:04:06.727
[Lacer (Kai)]: Ermstund.

04:04:07.954 --> 04:04:10.956
[Riv (DM)]: End of your turn, you're no longer stunned. Erendor, I think you were also stunned.

04:04:12.898 --> 04:04:13.598
[Riv (DM)]: Unfortunate.

04:04:15.299 --> 04:04:16.620
[Riv (DM)]: We come to our erinyes friend.

04:04:17.821 --> 04:04:30.450
[Riv (DM)]: After having been given a bit of rejuvenation from the nearby druid, she sort of like kicks off some of the vines that were starting to wrap around her feet. The ground is still a tangle, but I assume you are not restraining her or at least impeding her.

04:04:29.908 --> 04:04:30.012
[Vael (Berberis)]: No.

04:04:30.470 --> 04:04:35.974
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, so she is going to cross her sword over Double hands and bring it straight down on top of the bird's head.

04:04:38.456 --> 04:04:40.478
[Riv (DM)]: With advantage because it is restrained.

04:04:43.800 --> 04:04:49.365
[Riv (DM)]: All right, there we go. That one hits and she sends it to a different realm.

04:04:52.347 --> 04:04:52.688
[Riv (DM)]: All right.

04:04:54.087 --> 04:04:54.608
[Lacer (Kai)]: Jesus.

04:04:54.609 --> 04:05:17.800
[Riv (DM)]: First bird dies. She turns, unfurls the rope, takes one step forward and launches it out at the next bird. We will take a strength saving throw. Oh, and succeeds, okay. Let's see how far she can go. Yeah, she can get there easy.

04:05:19.401 --> 04:05:22.644
[Riv (DM)]: And she will take a swing at this guy with her last attack. Get out of there.

04:05:26.604 --> 04:05:27.387
[Vael (Berberis)]: Let's just go through.

04:05:28.549 --> 04:05:39.768
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, three attacks. She can do ropes and normal, yeah. You don't want to get hit by one of these things three times in a row, man. That's why I've been a little bit frustrated that she's fucking missing every attack, dude.

04:05:41.649 --> 04:05:53.096
[Riv (DM)]: All good. Amazing. So yeah. And last attack misses. But she's got like, you know, blood pouring down in front of her face. You can barely see. She's hanging on. All good. We go, yeah, we go to Berberacene.

04:05:55.676 --> 04:05:57.357
[Vael (Berberis)]: Move up to get a swing on this thing.

04:06:00.440 --> 04:06:03.602
[Vael (Berberis)]: It is on the ground, I believe. If she's swinging.

04:06:03.116 --> 04:06:04.678
[Riv (DM)]: You'll be able to reach it. Yeah, you can reach it.

04:06:08.241 --> 04:06:11.084
[Riv (DM)]: It was coming to try and pick someone else up in screeching at the same time.

04:06:15.372 --> 04:06:18.895
[Vael (Berberis)]: Just having a quick glance to see if I've got anything more interesting to throw at it.

04:06:22.798 --> 04:06:25.280
[Vael (Berberis)]: Hmm. She missed hers, didn't she?

04:06:27.500 --> 04:06:32.063
[Vael (Berberis)]: Um, fuck it, bonus action, gonna burn an ensnaring strike.

04:06:33.172 --> 04:06:34.772
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, nice. That's a good idea.

04:06:36.525 --> 04:06:39.707
[Vael (Berberis)]: So, I'll do the attack first, cause I need to actually hit the damn thing.

04:06:46.156 --> 04:06:47.556
[Riv (DM)]: All right, so as you hit it, yeah, yeah, cool.

04:06:47.551 --> 04:06:54.936
[Vael (Berberis)]: Uh, yes, yeah, DC 17 strength.

04:06:47.656 --> 04:06:48.377
[Riv (DM)]: Yep, 23 is good.

04:06:49.517 --> 04:06:50.797
[Riv (DM)]: So then it's strength save.

04:06:56.219 --> 04:06:56.519
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

04:06:57.599 --> 04:06:59.980
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, no, not good.

04:07:04.084 --> 04:07:27.619
[Riv (DM)]: What does that mean? Oh, it is large, though. So it gets advantage, so roll again. Still no good. All right, cool.

04:07:19.088 --> 04:07:20.048
[Vael (Berberis)]: Siloli damage.

04:07:24.630 --> 04:07:25.830
[Vael (Berberis)]: Is this thing wounded?

04:07:27.639 --> 04:07:29.140
[Riv (DM)]: This thing has not taken any damage.

04:07:27.651 --> 04:07:40.615
[Vael (Berberis)]: Okay, not yet. Nine damage from Siloli. I do not have a damage roll for the ensnaring, but it is 1d6.

04:07:50.080 --> 04:07:50.300
[Riv (DM)]: Cool.

04:07:51.084 --> 04:07:54.765
[Vael (Berberis)]: And it is now restrained, so second attack is at advantage.

04:07:56.223 --> 04:08:02.105
[Riv (DM)]: Second attack will be at advantage. I think the ensnaring damage happens on its turn, but it's all good. I'll just apply it now anyway.

04:08:09.991 --> 04:08:13.832
[Vael (Berberis)]: Also topple, because why not just load all of the fucking modifiers.

04:08:12.350 --> 04:08:12.710
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah.

04:08:15.811 --> 04:08:16.491
[Riv (DM)]: Unleash the bag.

04:08:17.752 --> 04:08:18.012
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah.

04:08:19.936 --> 04:08:20.396
[Riv (DM)]: Is that...

04:08:21.617 --> 04:08:22.437
[Riv (DM)]: Wait, did it come up?

04:08:24.358 --> 04:08:25.019
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, did you see...

04:08:24.480 --> 04:08:27.463
[Vael (Berberis)]: So it is now restrained and prone.

04:08:30.421 --> 04:08:33.043
[Riv (DM)]: I wonder if that thing is... Weird.

04:08:33.568 --> 04:08:40.074
[Vael (Berberis)]: Not that it gives me extra advantage, but I'll take it.

04:08:36.905 --> 04:08:39.846
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, cool. Looks like it failed its top check as well, by the way.

04:08:40.474 --> 04:08:40.734
[Vael (Berberis)]: 24 hit.

04:08:42.207 --> 04:08:43.568
[Riv (DM)]: Restrained and prone. Nice.

04:08:52.128 --> 04:08:52.448
[Riv (DM)]: Ooh, yeah.

04:08:54.856 --> 04:09:01.261
[Vael (Berberis)]: Damage plus an extra d8 because of Colossus.

04:09:04.572 --> 04:09:05.632
[Riv (DM)]: Nice, another 14.

04:09:08.093 --> 04:09:15.235
[Riv (DM)]: Dude with the Styx not doing too bad, eh? Just a... Yeah.

04:09:12.069 --> 04:09:20.396
[Vael (Berberis)]: Well I did like the last round was like 37 damage with the stick this round not so much. And our allies she's still looking rough.

04:09:23.988 --> 04:09:25.889
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, still pretty bad.

04:09:27.712 --> 04:09:40.481
[Vael (Berberis)]: So with the way magic action works now, I can still use a bonus action spell while I've attacked.

04:09:37.274 --> 04:09:37.974
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, yeah, definitely.

04:09:40.075 --> 04:09:42.796
[Riv (DM)]: Unless you're in Sneering Strike, use the spell slot.

04:09:43.263 --> 04:09:45.524
[Vael (Berberis)]: Oh, which it did. Yep, correct. Good call.

04:09:48.386 --> 04:09:52.429
[Vael (Berberis)]: So yeah, no bonus action. It is restrained, concentrating on that.

04:09:53.036 --> 04:09:53.296
[Riv (DM)]: Nice.

04:09:53.870 --> 04:09:56.572
[Vael (Berberis)]: I need some more action economy. I need to get like shield marks or something.

04:09:57.457 --> 04:09:58.998
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. You have played Divinity?

04:10:00.965 --> 04:10:03.326
[Vael (Berberis)]: A little bit of original Sin 2, I think.

04:10:04.099 --> 04:10:07.040
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. The action economy in that game is really interesting. I quite like it actually.

04:10:06.607 --> 04:10:10.288
[Vael (Berberis)]: Yeah, I've built characters with stupid action apparently before, but yeah, okay, that's my turn.

04:10:10.881 --> 04:10:20.063
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. All right. It's going to make a strength check. Actually, I think it might be athletics, right? To break from ensnaring.

04:10:22.772 --> 04:10:28.054
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, make an athletics check. Does it have any athletics mods? It doesn't, so it's just gonna roll straight.

04:10:33.957 --> 04:10:35.517
[Riv (DM)]: Nope, it fails.

04:10:36.252 --> 04:10:38.674
[Vael (Berberis)]: It only took one. We'll take the damage roll from before.

04:10:39.319 --> 04:10:45.342
[Riv (DM)]: I use its... Oh yeah, using an athletics check will actually be its action.

04:10:45.338 --> 04:10:45.939
[Vael (Berberis)]: Action, yep.

04:10:46.662 --> 04:10:47.302
[Riv (DM)]: Yerr.

04:10:49.463 --> 04:10:50.624
[Riv (DM)]: Okay, there it is.

04:10:51.049 --> 04:10:52.054
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh, it's still pruned.

04:10:52.336 --> 04:10:59.659
[Riv (DM)]: It's struggling to try and fight its way out of these bindings, but it fails. So yeah, it is still held on the ground.

04:10:59.167 --> 04:11:05.766
[Vael (Berberis)]: And because it's... Restrained, it has no movement so it can't get up, spend half its movement to get up.

04:11:06.361 --> 04:11:06.701
[Riv (DM)]: Yup.

04:11:08.322 --> 04:11:13.804
[Riv (DM)]: This thing's about to have a bad next round. Dondar is stunned, unfortunately, and we go to Rio.

04:11:18.469 --> 04:11:21.032
[JC (Ryo)]: Okay, bonus action move, Hunter's Mark.

04:11:22.573 --> 04:11:24.336
[JC (Ryo)]: What's the range on moving Hunter's Mark?

04:11:27.148 --> 04:11:28.109
[Riv (DM)]: Probably 120, right?

04:11:28.421 --> 04:11:29.702
[JC (Ryo)]: It's a 94 range.

04:11:28.509 --> 04:11:29.530
[Riv (DM)]: Everything you do is 120?

04:11:31.184 --> 04:11:34.228
[JC (Ryo)]: No, 90, so I'm assuming up within 90.

04:11:33.514 --> 04:11:38.078
[Riv (DM)]: What? Yeah, you're fine.

04:11:46.305 --> 04:11:49.448
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. You're looking for AC 15, so you're good.

04:11:56.200 --> 04:11:57.221
[JC (Ryo)]: And the second shot.

04:12:00.772 --> 04:12:18.022
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, all good.

04:12:04.104 --> 04:12:13.188
[JC (Ryo)]: Two off. Back on those marker rolls.

04:12:18.931 --> 04:12:20.832
[JC (Ryo)]: And I'll just move sideways a bit, because I can.

04:12:22.044 --> 04:12:23.685
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Okay, cool.

04:12:25.146 --> 04:12:39.068
[Riv (DM)]: We go to the top of the round. I will give you guys a choice. Do you want to see how much damage you can do on this thing? Or would you like to end the fight by slaying the bird and we move on?

04:12:40.769 --> 04:12:50.418
[Riv (DM)]: Don't necessarily just want to waste a whole few rounds just beating up this little thing if you want to skip it. But if you guys want to see how much damage you can do, I'm all for it as well.

04:12:53.803 --> 04:12:58.048
[Lacer (Kai)]: Make a poll. Do you want to beat it up or do you want to skip?

04:13:02.105 --> 04:13:10.333
[JC (Ryo)]: How much does it have?

04:13:02.322 --> 04:13:09.435
[Vael (Berberis)]: We've got it restrained, on the ground, prone. Everyone's waking up and is about to just fucking blast with it.

04:13:09.116 --> 04:13:15.838
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah. You kill this thing. What's that? Ah, it's about three quarters.

04:13:16.918 --> 04:13:22.940
[Riv (DM)]: Like, you kill this thing. Erinyes kills it pretty much single-handedly if she hits all three of her attacks with advantage.

04:13:24.260 --> 04:13:30.142
[Riv (DM)]: So we end. Like, it dies. I was just wondering if you guys wanted to roll some dice some more.

04:13:29.932 --> 04:13:36.695
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, I just want to hit Toll of the Dead. I want to see how much damage she can do on this thing when it's a death star.

04:13:35.824 --> 04:13:36.724
[Riv (DM)]: How much she can do.

04:13:37.155 --> 04:13:37.555
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah.

04:13:38.208 --> 04:13:39.709
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, we can roll here.

04:13:40.156 --> 04:13:42.437
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, after my Toll. Can I do my Toll of the Dead first?

04:13:42.531 --> 04:13:52.558
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, go on.

04:13:43.317 --> 04:13:49.779
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah. So I'll just do... Yeah, see if we can do a wisdom save. DC 17 wisdom save.

04:13:52.678 --> 04:13:56.261
[Riv (DM)]: Were you going to do any more charges with Ember Blade?

04:13:57.244 --> 04:13:57.388
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: No.

04:13:57.522 --> 04:13:58.442
[Riv (DM)]: Oh well, super failed.

04:14:02.545 --> 04:14:06.168
[Riv (DM)]: Roll your sneak attack damage again, Erendor. See how much you can get.

04:14:16.300 --> 04:14:19.463
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. Alright, she's gonna do... One.

04:14:24.206 --> 04:14:29.311
[Riv (DM)]: Alright, cool, I think these ones will hit.

04:14:30.612 --> 04:14:45.144
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, alright, sweet. So, damage is this. Oh shit!

04:14:45.460 --> 04:14:46.842
[JC (Ryo)]: Holy shit, this thing hits hard.

04:14:47.180 --> 04:14:48.521
[Lacer (Kai)]: Wow, does she, does she wipe it?

04:14:49.612 --> 04:14:54.115
[Riv (DM)]: So we've got 60... Yeah, it's like 83 damage.

04:14:49.988 --> 04:14:53.975
[JC (Ryo)]: That's 90 damage, pretty much. 93.

04:14:56.197 --> 04:14:56.937
[Riv (DM)]: Oh yeah.

04:14:56.349 --> 04:14:57.544
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: My max HP is 81.

04:14:58.938 --> 04:15:11.867
[Riv (DM)]: That thing just gets carved into pieces. She steps forward to it, seeing it tangled up in these vines. One slice down, wing comes off. Another slice, in an elegant motion, other wing, finishing with the head.

04:15:12.928 --> 04:15:15.170
[Riv (DM)]: Final slice, and it goes rolling across the ground.

04:15:17.091 --> 04:15:20.645
[Riv (DM)]: That's why you don't mess with... Certain creatures in the hells.

04:15:22.706 --> 04:15:27.409
[Riv (DM)]: And with that, you just hear the heavy breaths of the erinyes in front of you.

04:15:29.050 --> 04:15:36.274
[Riv (DM)]: She immediately turns towards you Kai and walks straight up to you, looking pissed.

04:15:39.209 --> 04:15:39.979
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm sorry.

04:15:39.552 --> 04:15:40.822
[JC (Ryo)]: Not as smart as he looks.

04:15:43.040 --> 04:15:43.953
[Vael (Berberis)]: Which is saying something.

04:15:47.259 --> 04:15:59.814
[Riv (DM)]: She comes right up to you. And looks like she might, you know, strike you or something, but she just sort of holds up a hand and says, Do not try that again here.

04:16:02.916 --> 04:16:08.379
[Riv (DM)]: If we had not made a bargain, I would slay you where you stand and take your souls with me.

04:16:10.520 --> 04:16:15.843
[Riv (DM)]: But I must relent.

04:16:17.344 --> 04:16:23.077
[Riv (DM)]: A pact was made. And I will stand by it. Let's make this quick. I have soldiers on the battlefield waiting for me.

04:16:25.018 --> 04:16:26.918
[Riv (DM)]: Come. I assume you seek the river.

04:16:29.159 --> 04:16:29.739
[Riv (DM)]: Try to keep up.

04:16:30.820 --> 04:16:33.760
[Riv (DM)]: And with that, she turns and starts jogging in the direction of the gorge.

04:16:35.681 --> 04:16:36.361
[Riv (DM)]: Without looking back.

04:16:39.380 --> 04:16:40.181
[Lacer (Kai)]: I look at them.

04:16:39.596 --> 04:16:40.013
[JC (Ryo)]: Tchaikov.

04:16:40.961 --> 04:16:42.202
[Lacer (Kai)]: I guess we gotta go.

04:16:45.944 --> 04:16:46.264
[Riv (DM)]: Alright.

04:16:46.846 --> 04:16:51.750
[Lacer (Kai)]: Was there anything interesting that caught our eye on these monsters as we were walking past?

04:16:52.492 --> 04:16:54.213
[Riv (DM)]: Would you like to wait behind to search them?

04:16:54.793 --> 04:17:03.140
[Lacer (Kai)]: No, because there's no time, but I'd like to just do a, if there was something shiny or anything, just a quick perception.

04:17:04.062 --> 04:17:06.984
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, you can roll a perception. It's a cheeky little perception.

04:17:06.556 --> 04:17:10.147
[Vael (Berberis)]: I'm just gonna quickly yank a couple of pin feathers as we go.

04:17:10.307 --> 04:17:11.328
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah, that's fine.

04:17:13.256 --> 04:17:34.830
[JC (Ryo)]: I was hoping to try and get half my arrows back, but... Can I lock the big guy's hand off as I walk past?

04:17:20.435 --> 04:17:48.436
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. yeah there's no they don't have tools they don't have weapons or anything like that these are creatures from the abyss um they tear things to pieces with their claws um i'll say you can take either your arrows or you can try to take a swing at the hand not both cool all right um this would just be a survival check i think

04:17:32.328 --> 04:17:32.649
[Lacer (Kai)]: Cool.

04:17:36.471 --> 04:17:37.292
[JC (Ryo)]: Try and take it with me?

04:17:37.967 --> 04:17:39.312
[Lacer (Kai)]: Take some poison damage.

04:17:43.576 --> 04:17:44.527
[JC (Ryo)]: I'll take a swing at the hand.

04:17:49.012 --> 04:17:56.454
[Riv (DM)]: See if you can get the right spot to sever the tendons and the bones. There's a big meaty hand, so it's decently high DC.

04:17:57.376 --> 04:17:58.463
[Vael (Berberis)]: What is this creature called?

04:17:59.395 --> 04:18:02.156
[Riv (DM)]: This is a hezrou.

04:18:03.496 --> 04:18:15.660
[Riv (DM)]: You take a hack at it, it's not super clean, but another follow-up hack and it kind of limply comes off when you grab it. It's big though, this thing like, you need like both hands to sort of carry it, right? Like it's a big chunky thing.

04:18:17.580 --> 04:18:18.941
[Lacer (Kai)]: I'm gonna put this in the bag of holding.

04:18:21.484 --> 04:18:23.165
[JC (Ryo)]: I'm scaring the bag I'm holding technically.

04:18:23.984 --> 04:18:24.564
[Lacer (Kai)]: Well, I am.

04:18:24.884 --> 04:18:29.608
[Riv (DM)]: I don't think anyone can... Is it Kai? Alright, cool.

04:18:27.606 --> 04:18:37.072
[Lacer (Kai)]: Yeah, most of the time I carry it because I put random shit in it. All right.

04:18:31.209 --> 04:18:31.369
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

04:18:32.052 --> 04:18:36.135
[JC (Ryo)]: I'll hold it in front of me and I'll start running and when I catch up to Kai I'll be like, here, open the bag.

04:18:36.953 --> 04:18:38.995
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Cool.

04:18:38.757 --> 04:18:41.820
[JC (Ryo)]: And I'm trying to hold it out so the blood doesn't drip all over my fucking feet.

04:18:44.659 --> 04:18:49.363
[Riv (DM)]: Nice. So... Just to...

04:18:52.072 --> 04:18:58.736
[Riv (DM)]: Ah, I was gonna say, I was trying to remember how long Bigby's lasts. So as you start to run off, Dondar, you still have your massive hand available.

04:19:00.917 --> 04:19:15.886
[Riv (DM)]: But, yeah, after a minute. So it can probably follow you along for a little bit. But after a minute it will disappear. Alright, cool. Very good. You've made a friend, potentially, in the hells. You made your first deal with the devil as well.

04:19:17.147 --> 04:19:23.054
[Riv (DM)]: And now we'll see how it plays out. As you're led towards the river Styx. We've seen it there, eh?

04:19:25.495 --> 04:19:32.959
[Riv (DM)]: Cool. I gotta say, I think that might be the most damage from a single roll we've ever seen. With the lava.

04:19:34.373 --> 04:19:36.408
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh yes, that lava punch.

04:19:34.684 --> 04:19:34.984
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yep.

04:19:35.324 --> 04:20:01.724
[JC (Ryo)]: Yeah, what's your spellcasting modifier?

04:19:36.842 --> 04:19:43.065
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah. Good shout, man. I think it had like, I think it's like 75 to 80 health left when you do that?

04:19:39.644 --> 04:19:54.195
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: Yeah, didn't expect it to be able to push it so far.

04:19:43.125 --> 04:19:47.728
[Riv (DM)]: So, it pretty much just, it was the right amount of damage. Fucked it up.

04:19:54.215 --> 04:20:06.084
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: How do I say that?

04:19:55.600 --> 04:19:55.940
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, yeah.

04:19:56.041 --> 04:19:58.763
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: Yeah, what, five times your spellcasting mode?

04:19:56.481 --> 04:19:56.721
[Riv (DM)]: Nah.

04:19:59.283 --> 04:20:00.264
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: What the hell?

04:19:59.402 --> 04:19:59.602
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

04:20:05.505 --> 04:20:06.166
[Riv (DM)]: Plus five, right?

04:20:05.612 --> 04:20:07.595
[JC (Ryo)]: Get to your spells page. Copy your spells page.

04:20:09.788 --> 04:20:13.850
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, sorry, it's a spell mod, so it'll be like end, just as whatever is end point, so it's plus four.

04:20:14.220 --> 04:20:15.723
[Lacer (Kai)]: Plus 4? So 20?

04:20:15.371 --> 04:20:15.551
[Riv (DM)]: Yeah.

04:20:15.743 --> 04:20:16.484
[Lacer (Kai)]: 20 feet?

04:20:17.217 --> 04:20:17.278
[Deathmouse (Eryndor)]: 25.

04:20:17.712 --> 04:20:19.793
[Riv (DM)]: Plus the additional fives from base, so.

04:20:19.350 --> 04:20:19.751
[Lacer (Kai)]: Oh yeah, 25.

04:20:21.114 --> 04:20:21.274
[Riv (DM)]: Yep.

04:20:22.180 --> 04:20:23.001
[Kiwi (Dondar)]: 18 plus four.

04:20:22.695 --> 04:20:23.015
[Riv (DM)]: Oh, yeah.

04:20:24.676 --> 04:20:34.963
[Riv (DM)]: Yep. Bigby's pretty sick. All right. Nice. Very good. Let me stop us over here.
